Method: 提出HALO（Heterogeneous Admission with Localized Obligations）运行时协议，核心机制包括：1）保留声明前置条件仍受支持的组件；2）在派发前对每个精确动作进行重新检查；3）被阻止的动作仅允许由新的候选进行替换。该协议通过精细化组件级别的接纳控制和本地化义务管理来解决异构响应的一致性问题。

Result: HALO匹配了全部96个接纳期望并通过了全部20个协议测试。在结构化响应回放中，保留了248/248个受支持组件（其中128/128个不受无关变化影响），而整体响应策略仅保留0/248个组件。在十次PX4/Gazebo冷启动会话中，HALO阻止了所有测试的过时路由，未观察到匹配的过时设定点，并完成了所有新的恢复操作。

Conclusion: HALO通过组件级别的选择性接纳和前提条件检查，在保留有用组件的同时确保了系统安全性，相比整体拒绝策略具有显著优势，并在真实无人机仿真环境中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Recent agentic AI systems may return a heterogeneous response containing notices, requests, handoffs, and actions. Conditions can change before external use, so components from the same response need not remain supported together. Rejecting the whole response discards useful components, whereas checking components independently can leave a dependent without its prerequisite. We present Heterogeneous Admission with Localized Obligations (HALO), a runtime protocol that preserves supported components whose declared prerequisites also remain supported, rechecks each exact action before dispatch, and allows blocked actions to be replaced only by fresh candidates. HALO matched all 96 admission expectations and passed all 20 protocol tests. In structured-response replay, it retained 248/248 supported components, including 128/128 unaffected by unrelated changes, while a whole-response policy retained 0/248. Across ten cold-start PX4/Gazebo sessions, HALO blocked every tested stale route, observed no matching stale setpoint, and completed all fresh recoveries.

</details>


### [204] [SCOPE: Synthetic Conditional Objectives for Policy Evolution in Black-Box Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27630)
*Nguyen Viet Tuan Kiet,Nguyen Huu Duc,Le Cong Bang,Tran Cong Dao,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE框架，通过学习条件化的合成目标来引导策略演化，在有限评估预算下提升黑箱组合优化的搜索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱组合优化中，未知的目标函数无法为搜索方向提供有效指导，导致在有限评估预算下难以系统性地发现高质量解。

Method: SCOPE学习一组基于搜索历史条件化的合成目标，每个目标表达对候选解的不同偏好，用于演化生成多样化候选的搜索策略。外循环自适应更新和选择合成目标，内循环返回一组高性能策略组合以降低单一代用偏好的风险。

Result: 在多个基准问题上，SCOPE在有限评估预算下一致提升黑箱搜索性能，并能泛化到多种组合结构。

Conclusion: 将目标设计重新定义为引导策略探索的机制，使搜索过程既能利用已观察到的证据，又能保持离散解空间中的结构化多样性。

Abstract: Black-box combinatorial optimization requires systematically identifying high-quality solutions under a limited evaluation budget, yet the unknown objective function provides little guidance for deciding where the search should explore next. We introduce SCOPE, a general framework for Synthetic Conditional Objectives for Policy Evolution in Black-Box Combinatorial Optimization. Rather than directly optimizing the inaccessible objective, SCOPE learns a set of synthetic objectives conditioned on the accumulated search history, where each objective is designed to expose a distinct and potentially useful preference over candidate solutions. These objectives are then used to evolve search policies that generate diverse candidates, whose true quality is subsequently assessed through black-box evaluations. The outer loop adaptively updates and selects synthetic objectives according to how effectively their induced policies discover promising regions. In contrast, the inner loop returns a portfolio of top-performing policies to reduce the risk of relying on a single surrogate preference. This formulation reframes objective design as a mechanism for guiding policy exploration, enabling the search process to exploit observed evidence while maintaining structured diversity across discrete solution spaces. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmark problems demonstrate that SCOPE consistently improves black-box search performance under limited evaluation budgets and generalizes well across diverse combinatorial structures.

</details>


### [205] [ReDiPPO: Reference-Guided Value Calibration and Discrepancy-Aware Token Reweighting for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27631)
*Zhenrong Zhang,Fei Wu,Jun Du,Jianshu Zhang,Si Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReDiPPO框架，通过引入参考引导的critic和参考-标准差异感知机制，提升PPO在数学推理任务中的token级信用分配精度，从而改善策略优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数学推理任务中，PPO虽然理论上能提供token级信用分配，但由于推理过程长、奖励稀疏，标准critic难以准确评估中间推理状态，导致优势估计噪声大、策略更新次优。

Method: ReDiPPO包含两个核心组件：一是参考引导的critic，利用参考答案作为训练时的特权信号提供更精确的价值估计；二是保留标准critic并量化两者在token级别的差异，将该差异作为困难推理状态的指示器，用于对相应token优势进行重新加权。

Result: 在多种数学推理基准上的实验表明，ReDiPPO提升了价值估计准确性，并在最终推理性能上一致优于PPO、DAPO和GSPO等强策略优化基线。

Conclusion: 参考引导与差异感知机制能够有效改善PPO在长推理跨度、稀疏奖励场景下的信用分配质量，为提升大语言模型数学推理能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for enhancing the mathematical reasoning capabilities of large language models. Among existing policy optimization methods, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) remains particularly appealing because its learned critic can, in principle, provide token-level credit assignment. However, in mathematical reasoning tasks characterized by long reasoning horizons and sparse outcome rewards, reliable token-level credit assignment remains challenging. The standard critic often fails to accurately evaluate intermediate reasoning states, resulting in noisy advantage estimates and suboptimal policy updates. In this paper, we propose ReDiPPO, a Reference-guided and Discrepancy-aware PPO framework for mathematical reasoning. ReDiPPO introduces a reference-guided critic that uses reference answers as training-time privileged signals to provide more accurate value estimation. Meanwhile, it retains a standard critic and quantifies the token-level reference-standard discrepancy between the standard value estimate and the reference-guided value estimate. This discrepancy serves as an indicator of difficult reasoning states and is used to reweight the corresponding token-level advantages during PPO optimization. Extensive experiments on diverse mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that ReDiPPO improves value-estimation accuracy and consistently outperforms strong policy optimization baselines, including PPO, DAPO, and GSPO, in final reasoning performance. Our code is available on https://github.com/cii030/ReDiPPO.

</details>


### [206] [HealthCAT: An Interpretable Encoder-only Transformer Framework for Health Indicator Prediction and Temporal Interpretation of Wearable Sensor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27635)
*Xiaotong Yu,Joshua Y. Kim,HaeJin Lee,Kalina Yacef*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HealthCAT框架，将Encoder-only Transformer与Attentive Class Activation Token（AttentiveCAT）相结合，在可穿戴传感器数据上实现高精度预测的同时，提供类别特定、时间步级别的可解释性分析。在306名参与者的两个真实数据集上，HealthCAT在F1分数和准确率上分别比深度学习基线提升最多17%和12%，且通过掩码实验验证了其识别的时间步具有显著预测信息价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感器持续采集细粒度的多变量时间序列数据，为建模与健康结果相关的行为模式提供了机会。然而，现有深度学习方法优先追求预测精度而忽视可解释性，限制了其在健康研究中的应用。研究者希望在不牺牲预测性能的前提下，实现对穿戴传感器数据的可解释分析。

Method: HealthCAT框架整合了两个核心组件：Encoder-only Transformer用于时间序列建模，Attentive Class Activation Token（AttentiveCAT）用于生成类别特定、时间步级别的解释。该解释可映射回领域相关行为周期（如一天中的时段），支持个体层面的可穿戴传感器数据分析。

Result: 在两个真实世界可穿戴数据集（共306名参与者）上，HealthCAT的F1分数比深度学习基线最高提升17%，准确率最高提升12%（p<0.05）。掩码实验中，HealthCAT识别出的时间步在所有掩码条件下比随机选择携带显著更多的预测价值（p<0.05），验证了其时间步级解释的预测信息价值。

Conclusion: HealthCAT通过将预测性能与经过验证的时间步级可解释性相结合，将可穿戴传感器分析从聚合指标推进到时间模式层面，支持健康监测、行为模式分析和干预设计。其核心意义在于能够准确预测健康指标的同时，揭示身体活动模式发生的时间和方式，而非仅依赖聚合摘要指标。

Abstract: Wearable sensors continuously capture fine-grained multivariate time-series data, providing opportunities to model behavioural patterns associated with health outcomes. However, existing deep learning methods prioritise predictive accuracy over interpretability, limiting their application in health research. In this study, we present HealthCAT, a flexible framework that integrates an Encoder-only Transformer with an Attentive Class Activation Token (AttentiveCAT) to generate class-specific, time-step-level interpretations. These interpretations can be mapped back onto behavioural cycles that are relevant to the domain (e.g., time-of-day), supporting individual-level analysis of wearable sensor data. We evaluated HealthCAT using two real-world wearable sensor datasets (306 participants in total). HealthCAT outperformed deep learning baselines by up to 17\% in F1-score and 12\% in accuracy on both datasets ($p<0.05$). In masking experiments, the time steps identified by HealthCAT carried significantly more predictive value than random selection across all masking conditions ($p<0.05$), indicating that the identified time steps are predictively informative. By coupling predictive performance with validated time-step-level interpretability, HealthCAT moves wearable sensor analysis beyond aggregated metrics towards temporal patterns that support health monitoring, behavioural pattern analysis, and intervention design in health research. The significance of this work is that it enables accurate prediction of health indicators from wearable sensor data while providing insights into when and how physical activity patterns occur, rather than relying solely on aggregated summary measures.

</details>


### [207] [Evaluating and Pricing Advertisements in AI-Generated Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27686)
*John L. Turner-Smith,Zimeng Huang,Yuhan Fu,Yihang Zhang,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于心理学的智能体模拟框架，为LLM驱动的搜索广告构建缺失的点击意图监督信号，并将其蒸馏为参数高效的评估器，能够平滑预测广告质量与点击意图，进而支持理论完备的广告定价机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着搜索向LLM驱动的答案引擎转变，广告嵌入在生成回复中，亟需同时评估用户效用与商业价值。核心挑战是点击意图信号缺失：行为日志不可用、人工标注难以校准、前沿LLM评判器混淆意图与语言流畅度。这些缺失相互叠加，因为原则性定价需要连续意图信号，而生成该信号又需要当前不可用的监督。

Method: 通过心理学基础的智能体模拟框架构造缺失的监督信号，蒸馏为参数高效的评估器以预测点击意图及三个广告质量维度（作为平滑、可微的估计值）；基于该估计构建定价层，推导出诚实报价最优的唯一支付规则，并在best-of-k分配及非单调分配上扩展机制。

Result: 通过符号确定的行为扰动验证，评估器在相关性敏感性上以79%超越前沿零样本评判器（60-67%），能跟踪分级内容退化，对103个虚构产品零错误泛化，在5名标注者的86%成对判断中与人类偏好一致，且一致性随评估器置信度上升。

Conclusion: 该可微信号既可用于广告定价机制，也可作为广告生成的训练目标，为LLM搜索广告的商业化提供了统一的评估与定价框架。

Abstract: As search increasingly shifts toward LLM-driven answer engines, advertising is becoming embedded within the generated response itself and should therefore be evaluated for both user utility and commercial value. The key challenge is click-through intent: behavioural logs are unavailable, human annotation resists calibration, and frontier LLM judges conflate intent with linguistic fluency. These gaps compound, as principled pricing presupposes a continuous intent signal, while generating such a signal presupposes supervision that is currently unavailable. We construct the missing supervision through a psychologically grounded agent simulation framework, and distil it into a parameter-efficient evaluator that predicts click-through intent, together with the three companion dimensions of ad quality, as smooth, differentiable estimates. Validated through sign-certain behavioural perturbations, the evaluator surpasses frontier zero-shot judges on relevance sensitivity (79% versus 60-67%), tracks graded content degradation, generalises without error to 103 fictional products, and agrees with human preference in 86% of pairwise judgements across five annotators, with agreement rising in the evaluator's confidence. Upon its estimates we build the pricing layer directly, deriving the unique payment rule under which truthful bidding is optimal, demonstrating it on a best-of-k allocation, and extending the mechanism to non-monotone allocations. The same differentiable signal stands ready as a training objective for ad generation.

</details>


### [208] [Rehearse: Stepping Back from the Confidence Cliff in Self-Improving Autoresearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27687)
*Jiazhen Ji,Shouhong Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究自动化研究（autoresearch）循环中，智能体在执行训练运行之前判断修改方案是否有效的可靠性问题。作者发现随着循环推进，智能体的选择性准确率从82.8%骤降至56.9%，即"置信度悬崖"（confidence cliff）。为此提出Rehearse方法，通过聚焦的过往尝试记忆，将后期选择性准确率提升至83.5%，并在4,000次预算训练运行中稳定提升三个基准任务的最终指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: autoresearch循环的效率取决于智能体在花费训练运行成本之前，能否准确判断提议的修改是否可能有效。现有系统在早期迭代中判断较为可靠，但随着成功修改的累积，这种预执行判断能力显著下降，导致训练预算浪费。理解并解决这一能力衰减问题是提升自动化机器学习效率的关键。

Method: 首先利用公共AutoSOTA日志和296对同基准修改对（每对含一个有效修改和一个无效修改，真实结果隐藏）构建基准，分析LLM评判器的选择性准确率变化。在此基础上提出Rehearse方法：提出多个想法→执行前相互比较→运行最有前景的方案→利用聚焦的过往相似尝试及结果记忆进行判断。该方法作为轻量级技能集成到autoresearch循环中。

Result: LLM评判器在严格共识给出裁决的对上达到79.5%准确率。但在完整366对基准上，选择性准确率从82.8%（早期）降至56.9%（后期），即"置信度悬崖"。Rehearse通过聚焦结果记忆将后期选择性准确率提升至83.5%。在4,000次预算训练运行中，Rehearse在nanochat、图像分类和时间序列预测三个循环上均提升了相同训练预算下的最终端点指标。

Conclusion: 自动化研究循环存在"置信度悬崖"现象——随着成功修改累积，智能体对修改方案的预执行判断能力显著下降。通过聚焦的过往尝试记忆（Rehearse方法）可以有效缓解这一问题，在相同训练预算下提升最终性能。这表明管理智能体的元认知判断能力与生成修改想法同样重要，结果记忆的组织方式直接影响判断可靠性。

Abstract: Autoresearch improves machine-learning code by proposing changes, running full training jobs, and keeping changes that improve the metric. The efficiency of this loop depends not only on generating ideas, but also on the agent's ability to decide, before spending a training run, whether a proposed modification is likely to work. We study how the reliability of this pre-execution judgment changes over the course of an autoresearch trajectory. In public AutoSOTA logs (Li et al., 2026; Tsinghua FIB Lab, 2026), the fraction of helpful modifications falls from 70% in the first two iterations to 43% by iteration 6+. On 296 same-baseline modification pairs from 39 paper-derived AutoSOTA tasks, each containing one modification that improved the metric and one that did not, with measured outcomes hidden, an LLM judge given candidate rationales but no prior-attempt history reaches 79.5% accuracy on the pairs where strict consensus returns a verdict. On the full 366-pair benchmark, however, this ability weakens substantially late in the loop. As successful changes accumulate, selective accuracy - accuracy conditioned on a strict-consensus verdict - falls from 82.8% to 56.9%, while the judge remains willing to decide. We call this operational pattern the confidence cliff. Rehearse implements the loop change as a lightweight skill for autoresearch loops: propose several ideas, compare them before execution, run the most promising, and judge with a focused memory of similar past attempts and outcomes. This focused outcome memory raises late selective accuracy to 83.5%. Across 4,000 budgeted training runs over three loops, Rehearse improves the endpoint under the same training-run budget on nanochat, image classification, and time-series forecasting.

</details>


### [209] [Guiding Large Language Models with Genetic Programming-Evolved Heuristic Knowledge for Dynamic Multi-Mode Project Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27698)
*Yuan Tian,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将遗传规划（GP）进化出的高质量调度规则知识反向注入到大语言模型（LLM）中，通过特征选择、特征提示、规则引用和规则跟随四种机制指导LLM进行动态多模式项目调度决策，评估了调度性能、令牌消耗、决策稳定性和生成理由的特征关注度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-GP混合方法通常是LLM辅助启发式规则进化，但LLM零样本决策缺乏领域知识、令牌消耗大且多次查询结果不稳定；而GP进化规则蕴含丰富调度知识，可作为指导LLM决策的知识来源，因此需要探索从GP到LLM的知识迁移。

Method: 从高质量GP规则中提取调度知识，通过四种机制注入LLM：特征选择（简化决策上下文）、特征提示（突出重要特征）、规则引用（提供参考规则）和规则跟随（提供显式决策逻辑），在动态多模式项目调度场景中进行在线决策评估。

Result: GP指导普遍优于无指导的LLM，但知识表示形式影响效果：简化决策上下文或提供显式决策逻辑比突出重要特征更有效；特征选择在令牌效率上最佳，规则跟随在性能上最强但令牌成本更高；指导还提高了决策稳定性并改变了生成理由中表达的特征焦点。

Conclusion: GP向LLM的反向知识迁移是可行的，知识表示方式对LLM决策效果至关重要，应根据令牌预算和性能需求选择合适的注入机制。

Abstract: In dynamic multi-mode project scheduling, activities have alternative execution modes and uncertain durations, while precedence relations and limited resources constrain their execution. Heuristic priority rules support fast online decisions, but their design requires substantial domain expertise. Genetic programming (GP) hyper-heuristics can automatically evolve such rules. Large language models (LLMs), meanwhile, provide a flexible interface for interpreting scheduling information and explaining decisions. However, zero-shot LLM decisions may lack domain knowledge, consume many tokens, and vary across repeated queries. GP-evolved rules therefore provide a potential source of scheduling knowledge for guiding LLM decisions. Unlike existing LLM--GP hybrids that use LLMs to support heuristic evolution, we transfer knowledge in the reverse direction, using knowledge extracted from high-quality GP rules to guide an online LLM decision maker. We extract knowledge from high-quality GP rules and inject it through Feature Selection, Feature Hint, Rule Reference, and Rule Follow. These mechanisms are evaluated in terms of scheduling performance, token consumption, decision stability, and the feature focus expressed in generated rationales. GP-derived guidance generally improves the unguided LLM, but its representation matters. Simplifying the decision context or supplying explicit decision logic is more effective than highlighting important features. Feature Selection offers the best token efficiency, whereas Rule Follow achieves strong performance at greater token cost. Guidance also improves decision stability and changes the features expressed in generated rationales.

</details>


### [210] [SpatialCLI: Learning to Reason With Spatial Tools, Then Without Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27703)
*Yang Zhou,Zixuan Huang,Sunzhu Li,Zhuo Yang,Chen Zhang,Shunian Chen,Caijun Yan,Jianyao Xu,Shunyu Liu,Weijie Fu,Peiliang Li,Xiaozhi Chen,Yuxiang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialCLI框架，通过三阶段流程（调用、学习、内化）让视觉语言模型学会使用空间工具并逐步将专家感知能力内化，同时在MindCube基准上将Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct从29.3%提升至84.6%（带工具）和73.8%（无工具，内化后），超越了GPT-5.6 Sol的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉语言模型（VLMs）能够推理整体任务级别决策，但常常遗漏决定成败的视觉细节；而专业视觉模型能捕捉细节却无法将其转化为任务级别决策，两者之间存在根本性的能力鸿沟。

Method: 提出SpatialCLI框架，包含三个阶段：(1) Call阶段将专业视觉模型作为空间工具暴露给VLM以增强感知；(2) Learn阶段使用Cold-Start SFT和agentic RL改进工具使用能力；(3) Internalize阶段通过语言化成功的工具使用轨迹，将专业感知能力内化为VLM自身的知识。

Result: 在MindCube基准上，SpatialCLI将Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct从29.3%提升至84.6%（带工具），超过GPT-5.6 Sol的72.1%（带工具）；内化后不带工具仍保持73.8%的性能。同时引入SpatialCLI-Bench，一个包含516个示例的组合感知基准，覆盖定位、分割、深度和姿态等任务。

Conclusion: SpatialCLI通过"工具调用-强化学习-知识内化"的递进式框架，有效弥合了通用VLM与专业视觉模型之间的能力鸿沟，使VLM既能进行任务级推理又能掌握关键视觉细节，且在内化后不依赖外部工具仍能保持高性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in embodied agents to interpret visual inputs, reason about spatial relationships, and make task-level decisions based on that reasoning. However, a fundamental capability mismatch remains: general VLMs can reason about the overall task but often miss the visual details that determine success, while specialist vision models can capture those details but cannot translate them into task-level decisions. In this work, we propose SpatialCLI, a framework that teaches VLMs to reason with spatial tools and progressively internalize the specialist perceptual capabilities they provide. SpatialCLI proceeds in three stages: (1) Call exposes specialist vision models as spatial tools to augment the VLM's perception; (2) Learn uses Cold-Start SFT and agentic RL to improve tool use; and (3) Internalize verbalizes successful tool-use trajectories to internalize specialist perceptual capabilities. We further introduce SpatialCLI-Bench, a 516-example benchmark for compositional perception across localization, segmentation, depth, and pose. On MindCube, SpatialCLI raises Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct from 29.3% to 84.6% with tools, surpassing GPT-5.6 Sol with tools (72.1%), while retaining 73.8% without tools after internalization.

</details>


### [211] [Albilich: Steerable Proof-State Orchestration for LLM-Based Mathematical Research with CAS Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27705)
*Ting Gong,Michael Ruofan Zeng,Yong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Albilich，一个开源的数学自主研究智能体框架，结合长程推理、计算机代数系统（CAS）、文献检索和SQLite上下文管理，在RealMath基准上10/10（有CAS）和9/10（无CAS）全部通过，并在Kourovka笔记本中的开放群论问题上解决了多项难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能为数学研究贡献有用想法，但长周期的证明尝试难以协调、评估和复现，需要更好的自主研究框架。

Method: 构建Albilich开源智能体框架，集成长程推理、计算机代数系统（CAS）、文献检索和基于SQLite的持久化上下文管理，用于数学领域的自主研究。

Result: 在RealMath基准上，有CAS时解决了10/10问题，无CAS时解决了9/10；在Kourovka笔记本的开放群论问题上，产出了对Problem 21.142的反例和对Problem 20.2的强化的证明。消融实验显示CAS启用时token减少32.0%，并验证了advisor agent在证明路径合成中的必要性。

Conclusion: Albilich被证明是一个人类可引导、CAS增强的可扩展AI辅助数学研究环境，支持可复现的长期数学推理任务。

Abstract: Large language models can contribute useful ideas to mathematical research, yet long-horizon proof attempts remain difficult to coordinate, evaluate, and reproduce. We present Albilich, an open-source agentic harness for autoresearch in mathematics that combines long-horizon reasoning, computer algebra systems (CAS), literature retrieval, and persistent SQLite-based context management.
  We evaluate Albilich on the RealMath benchmark (Zhang et al. 2025) and on open problems in group theory from the Kourovka Notebook (Khukhro and Mazurov 2026). It solved 10/10 problems on RealMath with CAS and 9/10 with no CAS. On the Kourovka problems, Albilich produced a counterexample to Problem 21.142 and a proof of a strengthening of Problem20.2. Anablation on Problem 17.91 demonstrates 32.0% token reduction when CAS is enabled. An ablation on Problem 21.142 demonstrates higher verifier-rejection rate and failure to synthesize proof routes in the absence of the advisor agent. These results support Albilich as a human-steerable, CAS-boosted environment for scalable AI-assisted mathematical research.

</details>


### [212] [MECA: A Mechanism-Centered Agent for Constructing Well-Specified and Valuable Mathematical Conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27709)
*Wentao Long,Yunfei Zhang,Chenyi Li,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MECA，一个以机制为中心的多智能体框架，通过联合开发候选命题及其支撑机制来自动构造精确定义且有价值的数学猜想。实验表明该方法能生成定义良好、值得研究且对现有自动证明器具有挑战性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动数学猜想构造常面临命题过于宽泛、定义不完整、难以与可行证明或反驳策略建立联系等问题，缺乏一个能同时保证命题精确性和数学价值的系统化框架。

Method: MECA采用多智能体协作框架：Explorer智能体提出机制（如不等式、不变量、分解或中间命题化简）、测试其适用性并据此修订候选猜想；Critic智能体评估命题的数学有效性和研究价值，其反馈引导对假设、范围和结论的调整。通过将宽泛研究方向转化为具备实质性数学支撑且保留明确未解决核心的精确猜想。

Result: 在两个场景中评估：一是与生成-修订基线对比，从目标条件化但文章不可见的来源材料中重构选定目标论文的结论；二是从文献种子和现有开放问题构建100个半开放问题，并利用自动证明器的独立证明和反驳尝试进行评估。结果表明机制中心化的细化能产出定义良好、具有研究价值的猜想，且对现有自动证明器仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 机制中心的细化方法能够有效生成定义精确且具有研究价值的数学猜想，这类猜想对当前自动证明器仍保持挑战性，为AI辅助数学发现提供了可行的新路径。

Abstract: Automatically constructing well-specified and valuable mathematical conjectures remains a central challenge in AI-assisted mathematical discovery. Many existing open problems and conjectures are often too broad, underspecified, or difficult to connect to plausible proof or refutation strategies. We view a mathematical mechanism as a structure or reasoning principle that connects the assumptions of a candidate problem to its target conclusion, such as an inequality, invariant, decomposition, or reduction to an intermediate claim. We present MECA (MEchanism-centered Conjecture Agent), a multi-agent framework that constructs conjectures by jointly developing candidate statements and their supporting mechanisms. Explorer agents propose mechanisms, test how they apply, and revise the candidate conjecture accordingly, while critic agents assess their mathematical validity and research value. Their feedback guides changes to the assumptions, scope, and conclusion. Through this process, MECA transforms broad research directions into precise conjectures with substantive mathematical support while retaining a clearly identified unresolved core. We evaluate MECA in two complementary settings. First, we compare it with a generate-and-revise baseline on reconstructing preselected target-paper conclusions from target-conditioned but article-blind source materials. Second, we construct 100 semi-open problems from literature-derived seeds and existing open problems and evaluate them through independent proof and refutation attempts by automated provers. Our results indicate that mechanism-centered refinement produces well-specified and research-worthy conjectures that remain challenging for current automated provers.

</details>


### [213] [New Synchronous Computation Dynamics for Hopfield Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27720)
*Francisco Requena-Domínguez,Rafaela Benítez-Rochel,Ezequiel López-Rubio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散微分滤波器（DDF）的新型同步动力学（SD-DDF），用于Hopfield网络，通过每次同时更新一个或多个神经元并确保每步能量下降最大，从而显著缩短总处理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Hopfield网络采用异步（顺序）更新机制，每次仅更新一个神经元，处理速度较慢。作者希望减少处理时间，因此需要开发一种能够同时更新多个神经元且保证收敛的新动力学方法。

Method: 作者提出了离散微分滤波器（DDF）这一新工具，并基于它开发了同步动力学SD-DDF。该方法在每一步计算使得能量下降最大的网络状态，同时确保收敛性。由于计算最优同步状态本身是一个组合优化问题，DDF被用于高效求解该问题。

Result: 论文包含理论论证和四个计算实验，通过实验评估了SD-DDF在处理时间上的加速效果。

Conclusion: SD-DDF同步动力学能够在保证收敛的前提下实现每步最大能量下降，从而确保最短的总处理时间，并通过实验验证了其相对于原始异步动力学的处理时间优势。

Abstract: The dynamics of the original Hopfield network is asynchronous (sequential) (updates the state of only one neuron per time step). In this paper, we propose a new tool and a new dynamics to reduce the processing time by updating one or more neurons simultaneously per instant while ensuring process convergence and aiming for the maximum energy decrease at each step, thus guaranteeing the shortest total processing time. From the point of view of synchronous dynamics, calculating the next network state at which energy decreases the most from the current state while ensuring convergence is itself a combinatorial optimization problem. We develop and use a new tool to solve it. We call this new tool Discrete Differential Filter (DDF) and, based upon it, we develop a new synchronous dynamics which we call SD-DDF (Synchronous Dynamics based upon Discrete Differential Filter). In this paper, we review the original asynchronous dynamics for Hopfield networks and present a new tool and a new synchronous dynamics with its theoretical justification and four computational experiments to assess the speed up in processing time empirically.

</details>


### [214] [Baikal: Structured Search for Deep Research over Data Lakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27726)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Rishitha Guttapalle Mohan,Aarti Kumari,Ashi Sinha,Athulya Anil,Kavitha Srinivas,Horst Samulowitz,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Baikal框架，将数据湖上的深度研究形式化为有预算的搜索问题，通过将异构证据聚类为语义区域并自适应搜索来平衡探索与利用，在HybridQA和TAT-QA数据湖上相比最强基线分别提升28%和36%的报告得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究方法采用迭代检索与生成的方式，让累积上下文决定下一步调查内容，这容易过度利用局部有希望的证据，并在固定预算下无法覆盖不同的语义区域，导致研究覆盖不全。

Method: Baikal框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将异构证据聚类为语义区域；(2) 在区域内生成并调查区域接地子问题，利用发现质量作为奖励更新区域价值估计；(3) 采用多种区域选择策略（随机、LLM引导、贝叶斯ε-greedy和UCB）在不同区域间进行搜索以平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在包含10,993和2,757张表格的数据湖（结合227K维基百科段落和13K财报段落）上，对15个查询进行评估，使用涵盖接地性、相关性、多样性和实用性的新评分标准。Baikal最佳配置相比最强基线在HybridQA上提升28%，在TAT-QA上提升36%。

Conclusion: 结构化语义探索对于异构数据湖上的系统性研究和发现具有重要价值，组织并探索语义证据区域能显著提升报告的接地性和多样性，并在相同子问题预算下产出更有用的发现。

Abstract: Deep research over data lakes requires an LLM agent to investigate evidence across thousands of heterogeneous tables and passages to synthesize a report. Existing methods perform iterative retrieval and generation, letting accumulated context determine what to investigate next, which can overexploit locally promising evidence and fail to cover distinct semantic regions under a fixed budget. To address this, we cast deep research over data lakes as a budgeted search problem and present Baikal - a framework that clusters heterogeneous evidence into semantic regions, then searches over them adaptively to balance exploration and exploitation. Within each selected region, Baikal generates and investigates region-grounded subquestions, using finding quality as rewards to update region-level value estimates and guide search under policies ranging from random and LLM-guided selection to Bayesian $ε$-greedy and UCB. We evaluate Baikal on 15 queries each over HybridQA and TAT-QA data lakes containing 10,993 and 2,757 tables, respectively, together with 227K Wikipedia passages and 13K financial report passages. We assess research quality with a new rubric covering groundedness, relevance, diversity, and utility, and use GPT-5-mini to score Baikal and strong baselines, including DeepSearcher and an OpenCode research agent with retrieval and clustering variants. Across both data lakes, Baikal performs strongly under several region-selection policies; its best configuration improves report scores over the strongest baselines by 28% on HybridQA and 36% on TAT-QA. Our analyses attribute these gains to organizing and exploring semantic evidence regions, which improves groundedness and diversity and yields more useful findings under the same subquestion budget. These results demonstrate the value of structured semantic exploration for systematic research and discovery over heterogeneous data lakes.

</details>


### [215] [VeriSkill: A Self-Evolution Framework for Program Verification Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27733)
*Changguo Jia,Tianqi Zhao,Zhiyou Xiao,Weiming Zhang,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriSkill是一个面向程序验证的LLM代理自进化框架，通过将验证失败归因于技能缺陷、提取可复用的诊断特征并迭代精炼候选技能，在多个验证工具、代理框架和LLM后端上持续优于所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的技能自进化方法在处理程序验证任务时存在两个关键问题：无法可靠地识别技能特定的失败，以及无法从模糊的验证器反馈中提取可操作的信号。这使得程序验证中的技能自进化难以实现。

Method: VeriSkill框架包含三个核心步骤：首先将验证失败归因于技能缺陷，其次从故障中提炼诊断特征为可复用的经验教训，最后迭代精炼候选技能，仅接受那些能在提升验证性能的同时保持程序语义的修订。

Result: 实验表明，VeriSkill在多种验证工具、代理框架和LLM后端上均一致地优于所有基线方法，展现了良好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VeriSkill有效解决了程序验证场景下技能自进化的两大核心障碍，通过精确的失败归因和可操作的反馈信号提取，实现了可靠的技能迭代改进，为LLM代理在程序验证领域的自动化应用提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Automating program verification with LLM agents requires generating specifications, annotations, auxiliary lemmas, and tool invocations, all of which depend on reusable skills. A natural remedy is skill self-evolution: distilling skills from trajectories and refining them through feedback. However, existing evolution methods struggle with program verification tasks because they cannot reliably identify skill-specific failures or extract actionable signals from opaque verifier feedback. In this paper, we propose VeriSkill, a self-evolution framework built for program verification. It attributes verification failures to skill deficiencies, distills diagnostic signatures into reusable lessons, and iteratively refines candidate skills, admitting only revisions that improve verification performance while preserving program semantics. Experiments show that VeriSkill consistently outperforms all baselines across multiple verification tools, agent frameworks, and LLM backends.

</details>


### [216] [SpecCal: Ambiguity-Aware Candidate Calibration for Infrared Spectrum-Based Molecular Structure Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27788)
*Yixuan Chen,Bo Liu,Yusen Tan,Guokun Yang,Wenjie Du,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpecCal 是一个无需训练的候选校准框架，用于从红外光谱预测分子结构。它通过重新排序现有候选分子并基于光谱一致性引入额外的结构替代方案，显著提升了多种基础模型在 SMILES 和骨架层面的 top-k 重构性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从红外光谱推断分子结构是一个基本但具挑战性的问题，因为光谱提供的信息有限，不同分子可能因共享相似官能团和局部振动模式而呈现高度相似的光谱响应。现有 IR-to-molecule 模型生成的有序候选集主要受模型学习偏好影响，可能无法充分涵盖最符合观测光谱约束的分子结构。

Method: SpecCal 是一个即插即用、模型无关的候选校准框架。它作用于现有基础模型的候选输出，通过两种方式改进预测集：(1) 基于光谱一致性重新排序当前候选分子；(2) 引入额外结构上可行的替代候选。整个过程无需任何参数更新，可与多种基础模型集成。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，SpecCal 在不同基础模型上一致地提升了 SMILES 和骨架层面的 top-k 分子重构性能。进一步分析证明，在光谱歧义下校准候选集是改进分子重构的实用途径。

Conclusion: 在光谱存在歧义的条件下，通过对候选集进行校准来改善 IR 光谱到分子结构的重构是可行且有效的。SpecCal 提供了一种无需训练的通用方案，可灵活集成到现有模型中，为红外光谱分子结构预测提供了实用的改进方向。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from infrared (IR) spectra is a fundamental yet challenging problem. A key difficulty is that an IR spectrum provides limited structural information: different molecules may share similar functional groups and local vibrational patterns, leading to highly similar spectral responses. Thus, even when an observed spectrum has a unique underlying structure, reconstructing it from the spectrum remains ambiguous. Existing IR-to-molecule models usually generate a ranked set of candidate molecules, but this set is largely determined by the model's learned generation preference and may not fully capture the structures that best satisfy the observed spectral constraints. To address this limitation, we propose SpecCal, a training-free candidate calibration framework for IR-to-molecule prediction. SpecCal operates on the candidate outputs of existing base models and improves the prediction set by re-ranking current candidates while introducing additional structurally plausible alternatives guided by spectral consistency. The framework is plug-and-play and model-agnostic, requiring no parameter updates for integration with diverse base models. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that SpecCal consistently improves top-k reconstruction at both SMILES and scaffold levels across different base models. Further analyses demonstrate that calibrating candidate sets under spectral ambiguity provides a practical way to improve molecular reconstruction from IR spectra. The code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SpecCal-B18A.

</details>


### [217] [Annotating Topical Legal Insights from Case Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27792)
*Subinay Adhikary,Dwaipayan Roy,Debasis Ganguly,Shouvik Kumar Guha,Kripabandhu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LeDA系统，一个用于法律数据标注的Web系统，支持动态创建新标签来标注法律案件中的概念，从而将法律文档从扁平文本表示为概念袋（bag-of-concepts），以支持后续下游任务如先例检索和判决预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文档采用结构化表示（而非词袋式扁平文本）能显著增强处理能力，因此需要从法律案件诉讼中识别和标注概念或主题。

Method: 提出了LeDA（Legal Data Annotation）系统，提供基于Web界面的通用标注和裁决功能；其新颖特性是支持动态创建新标注标签，适用于尚无预定义本体论、需由标注者在检视文档过程中自行发现概念的场景。

Result: 系统目前正在用于标注法律文档中的概念集，以构建文档的语义表示（概念袋）；并描述了3名评估者使用LeDA对印度最高法院案件诉讼中的法律概念名称进行标注和裁决的实践过程。

Conclusion: LeDA通过灵活的动态标注机制，为法律文档概念化提供了一种实用工具，支持先例检索和判决预测等下游应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we mainly concentrate on finding concepts or topics from the legal case proceedings, since adopting a structured representation for legal documents, as opposed to a mere bag-of-words flat text representation, can significantly enhance processing capabilities. To achieve this objective, we put forward a set of diverse concepts for legal case proceedings. With this motivation, we propose LeDA, a system for Legal Data Annotation. The system offers the generic functionality of annotating and adjudicating entities or concepts within documents via a web-based interface. A novel feature of our system is that it allows to dynamic create new tags for annotation, which is a particularly useful provision for situations where there exists no pre-defined ontology for the entities (concepts) that need to be annotated - these being rather discovered by annotators as they continue examining more documents. The system that we demonstrate is currently in use to annotate a set of concepts from legal documents to construct semantic representations of documents as bags of concepts that can then be used for several downstream tasks, such as prior case retrieval, judgment prediction, and so on. Along with the system features in general, we also describe how LeDA was used by 3 assessors to annotate and adjudicate legal concept names from Indian Supreme Court case proceedings.

</details>


### [218] [MemeBench: What LVLMs Miss When Interpreting Culture-Dependent Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27798)
*Weihang Wang,Kainan Tu,Jielei Zhang,Run Yang,Boheng Sheng,Yuchen He,Yu Xie,Pengyu Chen,Peiyi Li,Huyang Sun,Longwen Gao,Zhouhui Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemeBench诊断基准，包含1,253个中英文模因及其VIKR标注，分解解释中的视觉线索、身份链接、知识单元和推理机制。测试26个LVLM发现所有模型都存在视觉-知识差距，最强模型仍有22.6%的差距。提出KAR检索基线，可将VIKR成功率提升3.6-7.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在描述视觉内容方面有所进步，但准确描述不等于正确解释——当含义依赖于超出像素的知识时（如模因依赖文化实体、背景知识和社区惯例），模型往往无法正确理解。现有模因基准大多简化为标签或整体评分，无法定位解释在哪个环节出现问题。

Method: 构建MemeBench基准数据集（1,253个中英文模因，聚焦动漫、漫画、游戏及相关网络亚文化），采用VIKR模式将解释分解为视觉线索、身份链接、知识单元和推理机制四个维度，并引入基于CultureBase的实体引导检索基线KAR。

Result: 26个LVLM在所有模型中都表现出对可见内容的覆盖优于解释所需的知识；最强模型仍存在22.6%的视觉-知识差距。KAR在四个受控模型上将VIKR成功率提升3.6-7.4%，相比通用检索能修复更多答案且破坏更少。但两种检索条件均改善了身份和知识维度，同时在每个比较中都降低了视觉覆盖率。

Conclusion: MemeBench能够揭示解释是否成功、缺少什么内容，以及针对性证据能否填补已诊断出的差距，为模因理解和LVLM诊断提供了细粒度的评估框架。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have improved at describing visual content, but accurate descriptions do not ensure interpretation when meaning depends on knowledge beyond the pixels. Memes expose this gap because they rely on cultural entities, background knowledge, and community conventions. Most meme benchmarks reduce interpretation to labels or holistic scores, obscuring where an explanation breaks down. We introduce MemeBench, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,253 Chinese and English memes with human-written references and quality-controlled VIKR annotations, centered on anime, comics, games, and adjacent online subcultures. Its VIKR schema decomposes explanations into Visual clues, Identity links, Knowledge units, and Reasoning mechanisms. Across 26 LVLMs, every model covers visible content more reliably than the knowledge needed to interpret it, and even the strongest retains a 22.6% Visual-Knowledge gap. To test whether this diagnosis can guide improvement, we introduce KAR, an entity-guided retrieval baseline built on CultureBase. Across four controlled models, KAR raises VIKR Success by 3.6-7.4% and, compared with generic retrieval, repairs more answers and breaks fewer. Yet both retrieval conditions improve Identity and Knowledge while reducing Visual coverage in every comparison. MemeBench reveals whether an interpretation succeeds, what is missing, and whether targeted evidence fills the diagnosed gap.

</details>


### [219] [STEREODISCO: Discovering Stereotypicality in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27824)
*Farane Jalali Farahani,Corina Dima,Mojtaba Nayyeri,Raphael H. Heiberger,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STEREODISCO框架，将语义微分法引入LLM内部表征的刻板印象系统研究。该框架从WordNet构建约2000个候选语义轴，通过探针在LLM激活空间中恢复几何轴，并利用统计检验识别刻板印象轴。案例研究表明两个LLM在社交群体评分上彼此的一致性高于与人类的一致性，并发现了前人未研究的刻板印象轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM刻板印象计算研究仅关注社会心理学中少量语义轴，且操作对象是语言模型产生的词嵌入，未能回答LLM中还携带哪些语义轴的刻板印象关联，以及LLM内部如何表征这些轴。

Method: STEREODISCO框架包含三步：从WordNet反义词组构建约2000个候选语义轴；通过探针将每个轴恢复为LLM激活空间中的几何轴；通过概念投影的统计检验识别刻板印象轴。以LLAMA-3-8B-INSTRUCT和MISTRAL-7B-INSTRUCT为实例，应用于社会群体刻板印象研究。

Result: 两个LLM在社交群体评分上彼此的一致性高于与人类评分的共识，表明LLM编码的刻板印象内容与社会心理学记录存在分歧；同时发现了前人未研究的刻板印象轴（如谦逊vs骄傲、心胸狭隘vs心胸开阔、胆小vs勇敢），并经人类标注者独立确认。

Conclusion: STEREODISCO为系统研究LLM内部表征中的刻板印象提供了可扩展框架，揭示了LLM刻板印象与社会心理学既有知识的差异，并发现了新的刻板印象语义轴，为理解和缓解LLM偏见提供了新视角。

Abstract: LLMs encode, convey, and perpetuate stereotypes. Prior computational research focuses on a small set of semantic axes investigated in social psychology, and operates on word embeddings produced by language models, leaving open which other semantic axes carry stereotypical associations in LLMs and how LLMs internally represent such axes. We introduce STEREODISCO, a framework that adapts the semantic differential method (Osgood et al., 1957) to the systematic study of stereotypes in LLM internal representations. STEREODISCO constructs approx. 2,000 candidate semantic axes from WordNet antonym synsets, recovers each as a geometric axis in the LLM's activation space via probing, and identifies stereotypical axes via a statistical test over concept projections. As a case study, we apply STEREODISCO to social group stereotypes with LLAMA-3-8B-INSTRUCT and MISTRAL-7B-INSTRUCT. We find that the two LLMs agree with each other on social group ratings more than with humans, suggesting that LLM-encoded stereotype content diverges from that documented in social psychology. We also discover stereotypical axes not investigated in prior work -- including humble vs. proud, narrow-minded vs. broad-minded, and cowardly vs. brave, which human annotators independently confirm.

</details>


### [220] [Sign Language Question Answering: A New Task, Benchmark, and Baseline for Sign Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27826)
*Shiwei Gan,Lichen Wang,Xiao Liu,Yafeng Yin,Kuizhuang Liu,Sanglu Lu,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了手语问答（SLQA）这一新任务，并构建了两个基于PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily的SignQA基准数据集，覆盖五种问题类别，同时提出一个带有问题条件调制时间下采样模块和领域内知识迁移策略的基线模型，实验表明该基线优于代表性视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语理解任务（如连续手语识别和手语翻译）具有预定义目标，仅学习从手语视频到词汇或句子的固定映射，无法全面评估模型是否真正理解手语视频的语义内容。因此需要更灵活、更全面的评估框架。

Method: 1）提出SLQA新任务，要求模型回答关于手语视频的任意自然语言问题；2）基于PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily，利用模板自动生成问答对，构建覆盖位置推理、结构推理、视觉搜索、词汇识别和翻译理解五类问题的SignQA基准；3）提出基线模型，包含问题条件调制时间下采样模块和领域内知识迁移策略。

Result: 在全部问题类别上，所提出的基线模型一致性地优于代表性视觉语言模型，建立了SLQA任务的强基准。

Conclusion: SLQA为手语理解提供了更全面、更灵活的评估框架，所构建的SignQA基准和基线模型为未来手语问答研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in sign language (SL) understanding (SLU) have led to remarkable progress in tasks such as continuous SL recognition and SL translation. However, these tasks are designed with predefined objectives, requiring models to learn a fixed mapping from sign videos to glosses or spoken-language sentences. As a result, they provide only a limited assessment of whether a model truly understands the semantic content of SL videos. To address this limitation, \textbf{we first propose a new task, Sign Language Question Answering (SLQA)}, which evaluates SL understanding by requiring models to answer arbitrary natural language questions about SL videos. Unlike previous SLU tasks, SLQA provides a more flexible and comprehensive evaluation framework that assesses multiple reasoning capabilities beyond recognition and translation. To facilitate this task, \textbf{we further construct two SignQA benchmarks} based on PHOENIX14T and CSL-Daily by automatically generating question-answer pairs from existing gloss and sentence annotations using carefully designed templates. The resulting datasets cover five complementary question categories, including position reasoning, structural reasoning, visual search, gloss recognition, and translation understanding. \textbf{Finally, we propose a simple yet effective baseline model} equipped with a Question-Conditioned Modulated Temporal Downsampling module and an in-domain knowledge transfer strategy, enabling effective knowledge transfer from existing SLU tasks while enhancing question-aware temporal feature modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our baseline consistently outperforms representative vision-language models across all question categories, establishing a strong benchmark for future research on SLQA. Datasets are available at:{https://huggingface.co/datasets/hulala/SignQA-2026}.

</details>


### [221] [MemTxn: A Transaction Boundary for Source-Supported Updates and Complete-State Recovery in Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27834)
*Hanshuai Cui,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Fanshuai Meng,Qianli Ma,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemTxn是一个位于答案模型之外的管理治理层，为持久化记忆系统提供事务边界，支持更新验证、版本冲突解决和故障恢复。实验表明其在多项基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久化记忆系统虽然改善了存储和检索，但缺乏可靠的事务边界来保证更新的正确性和故障后的恢复能力。可写记忆中的错误会持续存在并破坏未来的行为，因此需要一种治理机制来解决这一问题。

Method: MemTxn采用三层机制：Ordered PatchTest验证写入是否被源支持；Temporal Resolver在事实冲突时选择可见版本；持久化快照日志用于故障后恢复应用可见状态。它作为治理层位于答案模型之外。

Result: 在项目不相交审计中，MemTxn接受全部60个受支持原始项并拒绝所有179个硬负样本。在LongMemEval-S和LoCoMo状态的多键持久故障下，无需知晓实际物理写入集即可恢复完整声明的活动映射。在MemoryAgentBench FactConsolidation中，其在全部12种答案模型配置中取得最高平均F1，在五种代表性设置中比Dense高出17.06-24.07分。

Conclusion: MemTxn通过提供事务边界有效解决了持久化记忆系统的可靠更新与恢复问题，在验证准确性、故障恢复能力和下游任务性能上均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Persistent memory lets long-running large language model agents reuse information across sessions and tasks. Yet errors in writable memory can persist and corrupt future behavior. Existing systems improve storage and retrieval, but they do not provide a transaction boundary for reliable updates and recovery. We therefore propose MemTxn, a governance layer outside the answer model. MemTxn verifies whether an update is supported by its source. It also selects the visible version when facts conflict and restores the application-visible state after a fault. The system uses Ordered PatchTest to validate writes, a Temporal Resolver to select versions, and a durable snapshot journal to recover state. On an item-disjoint audit, MemTxn accepts all 60 supported originals and rejects all 179 hard negatives. Under persistent multi-key faults on LongMemEval-S and LoCoMo states, it restores the complete declared active map without knowing the actual physical write set. On MemoryAgentBench FactConsolidation, MemTxn achieves the highest average F1 across all twelve answer-model configurations. It outperforms Dense by 17.06--24.07 points in five representative settings.

</details>


### [222] [Crossing the Margin Cliff: Toward Relearn-Robust LLM Unlearning via Margin Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27836)
*Xiangyu Yin,Jiaxu Liu,Zhen Chen,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型"反学习"（unlearning）在面对重新学习攻击时的脆弱性，发现其根源在于优化几何中的"边际悬崖"（margin cliff）现象——即各种后处理方法在遗忘数据上的逐token答案边际会收敛到一个狭窄区间。作者提出"边际校准"（Margin Calibration, MC）方法，通过在参考模型的边际上添加非饱和边际铰链损失和KL探针来恢复遗忘侧压力，在多个基准上取得了显著优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型反学习方法在重新学习攻击下表现持续脆弱——仅在TOFU数据集上用20个遗忘样本微调就能大幅恢复遗忘集的ROUGE分数。作者希望理解这种脆弱性的根本原因，并开发一种能抵抗重新学习攻击的反学习方法。

Method: 作者首先通过实验揭示了"边际悬崖"现象：14种后处理方法（涵盖梯度、偏好和蒸馏三大类）在41/42个方法-规模组合中，其逐token答案边际都收敛到保留参考之上的狭窄带宽内。作者从理论上证明，当保留耦合将遗忘内容的诊断对数几率保持在某个下界之上时，边际悬崖必然出现——这是token饱和损失在驻点处诱导的条件。基于此，作者提出边际校准（MC）方法：在参考模型的逐token边际上添加非饱和边际铰链损失，并配合在不相交指令语料上的KL探针，以恢复原生损失饱和时丢失的遗忘侧压力。在梯度主导条件（通过轨迹梯度特征测量验证）下，MC的驻点位于悬崖跨越侧，从而给出了重新学习边际提升的攻击预算上界。

Result: 在TOFU（三个Llama-3规模、三个遗忘层级）、MUSE-News（Llama-2-7B-hf）以及Phi-3.5面板上，单一固定配置的MC方法在全部14个正面比较的遗忘聚合指标和所有有数据的重新学习单元中胜出（面板平均攻击后ROUGE-L从0.41降至0.18），并在13/14个场景中降低了原始成员推理AUC。主要代价是保留侧效用的降低。部署变体无需保留训练参考即可获得相同增益。

Conclusion: 本文从理论到实践系统地揭示并解决了反学习方法在重新学习攻击下的脆弱性问题。边际悬崖是各种现有方法共享的根本缺陷，而边际校准通过非饱和损失设计有效地突破了这一局限，在多个模型和数据集上展现了显著优越的鲁棒性，为反学习研究提供了新的理论和实践框架。

Abstract: Large language model unlearning is consistently fragile under relearn attacks. On TOFU, fine-tuning on twenty forget examples substantially recovers held-out forget-set ROUGE for every method we evaluate, and we trace this fragility to optimization geometry. The per-token answer margin of fourteen post-hoc methods spanning gradient, preference, and distillation families converges into a narrow band above the retain reference in 41 of 42 method--size cells, a regularity we call the margin cliff. We prove that this cliff follows whenever the retain coupling holds the diagnostic log-odds of forget content above a floor, a condition that token-saturating losses induce at stationarity and that we verify directly on 34 of 42 cells. Margin Calibration (\textsc{MC}) is a plug-in polish adding a non-saturating margin hinge anchored at the reference's per-token margin plus a KL probe on a disjoint instruction corpus, restoring forget-side pressure where the native loss saturates. Under a stated gradient-dominance condition, whose on-trajectory gradient signature we measure by instrumenting the polish, its stationary set lies on the cliff-crossing side, yielding an attack-budget upper bound on the relearn margin lift. Across TOFU (three Llama-3 sizes, three forget tiers), MUSE-News on Llama-2-7B-hf, and a Phi-3.5 panel, a single frozen configuration wins all 14 head-to-head forget aggregates and all populated relearn cells (panel-mean post-attack ROUGE-L $0.41$ to $0.18$) and lowers raw membership AUC on 13/14, with reduced retain-side utility as the main cost. A deployment variant matches these gains without a retain-trained reference.

</details>


### [223] [Virtual Process Dossier: A Process-Aware Data Catalogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27840)
*Lukas Kubelka,Alexander Bott,Frank Döhner,Saksham Kiroriwal,Georg Zeeb,Julia Butte,Julius Pfrommer,Jürgen Beyerer,Tobias Käfer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出虚拟工艺档案（VPD），一个基于知识图谱的数据目录系统，同时捕获工作流溯源信息，用于多阶段制造场景中支持下游AI优化任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多阶段制造场景中，下游基于AI的优化任务需要区分各工作流步骤产生的数据集，但缺乏能够以FAIR方式提供这些数据集并显式记录前瞻性和回顾性工作流溯源的解决方案。

Method: VPD包含三个核心贡献：（1）VPD本体作为目录的语义核心；（2）VPD溯源框架将本体实例化集成到生产环境中；（3）VPD用户界面提供与知识图谱的人机交互。

Result: VPD以FAIR方式提供各工作流步骤的数据集，并使前瞻性和回顾性工作流溯源均显式化呈现。本体和代码已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: VPD通过知识图谱技术将数据目录与工作流溯源相结合，为多阶段制造场景中的AI优化任务提供了可追溯、可复用的数据基础设施。

Abstract: We propose the Virtual Process Dossier (VPD), a Knowledge Graph-based data catalogue that also captures workflow provenance. We developed VPD for multi-stage manufacturing use-cases where downstream AI-based optimization tasks require to distinct between datasets generated during individual workflow steps. VPD provides these datasets in a FAIR manner and makes both prospective and retrospective workflow provenance explicit. Our contributions are: (1) the VPD ontology that serves as the catalogue's semantic core; (2) the VPD provenance framework that integrates ontology instantiation into the production environment; and (3) the VPD user interface that provides human-centered interaction with the VPD Knowledge Graph. The ontology and code are available at https://github.com/kubeluk/VirtualProcessDossier .

</details>


### [224] [Simplifying Neural Networks During Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27854)
*Lorenzo Sciandra,Samuele Fonio,Roberto Esposito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受神经坍缩（NC）启发的训练框架，通过逆Fisher准则监控表征动态，识别特征提取与分类的切分点及可简化训练阶段，将尾部层替换为轻量分类头，在保持精度的同时大幅减少参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化深度神经网络的训练动态仍待深入理解，现有研究分别揭示了神经坍缩（NC）的结构化几何特性与隧道效应中仅部分层对特征提取关键的现象，但尚未将二者结合用于网络简化。

Method: 利用逆Fisher准则（Inverse Fisher Criterion）作为变异性坍缩行为的稳定高效代理指标，监控表征动态以确定特征提取与分类之间的切分点，同时识别训练中可以引入简化策略的阶段；随后将网络尾部层替换为轻量级分类头并继续训练精简后的模型。

Result: 在MLP、VGG和ResNet等多种架构的图像分类基准上，该方法实现了显著的参数量缩减，同时保持与完整模型相当的准确率。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了将神经坍缩理论用于网络动态简化的可行性，为深度网络的训练效率优化提供了新思路，且代码已开源以支持复现。

Abstract: Understanding and exploiting the training dynamics of overparameterized deep neural networks remains a central challenge in modern machine learning. Recent evidence on Neural Collapse (NC) shows that class representations and classifiers exhibit highly structured geometry, while the Tunnel Effect suggests that only a subset of layers is essential for feature extraction. We combine these two perspectives and propose an NC-inspired training framework for simplifying deep networks during training. Our method monitors representation dynamics through the Inverse Fisher Criterion, a stable and efficient proxy for the variability collapse behavior, to identify both the split point between feature extraction and classification and the training stage at which simplification becomes viable. We then replace the trailing layers with a lightweight classification head and continue training the reduced model. Experiments on image-classification benchmarks across MLP, VGG, and ResNet architectures show that the proposed method achieves substantial parameter reductions while maintaining accuracy comparable to that of the full model. Code to reproduce the experiments can be found at: https://github.com/LorenzoSciandra/NNS.

</details>


### [225] [Back to All-Entity Ranking: Sampler-Dependent Evaluation in Continuous-Time Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27861)
*Minwoo Yu,Young-guk Ha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间动态图（CTDG）中下一目的地预测任务里，负采样策略对模型评估的影响。研究发现非均匀负采样分布会改变贝叶斯最优排序，均匀采样也会因候选集规模不同而影响模型排名和模块效应测量。作者推荐使用全实体排序（all-entity ranking）作为架构比较的主要评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTDG中的下一目的地预测通常依赖于负采样进行评估，但采样分布和候选数量会影响评分结果，导致模型排名和消融结论不稳定，缺乏可靠性。

Method: 通过因子化实验（区分重复/新正向样本与已见/未见负向样本）、基于对历史成员关系的极简评分器以及受控表示干预，在六个模型和四个数据集（LastFM、MOOC、Reddit、Wikipedia）上进行系统评估，比较Uniform-20期望指标与全目录排序的结果。

Result: 在四个数据集中有三个数据集上，至少一对模型的相对排名在Uniform-20指标与全目录排序之间发生反转；同一模块的效果随候选集规模和训练目标的变化在大小和方向上均发生改变。

Conclusion: 基于采样负样本的基准测试得出的模型优劣和消融结论依赖于候选配置，不可靠。推荐使用全实体排序作为CTDG基准中架构比较的主要评估证据，因为它能消除负采样自由度和采样变异。

Abstract: Next-destination prediction in continuous-time dynamic graphs (CTDGs) commonly ranks an observed interaction against sampled negative destinations. The resulting score is conditional on both the negative distribution and the number of candidates chosen by the researcher. We show that a non-uniform negative distribution changes the Bayes-optimal ranking, while even a finite candidate set drawn uniformly can destabilize model rankings and measured module effects.
  Time-varying source-destination history membership and model operations that use this information directly transmit the sampler's influence to the evaluation score. We examine this mechanism using a factorial evaluation of repeated and new positives against seen and unseen negatives, a minimal scorer based solely on pair-history membership, and controlled representation interventions. Across six models on LastFM, MOOC, Reddit, and Wikipedia, at least one model pair changes relative order between the expected Uniform-20 metric and the full catalog on three of the four datasets. The measured effect of the same module also changes in magnitude and direction with the candidate-set size and training objective.
  These results establish that model-superiority and ablation conclusions from sampled-negative benchmarks are conditional on the stated candidate configuration. All-entity ranking evaluates every destination in a fixed catalog, eliminating negative-selection freedom and sampling variation while retaining the original CTDG scorer. We therefore recommend all-entity ranking as the primary evidence for architecture comparisons on CTDG benchmarks with an enumerable, fixed destination catalog.

</details>


### [226] [Orca: Neural Operators for Causal Reasoning in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27867)
*Gerrit Großmann,David A. Selby,Sebastian J. Vollmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Orca框架，将神经算子学习（neural operator learning）引入结构因果模型（SCM），用于处理随时间连续演化、观测时间不规则且存在反馈循环的系统。Orca将因果图中的每个节点视为时间的函数，每个机制学习为函数空间之间的映射，支持因果干预与反事实推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构因果模型假设变量是静态的、通常只观测一次，且禁止循环依赖。然而许多真实系统（如患者、气候、经济）随时间连续演化、在不规则时间点观测并包含反馈回路，现有的SCM框架无法自然建模这类动态因果系统。

Method: Orca将因果因果图中的每个节点定义为时间的函数，每个因果机制建模为从父节点函数空间到子节点函数空间的神经算子映射。具体而言，机制以多个父函数作为输入，尊重时间箭头（因果方向），并将潜在外生噪声建模为可推断、可复用于反事实推理的函数。作者扩展了现有神经算子架构以表达这些因果机制。

Result: 论文形式化了该模型类别，并在合成连续时间示例上展示了反事实推理能力。实验验证了Orca框架能有效处理连续时间动态系统中的因果干预与反事实问题。

Conclusion: 神经算子学习为连续时间动态系统中的因果推理提供了自然且有前景的基础。Orca框架填补了传统SCM无法处理动态、不规则采样及反馈循环系统的空白。代码已开源供社区研究和扩展。

Abstract: Structural causal models are the standard language for reasoning about interventions and counterfactuals, but they describe static variables, typically measured once, and usually forbid cyclic dependencies. Many systems we care about, such as patients, climates, and economies, instead evolve continuously in time, are observed at irregular time points, and contain feedback loops. We argue that neural operator learning provides a natural foundation for causal reasoning in this setting, and propose Orca, a framework in which each node of the causal graph is a function of time and each mechanism is a learned map between function spaces. We extend existing neural operator architectures to express causal mechanisms: a mechanism computes the function value of a node from its parent nodes by taking several parent functions as input, respects the arrow of time, and treats latent exogenous noise as a function that can be inferred and reused for counterfactuals. We formalize the model class and demonstrate counterfactual reasoning on synthetic continuous-time examples. Code is available at https://github.com/gerritgr/orca

</details>


### [227] [Search as Computation Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27871)
*Alexander Tuisov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文正式化"终端计算分配问题"（terminal computation-allocation problems），即算法在返回最终决策前消耗内部计算资源，且仅通过终端决策损失来评估。通过Bellman方程刻画最优分配，并将计算价值（VOC）与信息论联系起来：互信息在log loss下等价于贪婪VOC，简单遗憾下的VOC是知识梯度量；信息增益对VOC给出单侧上界但可能排序极差。该框架统一了Bandit拉动、树搜索模拟和节点扩展等计算拓扑，并证明在显式前沿解析与启发式误差模型下，最大化近似VOC可恢复加权A*，A*和贪婪最佳优先搜索是极限情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多算法在执行过程中消耗内部计算资源（如bandit拉动、树搜索模拟），但现有的评估框架通常只关注终端输出的质量，缺乏对这些计算资源如何最优分配的正式理论刻画。作者旨在为这类问题建立统一的决策理论框架，并揭示计算价值与信息论之间的深层关系。

Method: 将终端计算分配问题形式化为Bellman方程，覆盖固定预算、定价计算和精确认证三种场景；通过理论推导建立VOC与互信息、知识梯度等信息量指标的关系；用不同计算拓扑（bandit拉动、树模拟、节点扩展）统一建模；最后在显式前沿解析与启发式误差模型下推导出最大化近似VOC与加权A*的等价性。

Result: 证明互信息等于log loss下的贪婪VOC，简单遗憾下VOC是知识梯度量；信息增益虽然给出VOC的单侧上界，但可能对计算排序极差。在启发式搜索场景下，最大化近似VOC恢复加权A*，其中A*和贪婪最佳优先搜索是加权参数的两个极限情形。

Conclusion: 该理论揭示了一个共享的决策问题结构，但并不断言某一种特定的采集规则在任何场景下都是最优的。不同计算拓扑和信息度量下的最优策略各有适用条件，为理解各类基于计算分配的算法提供了统一的决策理论基础。

Abstract: Many algorithms spend an internal resource before returning a decision and are evaluated only by the quality of that terminal output. We formalize such procedures as terminal computation-allocation problems: costly computations produce observations, update beliefs about a latent environment, and matter only through terminal decision loss. Bellman equations characterize optimal allocation under fixed budgets, priced computation, and exact certification. We then relate value of computation (VOC) to information. Mutual information equals myopic VOC under log loss, whereas under simple regret VOC is a knowledge-gradient quantity; moreover, information gain can rank computations arbitrarily poorly, although it gives a one-sided upper bound on VOC. Bandit pulls, tree simulations, and node expansions illustrate the same model under different computation topologies. Finally, under an explicit frontier-resolution and heuristic-error model, maximizing approximate VOC recovers weighted A*, with A* and greedy best-first search as limiting cases. The theory identifies a shared decision problem without asserting that one acquisition rule is universally optimal.

</details>


### [228] [An Empirical Study of Coordination Mode as the First-Class Citizen in From-Scratch Multi-Agent Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27877)
*Yanyu Ren,Yunfeng Bai,Xizheng Wang,Li Chen,Dan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MSEval基准测试，用于在真实全栈项目上评估多智能体协作编码，并引入LegoGent执行引擎测试10种协作拓扑，通过TAgent自动化评分器联合衡量功能成功率、延迟和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体编码基准依赖合成环境，忽略了实际的时间和金钱成本，混淆推理与通信，且只奖励表面完成度，无法真实反映多智能体团队的软件开发能力。

Method: 构建基于10个跨领域真实全栈项目的MSEval基准，使用分层需求和确定性评分规则；通过LegoGent引擎测试10种协作拓扑（周期性同步间隔协调、原生CI/CD部署）；使用TAgent自动化评分器动态探测实现，联合衡量功能成功率、延迟和前缀缓存令牌成本。

Result: 在100次运行中，组织拓扑对速度-成本-质量权衡的影响与模型能力相当；相同任务和模型下，不同拓扑可使分数偏差超过30分并使墙钟时间翻倍；结构化流水线收敛最快且质量最高，而重度管理监督会降低性能。

Conclusion: MSEval建立了严格的、可复现的多智能体团队软件构建能力评估标准，揭示了组织拓扑在塑造多智能体编码性能中的关键作用。

Abstract: Multi-agent vibe coding promises to accelerate software development, yet existing benchmarks rely on synthetic environments that ignore practical time and monetary costs, conflate reasoning with communication, and reward only superficial completion. We introduce multi-agent from-scratch evaluation benchmark, MSEval, evaluating multi-agent coding on real-world tasks. Grounded in 10 authentic, full-stack projects across 10 domains, MSEval scores performance using hierarchical requirements and deterministic rubrics.
  Its execution engine, LegoGent, tests 10 collaboration topologies where agents coordinate via periodic sync intervals and deploy through native CI/CD pipelines. Concurrently, the automated grader TAgent dynamically probes implementations to jointly measure functional success, latency, and prefix-cached token cost. Across 100 runs, MSEval reveals that organizational topology rivals model capability in shaping the speed--cost--quality trade-off. For identical tasks and models, varying the topology shifts scores by over 30 points and doubles wall-clock time. Structured pipelines converge fastest with the highest quality, whereas heavy managerial oversight degrades performance. Ultimately, MSEval establishes a rigorous, reproducible standard for measuring how multi-agent teams actually build software. The benchmark is released at https://github.com/robinren03/MSEval.

</details>


### [229] [Not All Tokens Deserve Equal Credit: Counterfactual Sensitivity Credit Reallocation for Long-CoT Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27888)
*Qiangqiang He,Zhongheng Wu,ZiJian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现RLVR中传统方法（如GRPO、OPSD）将奖励均匀分配给所有token或依赖特权自蒸馏的方向性监督均不可靠——大多数受影响token在正确与错误两种相反条件下朝同一方向移动，且大位移集中在可替代的表面形式token上。据此提出Counterfactual Sensitivity Credit Reallocation (CSCR)，降低高敏感token的信用并重新归一化优势，在长CoT数学推理基准上稳定超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR对提升大模型长思维链推理至关重要，但现有方法存在缺陷：GRPO将response级奖励均匀广播到所有token，忽略了各token对最终结果的不等贡献；OPSD通过特权自教师提供密集分布监督，但其隐含假设——似然位移编码可靠的答案对齐信息——缺乏验证。本文旨在测试这一前提并寻求更优的token级信用分配方案。

Method: 首先通过"固定轨迹、在两种相反结果条件下重新评分"的实验诊断OPSD的privileged shifts是否可靠。基于诊断发现（位移方向不一致、大小主要反映反事实敏感性），提出CSCR：作为GRPO的简单扩展，降低高敏感性token的信用并将token级优势重新归一化，以保持原始信用预算和验证器决定的方向。

Result: 在长CoT数学推理基准上，CSCR在相同策略更新次数下一致优于GRPO基线。针对性消融实验证实了诊断结论：特权诱导的方向不可靠，适度的降权最有效，过强调制会破坏优化稳定性。

Conclusion: token位移的幅度主要反映反事实敏感性而非token级学习价值，CSCR通过减少高敏感token的信用分配有效改善RLVR优化质量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is central to improving long-CoT reasoning in large language models. Critic-free methods such as GRPO convert response-level rewards into advantages and uniformly broadcast them across tokens, overlooking their unequal contributions to the final outcome. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) instead provides dense distributional supervision by minimizing the forward KL divergence between an unprivileged policy and a privileged self-teacher, implicitly assuming that the resulting likelihood shifts encode reliable answer-aligned information. We test this premise by fixing each sampled trajectory and re-scoring it under two opposing outcome conditions, one asserting correctness and the other incorrectness. Most affected tokens shift in the same direction under both conditions, with few sign reversals and substantial overlap in the induced optimization signals. Large shifts also concentrate on highly substitutable surface-form tokens, whereas tokens carrying problem-specific reasoning content are less sensitive. These findings show that privileged shifts fail to provide reliable answer-aligned directions, while their magnitudes primarily reflect counterfactual sensitivity rather than token-level learning value. Based on these observations, we propose Counterfactual Sensitivity Credit Reallocation (CSCR), a simple extension of GRPO that reduces credit for highly sensitive tokens and renormalizes token-level advantages to preserve both the original credit budget and verifier-determined direction. On long-CoT mathematical reasoning benchmarks, CSCR consistently outperforms GRPO baseline with the same number of policy updates. Targeted ablations further corroborate our diagnosis: privilege-induced directions are unreliable, moderate downweighting is most effective, and stronger modulation destabilizes optimization.

</details>


### [230] [Dynamic Spectral Filtering for Temporal Graph Learning: Learning Evolving Propagation Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27891)
*Yan Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出动态谱滤波（DSF），将图传播机制本身视为随时间演化的对象，通过带时间相关向量系数的切比雪夫多项式滤波器表示每个时间快照的传播过程，实现高效的时序图链接预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序图学习方法通常关注节点状态演化或交互历史编码，但很少探讨图传播机制本身是否应随时间演化。作者希望探索这一未被充分研究的算子中心视角。

Method: DSF将传播表示为具有向量值、时变系数的切比雪夫多项式滤波器，将这些紧凑的多阶系数显式视为循环时序状态。一个循环分支提出系数更新，乘性全局和阶次特定门控调节更新幅度，且时序状态与节点数量无关。

Result: 在MOOC、Wikipedia和Reddit三个时序链接预测基准上，收敛后的DSF分别达到0.7851、0.9088和0.9860的AP分数，仅需93K-133K可训练参数、68-182MB峰值GPU内存和每轮1.6-2.1秒训练时间。相比同类DEFT基线，参数减少8.3-8.6倍，GPU内存减少25-33倍，每轮时间减少5-19倍。

Conclusion: 直接进行谱响应演化是一种有用的时序归纳偏置，尤其在计算效率作为首要需求时，DSF以极小资源开销达到与强基线相当的性能。

Abstract: Temporal graph learning is commonly organized around the evolution of node states or the encoding of interaction histories. We study an underexplored, operator-centric question: should the graph propagation mechanism itself evolve over time? We introduce Dynamic Spectral Filtering (DSF), which represents propagation at snapshot t by a Chebyshev polynomial filter with vector-valued, time-dependent coefficients. DSF explicitly treats these compact multi-order coefficients as recurrent temporal states. A recurrent branch proposes updates, while multiplicative global and order-specific gates regulate their magnitude. The temporal state is independent of the number of nodes. On MOOC, Wikipedia, and Reddit temporal link-prediction benchmarks, converged DSF runs attain AP scores of 0.7851, 0.9088, and 0.9860, respectively, with 93K to 133K trainable parameters, 68 to 182 MB peak GPU memory, and 1.6 to 2.1 seconds of training per epoch. Against the closely related DEFT baseline, DSF is better on MOOC, within 0.001 AP on Reddit, and modestly lower on Wikipedia, while using 8.3 to 8.6 times fewer parameters, 25 to 33 times less GPU memory, and 5 to 19 times less time per epoch. Relative to all measured alternatives, it uses 3.3 to 38.6 times less GPU memory. These results support direct spectral-response evolution as a useful temporal inductive bias when computational efficiency is a first-class requirement.

</details>


### [231] [MMHBench: A Multi-Perspective Benchmark for Mental Health Understanding in Long-Form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27895)
*Jinpeng Hu,Erqiang Wang,Shan Wang,Zhuo Li,Peipei Song,Xun Yang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MMHBench——一个面向长视频心理健康理解的多视角多模态基准，包含268个长视频和2,184个精心设计的问题，并提出了多智能体问题生成（MAQG）框架来从不同社会角色视角合成问题。对22个代表性多模态大语言模型的评估表明，长视频心理健康理解仍然极具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将心理健康理解简化为粗粒度分类任务，无法验证模型是否真正理解心理现象还是依赖表面相关性，缺乏对模型多视角推理能力的深入评估。

Method: 构建MMHBench基准，包含两个互补评估设置：（1）第三人称评估（605题）关注可观察行为和多模态证据的解读；（2）第一人称视角换位推理（1,579题）要求基于视角条件的推理。提出多智能体问题生成（MAQG）框架，模拟多种社会角色合成问题，通过多角色反馈和迭代优化精炼，并经专家验证保证质量。

Result: 对22个代表性多模态大语言模型（涵盖开源和领先闭源模型）的广泛评估表明，长视频心理健康理解任务仍然高度具有挑战性，现有模型在该基准上的表现有限。

Conclusion: 长视频心理健康理解仍是多模态大语言模型面临的重大挑战，MMHBench为未来研究提供了更全面的评估基准，揭示了现有模型在多视角心理健康推理方面的不足。

Abstract: Mental health understanding in long-form videos requires nuanced reasoning over observable behavior, interpersonal context, and latent psychological states. Existing benchmarks largely reduce this task to coarse-grained classification, providing limited insight into whether models truly understand psychological phenomena or rely on superficial correlations. To address this limitation, we introduce MMHBench, a comprehensive multimodal benchmark for multi-perspective mental health understanding, comprising 268 long-form videos and 2,184 carefully curated questions. MMHBench organizes the evaluation into two complementary settings: (1) third-person assessment, consisting of 605 questions that focus on the interpretation of observable behaviors and multimodal evidence, and (2) first-person perspective-taking, comprising 1,579 questions that require perspective-conditioned reasoning to identify the interpretation of the mental state supported by the available multimodal evidence. We propose a Multi-Agent Question Generation (MAQG) framework that simulates diverse social roles to synthesize questions from multiple perspectives. The generated questions are refined through multi-role feedback and iterative optimization, followed by expert-guided verification to ensure quality and validity. Extensive evaluation of 22 representative multimodal large language models (MLLMs), spanning both open-source and leading closed-source models, demonstrates that long-form video mental health understanding remains highly challenging.

</details>


### [232] [A Cross-Architecture Audit of Direction-Based Inference-Time Defences in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27910)
*Xiangyu Yin,Tora Bodin,Rohan Menon,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种推理时防御方法在15个模型-层组合（来自四个架构家族）上的表现，发现没有任何单一候选方法能在拒绝恢复和效用保持两方面都占优；图像条件偏移在LLaVA 1.5和Pixtral 12B上领先且效用损失始终处于测量噪声底，提示指令在Qwen2.5 VL上领先，攻击特定残差在Qwen2 VL 2B上领先。CMRM方向与图像条件偏移在所有15个单元中余弦对齐（均值0.35），表明两种方法恢复部分重叠的几何结构，但方向防御需针对每个语言解码器家族单独校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）面临越狱攻击威胁，现有推理时防御通常从残差流中减去校准方向，但不同防御候选方法的优劣尚未在受控条件下系统比较，且跨架构的可迁移性未知。

Method: 采用幅度受控协议：对每个提示匹配干预大小，并将每个方向与同范数的随机对照配对。比较五种候选方法：均值图像条件偏移、CMRM风格拒绝方向、ShiftDC风格攻击特定残差、忽略图像的提示指令、随机对照。在15个模型-层组合（四个架构家族）上评估拒绝恢复与效用保持。

Result: 没有单一候选在所有指标上占优：图像条件偏移在LLaVA 1.5和Pixtral 12B上领先，且是唯一在每一家族中效用损失保持测量噪声底的候选；提示指令在Qwen2.5 VL上领先；攻击特定残差在Qwen2 VL 2B上领先。图像条件方向在15个单元中的13个是方向特异的，但强架构特异且不可迁移。CMRM方向与图像条件偏移在15个单元中余弦对齐，均值0.35，范围0.17-0.65，为随机向量零假设的15-25倍，符号检验p值约3e-5。

Conclusion: CMRM风格拒绝方向与图像条件偏移恢复了部分重叠的几何结构，说明文本与多模态拒绝几何存在联系；但方向防御必须针对每个语言解码器家族单独校准，不能跨架构直接迁移。

Abstract: Inference time defences against vision language model jailbreaks often subtract a calibrated direction from the residual stream at a chosen decoder layer. We compare five defence candidates across 15 model and layer cells from four architectural families under a magnitude controlled protocol that matches the intervention size for each prompt and pairs every direction with a random control of the same norm. The candidates are the mean image conditioning shift, a CMRM style refusal direction, a ShiftDC style attack specific residual, a prompt instruction to ignore the image, and a random control. No single candidate dominates on both refusal recovery and utility preservation. The image conditioning shift leads on LLaVA 1.5 and Pixtral 12B and is the only candidate whose utility loss remains at the measurement noise floor in every family. The prompt instruction leads on Qwen2.5 VL, while the attack specific residual leads on Qwen2 VL 2B. The image conditioning direction is direction specific in 13 of 15 cells, but strongly architecture specific and nontransferable across the only dimension compatible pair, LLaVA 1.5 13B and Pixtral 12B. We also connect text only and multimodal refusal geometry. The CMRM direction has positive cosine alignment with the image conditioning shift in all 15 cells, with mean 0.35, range 0.17 to 0.65, 15 to 25 times the random vector null, and a sign test p value of about 3e-5. These results show that the two recipes recover partially overlapping geometry and that direction based defences should be calibrated separately for each language decoder family.

</details>


### [233] [IFHierBench: Hierarchical Instruction Following for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27912)
*Yuetian Mao,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IFHierBench，一个包含600个提示的分层指令遵循基准，覆盖四种约束树深度和35种约束，每个提示配有确定性检查器。评估七个领先模型后发现，即使最强模型在提示级准确率上也仅略超50%，且准确率随约束深度增加急剧下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令遵循基准将约束视为均匀作用于整个响应的扁平列表，无法将检查范围限定到输出的特定部分。但实际部署中，LLM常常需要在一个提示中处理具有层级结构的输出，其整体工件、结构章节和嵌套字段各自需要满足具体约束。

Method: 构建IFHierBench基准，包含600个提示，按四种约束树深度分层，涵盖35种不同约束。每个提示配有确定性检查器，用于验证每个作用域的约束满足情况。使用该基准评估七个领先的专有和开源权重模型。

Result: 即使最强的模型在提示级准确率上也仅略超50%，且准确率随着约束深度增长急剧下降。模型在可靠地遵循嵌套约束方面存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在可靠遵循嵌套/层级约束方面仍存在实质性差距，这促使未来的训练方法需要在更细粒度上考虑约束遵循，以提升指令遵循能力。

Abstract: Instruction-following ability is critical for deploying large language models in real-world applications, where downstream components depend on the output satisfying specific constraints. Modern deployments increasingly handle the full task in a single LLM call, with one prompt specifying a layered output whose overall artifact, structural sections, and nested fields must each satisfy concrete constraints. Existing instruction-following benchmarks treat the constraint set as a flat list applied uniformly to the response, so they cannot scope a check to a particular section of the output. We introduce IFHierBench, a hierarchical instruction-following benchmark of 600 prompts stratified across four constraint-tree depths and 35 distinct constraints, each prompt paired with a deterministic checker that verifies satisfaction at every scope. Evaluating seven leading proprietary and open-weight models, we find that even the strongest model only marginally exceeds 50% prompt-level accuracy and that accuracy degrades sharply as constraint depth grows. Reliably following nested constraints remains a substantial gap for current LLMs, motivating future training methods that consider constraint adherence at finer granularity to achieve better instruction-following ability.

</details>


### [234] [One Anchor for All: Unified Multilingual and Multimodal Safety Alignment for LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27917)
*Enyi Shi,Fei Shen,Chuancheng Shi,Linxia Zhu,Shuyi Miao,Jinhui Tang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经元级别的跨维度安全对齐框架（MLS-Neurons），通过识别跨语言和跨模态共享的安全神经元，仅更新约0.03%的参数即可将英语安全监督迁移到多语言和多模态场景，有效防御复合攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在全球部署时面临多语言指令与视觉信息结合的复合攻击，但现有防御方法将语言和模态防御割裂，加之安全数据稀缺和微调成本高昂，难以有效应对此类攻击。

Method: 1) 通过对比有害与良性样本的响应，识别单语言、单模态的安全神经元；2) 在同一语言内对单模态神经元取交集，提取模态共享安全神经元（MS-Neurons）；3) 以英语为语义锚点，跨语言取交集得到模态与语言共享安全神经元（MLS-Neurons）；4) 仅更新这一最小共享神经元子集（约0.03%参数）进行安全对齐。

Result: 在多种多语言和多模态安全基准测试中，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法，同时保持了模型的一般实用性。

Conclusion: 通过定位跨语言和跨模态共享的安全神经元，可以用极低的参数更新成本实现跨维度的安全对齐，为LVLMs的复合攻击防御提供了高效且可迁移的解决方案。

Abstract: As large vision-language models (LVLMs) are deployed globally, the combination of multilingual instructions and visual information makes malicious attacks more covert and sophisticated than ever before. However, existing methods isolate language and modality defenses, which, coupled with the scarcity of safety data and high fine-tuning costs, makes it difficult for models to defend against compound attacks. To address this severe challenge, we propose a neuron-level cross-dimensional safety alignment framework driven by modality- and language-shared safety neurons (MLS-Neurons). First, we identify monolingual and unimodal safety neurons by comparing responses to harmful and benign samples, quantifying functional saliency through activation strength and downstream impact. Then, by intersecting these unimodal neurons within each language, we extract modality-shared safety neurons (MS-Neurons) responsive to both visual and textual risks, bridging the safety representation gap between modalities. Furthermore, using English as a semantic anchor, we intersect MS-Neurons across languages to identify modality- and language-shared safety neurons (MLS-Neurons), serving as key defenses against compound attacks. Finally, we update only this minimal subset of shared neurons (~0.03% of parameters), transferring English-only safety supervision to multilingual and multimodal scenarios. Extensive experiments show that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across diverse multilingual and multimodal safety benchmarks while preserving general utility.

</details>


### [235] [Meta-Task: Turning Terminal Task Synthesis into a Terminal Task for Scalable Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27929)
*Zhihong Pan,Jiyuan He,Kai Zhang,Yupeng Han,Ze Liu,Yuze Zhao,Yongcong Ye,Zhaohua Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Meta-Task框架，将终端任务合成重新定义为Terminal-Bench格式任务本身，让智能体在真实容器环境中迭代生成、执行和验证任务，从而解决现有任务合成方法可靠性弱和多样性受限的问题。实验表明仅用3,221条合成轨迹微调，Qwen3-14B和Qwen3-32B分别达到22.5%和31.8%的Avg Pass@1，优于同期方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练终端智能体需要多样化、可验证的终端任务和高质交互轨迹，但现有数据合成方法存在两大局限：任务生成与真实执行脱节导致可靠性弱；依赖现有仓库导致多样性和可扩展性受限。

Method: Meta-Task将任务合成本身建模为Terminal-Bench格式任务：智能体在真实容器中迭代生成、执行、验证任务，确保合成组件在生成循环内即被检查内部一致性和可执行性。同时沿多个维度解耦目标任务需求，引入多阶段机制先动态设计新颖任务规格再生成实际任务，并结合可选外部材料支持增强多样性与真实性，最后通过LLM-as-Judge过滤保证训练数据质量。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0上，仅用3,221条Meta-Task合成轨迹进行微调，Qwen3-14B和Qwen3-32B分别达到22.5%和31.8%的Avg Pass@1，以显著更少的训练数据超越了同期方法。

Conclusion: Meta-Task通过将任务合成闭环嵌入真实执行环境，有效解决了终端任务数据合成的可靠性和多样性瓶颈，展示了以更少数据实现更强终端智能体性能的可行性。

Abstract: Training terminal agents at scale requires diverse, verifiable terminal tasks and high-quality interaction trajectories, yet acquiring such data remains a significant challenge. Existing synthesis methods face two key limitations: (1) weak reliability caused by the disconnect between task generation and real execution, and (2) limited diversity and scalability due to dependence on existing repositories. We propose Meta-Task, a framework that redefines terminal task synthesis as a Terminal-Bench-format task itself: an agent operates within a real container environment to iteratively generate, execute, and verify tasks, so that synthesized components are checked for internal consistency and executability within the generation loop itself. Building upon this, we decouple the target task requirements along multiple dimensions, introduce a multi-phase mechanism that dynamically designs novel task specifications before producing the actual tasks, and incorporate optional external material support to enhance diversity and realism. We additionally apply LLM-as-Judge filtering to ensure the quality of the final training data. Experiments on Terminal-Bench 2.0 show that fine-tuning on only 3,221 Meta-Task synthesized trajectories achieves 22.5% and 31.8% Avg Pass@1 for Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B respectively, outperforming concurrent approaches with significantly less training data.

</details>


### [236] [The Geometric Nature and a Free Proxy for Flow-Matching Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27933)
*Ziyang Rao,Yiren Zhao,Weiyu Guo,Ben Fei,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于去噪加速度（accel）的流匹配（FM）不确定性估计方法，从速度场的几何解释出发，通过单次前向传播测量去噪轨迹的弯曲程度来代理不确定性，无需额外训练、重采样或模型评估，在失败检测任务上匹配甚至超越昂贵的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配已成为现代具身模型的主流动作头范式，但作为一种条件生成模型，它无法显式暴露固有不确定性，在场景误解或遇到分布外（OOD）输入时会产生错误的动作块。现有不确定性估计方法存在额外训练预算、高计算开销和低泛化能力等问题，因此需要一种高效且可靠的不确定性估计方法。

Method: 本文首先对FM不确定性给出速度场中的几何解释，指出不确定性表现为偏离理想仿射各向同性收缩场。基于此观察，提出去噪加速度（accel）作为不确定性代理，通过单次前向传播测量去噪轨迹的弯曲程度，无需额外模型评估、训练或重采样。

Result: 理论和实验均证明accel是FM不确定性的忠实代理。在在线失败检测测试中，accel能在终止前及早识别失败的rollout，在现实部署预算下匹配甚至超越基于重采样和基于训练的昂贵基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种高泛化性且零额外成本的不确定性估计方法，为FM生成动作在具身智能系统中的安全部署提供了可靠的信任判断依据。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) has become a popular action head paradigm for modern embodied models. However, as a conditional generative model, it does not explicitly expose its inherent uncertainty, producing faulty action chunks even when it misinterprets the scene or encounters out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs. Therefore, determining when an FM-generated action can be trusted is essential for safe deployment, yet existing uncertainty estimation methods on real-time control suffer from several issues: extra training budget, high computational overhead, and low generalization ability. In this work, we provide a geometric interpretation of FM uncertainty in the velocity field, showing that uncertainty manifests as deviation from an ideal affine-isotropic contraction field. Building on this observation, we introduce denoising acceleration ($\mathrm{accel}$), a highly-generalizable and cost-free uncertainty proxy that measures the bending of the denoising trajectory from a single forward pass, without additional model evaluations, training, or resampling. We theoretically and empirically demonstrate that $\mathrm{accel}$ is a faithful proxy for FM uncertainty and further test its utility in online failure detection. Results show that $\mathrm{accel}$ identifies failing rollouts well before termination, matching or even outperforming costly resampling- and training-based baselines across settings under realistic deployment budget. Code and demos available at: https://github.com/rrrrrrzy/fm-geometry.

</details>


### [237] [Shapes from Examples: Foundations of Shape Learning in Recursive SHACL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27934)
*Bente Gortworst,Cem Okulmus,Magdalena Ortiz,Anni-Yasmin Turhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究SHACL形状学习中基于拟合的方法：给定正负示例节点集合，计算一个核心SHACL片段（对应描述逻辑ELI）中的形状表达式C，使其验证所有正例且不验证任何负例。针对递归形状目录的良基、稳定和支持三种语义，研究了拟合存在性和最具体拟合计算问题，建立了紧指数时间上界，并在相关特殊情形下获得多项式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHACL形状用于数据图验证，而自动形状学习对知识图谱应用至关重要。现有的拟合方法虽然在实践中被广泛使用，但其理论性质（计算复杂度、最坏情况上界）尚不明确，亟需系统性分析。

Method: 将拟合问题形式化：给定正例集合P和负例集合N，计算核心SHACL片段（对应ELI描述逻辑）中的形状表达式C，使C验证所有P中节点且不验证任何N中节点。考虑了递归形状目录的良基、稳定和支持三种语义，分别分析拟合存在性判定和最具体拟合计算两个问题。

Result: 对拟合存在性和最具体拟合计算两个问题，建立了紧的指数时间上界；在相关特殊情形下，获得了多项式时间复杂度的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为SHACL形状学习的拟合方法提供了严格的计算复杂度分析，明确了在不同语义和约束条件下的复杂度边界，为实际系统的设计与优化提供了理论指导。

Abstract: SHACL shapes enable data graph validation, making automatic shape learning essential for knowledge graph applications. We investigate the well-known fitting approach to this task: given sets P and N of positive and negative example nodes from an input graph, compute a shape expression C, possibly using shape names defined in a recursive shape catalogue, that validates at every node in P and none in N. We focus on the case where C is written in a core fragment of SHACL corresponding to the Description Logic ELI. For the catalogue, we consider the well-founded, stable, and supported semantics.
