To fill the gap, in this paper, we propose a format-aware metamorphic testing framework with three metamorphic relations to comprehensively evaluate the format robustness of end-to-end LLM document workflows. Based on this framework, we conduct a large-scale empirical study spanning four representative LLM workflows, four real-world tasks, and four document formats, comprising a total of 48,000 workflow executions. Our findings reveal that format variation poses a systematic and serious threat. Merely switching formats can cause accuracy to drop by up to 53.63% and trigger decision drifts in over 41% of instances. We further design lightweight mitigation strategies from the users' perspective that recover up to 44.21% of format-induced decision drift without model retraining. Our study demonstrates that document format is not a neutral wrapper but a critical factor affecting the reliability of LLM software systems, calling for corresponding testing and safeguards in the deployment in real-world high-stakes scenarios.

</details>


### [305] [Can Large Language Models Resolve Real Java Merge Conflicts? An Evaluation with a Calibrated LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27674)
*Bowen Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用大语言模型（LLM）解决Java合并冲突，并构建了一套两指标评估体系来可靠地衡量LLM解决方案的质量。作者构建了基于"生成-验证-重试"的LLM求解器，仅使用推理时信号（冲突标记、Java解析器、重复声明检查）而不参考开发者的答案；同时设计了LLM-as-judge（G-Eval指标，针对ConflictBench人工标签校准）和确定性结构有效性检查组成的两指标评估套件。在292个带人工标签的案例上，该评判器达到100%精确率（零误接受）和64.6%召回率。LLM求解器在约55%的真实冲突上与开发者解决一致（保守下限），在覆盖公平比较中LLM（55-59%）比最强传统工具AutoMerge（36.7%）高出18-22个百分点，优势几乎完全来自覆盖率的提升而非原始准确性，因为传统工具在20-90%的冲突上选择弃权而LLM从不弃权。此外，LLM评判器接受了5个未通过确定性结构检查的解决方案中的4个，表明结构正确性不能委托给LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合并冲突是协作软件开发中的高频成本。传统结构化/半结构化合并工具在使用启发式方法不适用时常常弃权，留下未解决的冲突。LLM虽然几乎能为任何冲突生成候选解决方案，但大规模评估这些解决方案是否真正优质十分困难，因为对每个模型输出获取人工可接受性判断无法扩展。本文希望同时解决这两个问题：构建不依赖开发者答案的LLM求解器，以及建立可扩展且可信的评估体系。

Method: 1) 构建LLM合并冲突求解器：采用"生成-验证-重试"代理框架，仅依赖推理时信号（冲突标记、Java解析器、重复声明检查），全程不接触开发者的实际答案。2) 构建两指标评估套件：(a) 开发者匹配LLM-as-judge（G-Eval指标），在使用前针对ConflictBench人工标签进行校准；(b) 确定性结构有效性检查，不使用LLM。3) 在ConflictBench的真实Java合并冲突上进行比较实验，包括与传统工具AutoMerge的覆盖公平对比，以及在292个人工标注案例上进行元评估。

Result: 元评估显示LLM评判器在292个人工标注案例上达到100%精确率（零误接受）和64.6%召回率，这意味着每次接受都可信。在此严格评判下，LLM求解器在约55%的真实冲突上与开发者解决方案一致（保守下限）。在覆盖公平比较中，LLM（55-59%）比最强的传统工具AutoMerge（36.7%）高出18-22个百分点，优势几乎完全来自覆盖率（传统工具在20-90%的冲突上弃权，而LLM强制求解下从不弃权）而非原始准确性。关键发现：LLM评判器接受了5个未通过确定性结构检查的解决方案中的4个，证明结构正确性检查不能依赖LLM。

Conclusion: LLM求解器在合并冲突解决上的核心优势在于覆盖率的突破——传统工具频繁弃权而LLM永不放弃，即使原始准确性相当，总体成功率仍然显著更高。评估方面，经过校准的LLM-as-judge可以实现零误接受的可信评估，但结构正确性必须依赖确定性检查而非LLM判断，两种指标应互补使用。该工作为大规模可信评估LLM驱动的代码补丁提供了方法论参考。

Abstract: Merge conflicts are a recurring cost of collaborative software development, and the traditional structured and semi-structured merge tools that address them frequently abstain: when their heuristics do not apply, they leave the conflict unresolved. Large language models (LLMs) can instead produce a candidate resolution for almost any conflict, but measuring whether those resolutions are actually good at scale is hard, because obtaining human desirability judgments for every model output does not scale. We study both problems together on real Java merge conflicts from ConflictBench. We first build an LLM solver as a generate-validate-retry agent that uses only inference-time signals (conflict markers, a Java parser, and duplicate-declaration checks) and never sees the developer's answer. We then evaluate its resolutions with a two-metric suite: (1) a developer-match LLM-as-judge implemented as a G-Eval metric and, crucially, calibrated against ConflictBench's human labels before use, and (2) a deterministic structural-validity check that uses no LLM. On a meta-evaluation of 292 human-labeled cases, the judge reaches 100% precision (zero false accepts) at 64.6% recall, so every acceptance is trustworthy and every downstream rate is a conservative lower bound. Under this validated judge, LLM solvers match the developer's own resolution on about 55% of true conflicts (conservative floor), and under a coverage-fair comparison the LLMs (55-59%) beat the strongest traditional tool (AutoMerge, 36.7%) by roughly 18-22 points; the edge comes almost entirely from coverage, not raw accuracy, since the tools abstain on 20-90% of conflicts while the LLM under forced resolution abstains on none. Finally, the LLM judge accepted 4 of the 5 resolutions that fail the deterministic structural check, evidence that structural correctness must not be delegated to an LLM.

</details>


### [306] [A comparative analysis of automated techniques for security bug report identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27893)
*Muhammad Laiq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对多种自动化技术（传统机器学习、大语言模型、预训练模型和少样本学习框架）在安全相关缺陷报告识别任务上进行了系统性比较分析，发现SetFit表现最佳（F1=0.80），RoBERTa紧随其后，而GPT-5.2在零样本和少样本设置下均表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全相关缺陷报告的及时识别对减少软件系统漏洞窗口至关重要，但人工筛查耗时且易出错。现有研究碎片化，各技术在不同实验设置下评估，难以直接比较，导致研究人员和实践者缺乏选择合适技术的明确指导。

Method: 使用基准数据集，对六种技术（Logistic Regression、SVM、随机森林、GPT-5.2、BERT-base、RoBERTa、SetFit）进行统一框架下的系统比较评估，并开展跨项目实验以检验迁移学习的效果。

Result: SetFit取得最佳综合性能（F1=0.80），在四个数据集中三个上表现最优；RoBERTa在部分项目中接近SetFit；传统ML方法（尤其Logistic Regression）在特定场景下仍是强基线；GPT-5.2在零样本和少样本设置下表现均差。跨项目实验发现迁移学习能提升数据有限项目的性能，但可能损害具有强项目特定特征项目的效果。

Conclusion: 少样本学习框架SetFit是当前安全缺陷报告识别任务中最有效的技术选择，传统机器学习在特定条件下仍具竞争力，而GPT-5.2尚不适合该任务。迁移学习的适用性需视项目数据量和项目特定特征而定。

Abstract: Timely identification of security-related bug reports is essential to minimize the window of vulnerabilities in software systems. Manually screening incoming bug reports to identify security-related issues is time-consuming, error-prone, and non-scalable for large-scale software systems. Thus, a variety of automatic techniques, including traditional machine learning (ML) techniques and large language models, have been proposed to facilitate this task. However, the literature remains fragmented. Most studies introduce or optimize a particular technique and evaluate it against a limited set of baselines, often under different experimental setups. As a result, it is difficult to compare their results and draw reliable conclusions about the effectiveness of existing approaches, leaving researchers and practitioners without clear guidance on which techniques are most suitable for the task.
  To address this gap, we conducted a comparative analysis of several promising automated techniques to identify security-related bug reports using benchmark datasets. We evaluated Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machines, Random Forest, OpenAI's GPT-5.2, BERT-base, RoBERTa, and SetFit (a state-of-the-art few-shot learning framework).
  Our results indicate that SetFit achieves the best overall performance, achieving an F1-score of 0.80 and outperforming other techniques on three of the four datasets. RoBERTa performs competitively and approaches SetFit in some projects, while traditional ML techniques, particularly Logistic Regression, remain a strong baseline in certain contexts. In contrast, GPT-5.2 performs poorly in both zero-shot and few-shot settings. In addition, cross-project experiments demonstrate that transfer learning can improve performance for projects with limited data, but may degrade results for projects with strong project-specific characteristics.

</details>


### [307] [The Case for Vibe Modeling: A Missing Step in AI-Based Trustworthy Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27923)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Michael Mittermaier,Judith Michael*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"氛围建模"（vibe modeling）作为自然语言交互与代码生成之间的轻量级中间抽象层，以解决LLM生成软件时在理解、验证、可追溯性和信任方面的挑战，并通过学生调查研究其感知效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助开发过度关注直接代码生成，缺乏能保留人类意图并支持系统行为推理的中间表示，导致理解、验证、可追溯性和信任等方面存在挑战。

Method: 提出"氛围建模"作为自然语言与代码生成之间的轻量级中间抽象，并通过学生调查问卷研究多个AI辅助开发场景中用户对LLM输出理解、验证工作量、信任度及氛围建模感知有用性的看法。

Result: 调查结果为未来在基于氛围建模的可信可解释AI软件工程研究提供参考依据。

Conclusion: 氛围建模有望成为提升AI辅助软件开发中可理解性、可验证性和信任度的有效中间表示方案，为后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate software artifacts from natural language prompts. While this enables rapid prototyping and lowers the barrier to software creation, it also introduces challenges related to understanding, validation, traceability, and trust. In this paper, we argue that current AI-based development practices focus too heavily on the direct generation of code and insufficiently on intermediate representations that preserve human intent and support reasoning about system behavior. We argue for vibe modeling as a lightweight intermediate abstraction between natural language interaction and code generation. To explore its potential, we present a student survey study that examines perceptions of LLM output understanding, validation effort, trust and the perceived usefulness of vibe modeling across several AI-assisted development scenarios. Our results are intended to inform future studies for trustworthy and explainable AI-based software engineering via vibe modeling.

</details>


### [308] [Specification-Guided Synthesis of Deadlock-Free Communication Protocol Refinements with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27964)
*Yang Li,Ping Hou,Nobuko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Syntropy框架，结合多参与者会话类型（MPST）规范与大型语言模型（LLM），在协议细化生成过程中直接嵌入细化约束，确保生成变体满足行为正确性（如无死锁），实现了95.6%-99.5%的有效性和高语法正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式软件系统中通信协议的行为正确性至关重要，细微不一致可能导致死锁。LLM虽在代码生成方面表现出色，但缺乏可靠产生正确行为输出的机制；形式化规范方法如MPST虽能提供无死锁等严格保证，但对自动构造协议细化的支持有限。

Method: 提出Syntropy框架，将MPST规范与LLM结合以合成协议细化。其核心思想是将细化约束直接纳入生成过程，确保生成的协议变体满足正确性保障。

Result: 综合评估表明Syntropy在多种LLM上实现了95.6%-99.5%的有效性，同时保持高语法正确性，并能生成多样化、非平凡的协议细化。

Conclusion: Syntropy成功地融合了形式化方法（MPST）的严格保证与LLM的生成能力，为协议细化的自动合成提供了可靠框架，在不牺牲语法正确性的前提下实现了高有效性。

Abstract: Ensuring behavioural correctness in communication protocols is a central challenge in distributed software systems, as subtle inconsistencies can lead to deadlocks. In such settings, protocol refinement - the safe substitution of a protocol that preserves correctness and compatibility with other components - is essential. Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in code generation and program synthesis, yet lack mechanisms to reliably produce outputs with correct behaviour. Formal specification approaches, such as multiparty session types (MPST), offer rigorous guarantees, including deadlock freedom, but provide limited support for automatically constructing protocol refinements. In this paper, we present Syntropy, a framework for synthesising protocol refinements guided by MPST specifications and LLMs. It incorporates refinement constraints directly into the generation process, ensuring the generated variants satisfy these guarantees. Our comprehensive evaluation indicates that Syntropy achieves 95.6%-99.5% validity while maintaining high syntactic correctness, and produces diverse, non-trivial refinements across multiple LLMs.

</details>


### [309] [Advancing Awkward Arrays for High-Performance CPU and GPU Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28145)
*Ianna Osborne,Manasvi Goyal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Awkward Array库的GPU后端最新进展，利用NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries（CCCL）实现CUDA加速，针对嵌套变长数据（不规则数组）设计了分段归约算法和优化内存管理，在保持原有Python编程模型的同时显著提升了GPU吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理领域HL-LHC分析越来越依赖加速硬件，而嵌套、变长数据结构在GPU上难以高效执行（需要间接索引、分段操作和不规则内存访问），亟需在不改变Python编程模型的前提下加速这些不规则工作负载。

Method: 基于NVIDIA CCCL库构建CUDA实现，包括优化内存管理策略、针对不规则数组的分段归约算法，并设计了自动化验证框架以及CPU、CuPy、CUDA实现之间的基准对比。

Result: 这些GPU后端开发在保持既有Python编程模型的同时，显著提升了不规则工作负载的GPU吞吐性能，并通过自动化验证框架保证了实现的正确性。

Conclusion: Awkward Array的GPU后端为高能物理中广泛使用的嵌套变长数据提供了高效的GPU加速方案，证明了不规则数据结构的加速是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Awkward Array is a Python library for representing and processing nested, variable-length data that is widely used in high-energy physics. As HL-LHC analyses increasingly rely on accelerator hardware, efficient execution of irregular workloads has become essential. While dense numerical arrays map naturally to GPUs, nested and variable-length data structures remain significantly more difficult to accelerate because they require indirect indexing, segmented operations, and irregular memory access patterns. We present recent developments in the Awkward Array GPU backend, including CUDA implementations built on NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries (CCCL), optimized memory management, and segmented reduction algorithms for ragged arrays. These developments preserve the existing Python programming model while substantially improving GPU throughput on irregular workloads. We describe the backend architecture, automated validation framework, and benchmark results comparing CPU, CuPy, and CUDA implementations.

</details>


### [310] [Integrating AI into Requirements Quality Learning in Software Engineering Education: A TPACK-Guided Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28176)
*Hansika Ekanayake Mudiyanselage,Rohan Jai Dharmaraj,Malik Abdul Sami,Zheying Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了将TPACK框架指导下的多智能体AI工具整合到硕士层次的需求工程（RE）作业中，通过混合方法（N=100，分析72份提交）考察结构化作业设计如何影响学生的AI使用方式、对用户故事质量标准（如价值表达、可测试性、可协商性）的理解，以及他们对AI优势与局限的感知。结果显示学生选择性使用AI作为分析与评估支持而非自动化工具，有条件信任、主动改进和标准意识提升等积极效果，但也存在可用性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在软件工程实践中的快速普及，要求软件工程教育（尤其是需求工程这类概念密集型课程）采用教学法上有依据的AI整合方式，但当前缺乏TPACK等框架指导下的系统研究。

Method: 采用混合方法设计，以TPACK框架为指导，将多智能体AI工具整合到硕士级需求工程课程的用户故事质量分析作业中；收集100名学生的数据，对其中72份提交进行分析。

Result: 学生主要将AI用作分析与评估支持而非自动化工具；价值表达和可测试性等结构性需求质量维度提升最明显，而可协商性维度效果好坏参半；学生表现出条件信任、主动改进和标准意识提升，但也遇到适度的可用性挑战。

Conclusion: TPACK引导的脚手架设计能够将AI的能供性与教学目标和需求工程内容对齐，为需求工程教育中负责任的AI整合提供了设计指导。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) in software engineering (SE) practice creates a need for pedagogically grounded approaches to AI integration in SE education, especially in conceptually intensive subjects such as requirements engineering (RE). This study examines a TPACK-guided integration of a multi-agent AI tool into a master-level RE assignment on requirements quality analysis. Using a mixed-methods design (N=100; 72 submissions analysed), we examine how structured assignment design shaped students' AI use, affected their understanding of user story quality criteria, and influenced their perceptions of AI's benefits and limitations. Results show that students used the AI tool selectively, mainly as support for analysis and evaluation rather than automation. Alignment improvements were most evident for structurally concrete requirements quality dimensions, such as value articulation and testability, while negotiability showed mixed effects. Students reported conditional trust, active refinement, and increased awareness of quality criteria, alongside moderate usability challenges. The findings show that TPACK-guided scaffolding can align AI affordances with pedagogical goals and RE content, offering design guidance for responsible AI integration in RE education.

</details>


### [311] [Agentic Method for Deterministic Validation of Legacy Code Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28271)
*Andras Ferenczi,Jordan Docherty,Mariya Bessonov,Matthew Findlay,Krishna Lingamneni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Locksmith Loop"的智能体测试合成方法，用于验证COBOL到Java程序迁移的正确性。该方法通过在COBOL源码和生成的Java目标两个运行时环境中进行迭代式智能体循环，结合Witness Search和奇偶保持变异来渗透程序分支，并在路由边界处识别"锁定的段落"以突破探索瓶颈。在三个案例研究中（包括两个开源程序和一个人内部生产级COBOL程序，代码量430至4,114行），该方法在输入搜索平台期之上持续提升覆盖率，在两个开源程序上达到接近完全覆盖，在内部生产级程序上达到91.90%分支覆盖率，且所有接受测试用例均通过确定性奇偶校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留COBOL程序迁移到Java需要大量测试以确保功能正确，但常因缺乏测试数据和难以验证所有边界情况而变得复杂。现有的测试方法在覆盖率上容易陷入平台期，缺乏系统性的突破手段。

Method: 提出"Locksmith Loop"智能体测试合成方法：首先准备两个运行时环境（COBOL源码和生成的Java目标均在商品硬件上使用mock离线执行），然后通过迭代式智能体循环执行Witness Search（在输入mock上搜索以渗透程序分支）和奇偶保持变异；当到达路由边界时，分析器识别"锁定段落"（阻止更深探索的条件），从而突破覆盖瓶颈。

Result: 在三个COBOL-Java案例研究中（两个开源程序和一个内部生产级程序，430至4,114行代码），Locksmith在输入搜索平台期之上持续提升覆盖率：两个开源程序达到接近完全覆盖，内部生产级COBOL程序达到91.90%分支覆盖率。生成的Java在所有接受的测试用例中均与COBOL参考在确定性奇偶校验下匹配。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种利用确定性oracle验证智能体编码输出的新颖方法，有效解决了COBOL到Java迁移中的测试数据缺乏和边界情况验证难题，显著提升了测试覆盖率。

Abstract: Migration of legacy COBOL programs to Java requires extensive testing to ensure correct functionality. This effort is often complicated by the lack of test data and the difficulty of validating all corner cases. In this paper we propose a novel agentic test-synthesis method, the "Locksmith Loop," which is initiated by preparing two runtime environments: the COBOL source and the generated Java target are each instrumented with mocks and executed off-mainframe on commodity hardware, then an iterative agentic loop performs Witness Search over input mocks to penetrate program branches, followed by parity-preserving mutations. When routing boundaries are reached, an analyzer identifies a Locked Paragraph: a condition preventing deeper exploration. Across three COBOL-Java case studies, spanning two open-source programs and one internal production-like COBOL program and ranging from 430 to 4,114 source lines, Locksmith consistently improved coverage beyond input-search plateaus, reaching nearly complete coverage on the two open-source programs and 91.90% branch coverage on the internal production-like COBOL program. The generated Java matched the COBOL reference under deterministic parity checks in all accepted test cases. Through these findings we demonstrate, to the best of our knowledge, a novel approach for validating agentic coding output using a deterministic oracle.

</details>


### [312] [From Textual Requirements to Microservice Architectures - A Comprehensive Evaluation of LLM-Based Design Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28307)
*Danyllo Albuquerque,José Renan,Guillermo Rodríguez,Guillermo Rodríguez,Emanuel Dantas,Ademar França,Mirko Perkusich,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探索OpenAI o3能否仅从自然语言需求生成微服务架构，通过零样本(few-shot)提示在Bookstore和PetClinic两个系统上进行混合方法评估，结果表明少样本提示显著优于零样本，服务识别F1达到0.97，通信恢复F1达到0.82，专家评估也验证了少样本输出的更高模块化和合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构已成为现代化单体系统的主流方式，但服务识别仍然困难且大多依赖人工。现有分解方法主要基于代码，在仅有文本需求的设计早期阶段适用性有限。尽管LLM取得了进展，但缺乏其从自然语言需求中综合生成完整微服务架构（包括服务定义和交互）的经验证据。

Method: 采用混合方法研究，使用OpenAI o3在零样本(ZS)和少样本(FS)提示条件下，对Bookstore和PetClinic两个系统各执行一次。架构通过两种方式评估：(1) 与参考架构比较，使用精确率、召回率和F1分数评估服务识别和通信恢复；(2) 盲法专家评估正确性、完整性、模块性和合理性，并进行开放式反馈综合。

Result: OpenAI o3在少样本提示下服务识别一致性更高(ZS F1=0.79 vs FS F1=0.97)。通信恢复更具挑战性：零样本生成密集架构但召回率高精确率低(F1=0.61)，少样本将F1提升至0.82并减少不支持的依赖。专家评估佐证这些结果，少样本架构被认为更模块化、连贯和合理。

Conclusion: OpenAI o3在示例提示引导下展现出从需求驱动综合生成微服务架构的潜力。但结果来自两个小型系统，具有模型和上下文特异性，不能作为与模型无关的普适性证明。

Abstract: Microservice architectures have become dominant for modernizing monolithic systems, yet identifying appropriate services remains challenging and largely manual. Existing decomposition approaches are predominantly code-centric, limiting applicability in early design stages where only textual requirements are available. Despite advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), limited empirical evidence exists on their ability to synthesize complete microservice architectures from natural-language requirements, including service definitions and inter-service interactions. This study investigates whether an LLM can bridge requirements engineering and architectural design, generating architectures solely from textual requirements and evaluating structural agreement and perceived quality of results. We conduct a mixed-method study using OpenAI o3 under zero-shot (ZS) and few-shot (FS) prompting across two systems (Bookstore, PetClinic), one execution per system/condition. Architectures are evaluated through (i) comparison with reference architectures using precision, recall, and F1-score for service identification and communication recovery, and (ii) a blinded expert assessment of correctness, completeness, modularity, and plausibility, plus open feedback synthesis. OpenAI o3 identifies services with higher agreement under FS prompting (F1 = 0.79 for ZS versus = 0.97 for FS). Communication recovery is more challenging: ZS produces dense architectures with high recall but low precision (F1 = 0.61), while FS improves agreement, reaching F1 = 0.82 and reducing unsupported dependencies. Expert evaluation corroborates these results, with FS architectures perceived as more modular, coherent, and plausible than ZS outputs. OpenAI o3 shows potential for requirements-driven synthesis when guided by exemplar prompting. Results are model- and context-specific from two small systems, not model-independent proof.

</details>


### [313] [Structural Validation of LLM-Generated Microservice Decompositions Using Source-Code Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28331)
*Daniel Silva,Renan Alves,Emanuel Dantas Filho,Ademar Sousa Neto,Mirko Perkusich,Danyllo Wagner Albuquerque,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了OpenAI o3在PetClinic和Bookstore两个系统上生成的微服务分解方案是否保留了源代码中的结构依赖关系，提出了一种基于静态依赖分析的自动化验证管道，并比较了零样本和少样本提示策略。经过归一化处理后，两种提示策略产生的结构符合性相当（TPD分别为68.0%和83.3%），表明评估时应显式控制映射覆盖率，否则可能引入方法学偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM能从文本需求生成语义上合理的微服务分解方案，但这些方案是否保留了源代码中已实现的结构依赖关系尚不明确，需要系统性的评估方法来验证其结构忠实度。

Method: 提出基于静态依赖分析的自动化验证管道，使用依赖保持（TPD）和依赖违反（TVD）指标，对比零样本与少样本提示策略，并进行鲁棒性分析以控制类到服务映射覆盖率的差异。

Result: 归一化后两种提示策略产生等价的结构符合性，PetClinic的TPD为68.0%，Bookstore为83.3%。提示策略之间的表观差异主要反映方法学偏差而非真实的架构质量差异。

Conclusion: 对LLM生成的微服务分解进行结构评估时，必须显式控制映射覆盖率，否则结果可能受方法学偏差影响。本研究为评估LLM在软件现代化任务中的可靠性提供了方法学基础。

Abstract: Decomposing monolithic systems into microservices is a key activity in software modernization. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate semantically plausible decompositions from textual requirements, it remains unclear whether these proposals preserve the structural dependencies implemented in the source code. This paper evaluates the structural adherence of microservice decompositions generated by OpenAI o3 for the PetClinic and Bookstore systems. We propose an automated validation pipeline based on static dependency analysis and compare zero-shot and few-shot prompting using dependency preservation (TPD) and dependency violation (TVD) metrics. A robustness analysis was conducted to control for differences in class-to-service mapping coverage. After normalization, both prompting strategies produced equivalent structural adherence, achieving TPD values of 68.0% (PetClinic) and 83.3% (Bookstore). The findings demonstrate that structural evaluations of LLM-generated decompositions should explicitly control for mapping coverage, as apparent differences between prompting strategies may otherwise reflect methodological bias rather than genuine architectural quality.

</details>


### [314] [Demystifying Solana Bots: From GitHub Blueprints to On-Chain Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28424)
*Xiaoye Zheng,Yujing Chen,Minghao Wu,David Lo,Difan Xie,Daoyuan Wu,Xiaohu Yang,Zhiyuan Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Solana区块链上的交易机器人进行了大规模实证研究，从GitHub上的586个机器人代码仓库和链上的200个机器人地址（超过4400万笔交易）两个视角出发，构建了基于实现细节的Solana机器人分类体系（5大领域、15个类别），识别出机器人普遍遵循的五阶段操作流水线，并发现不同交易平台和资产上机器人链上交易行为的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana以其高吞吐量和低交易费用吸引了大量机器人参与链上交易，2026年1月去中心化交易所的机器人相关日交易量已超过2.5亿美元，但现有研究主要关注系统性能、智能合约安全或特定链上现象，缺乏对Solana机器人在实际实现层面及其链上执行指纹的系统性理解。

Method: 采用双视角的大规模实证研究方法：(i) 从GitHub收集586个机器人代码仓库进行分析；(ii) 追踪Solana上200个机器人地址，分析其超过4400万笔链上交易。从实现和链上行为两个层面交叉验证，推导出可落地（implementation-grounded）的机器人分类体系。

Result: 研究得出三大核心成果：构建了包含15个类别、5大领域（如交易操作、MEV、链上分析）的Solana机器人分类体系；识别出机器人实现中普遍共享的五阶段操作流水线；发现了Solana机器人在不同交易平台和资产上链上交易行为的系统性变化模式。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，论文指出了未来研究方向，并为在Solana区块链上构建和运营机器人提供了实用建议，填补了Solana机器人系统性理解的空白。

Abstract: Solana is an emerging blockchain platform designed for high throughput and low transaction fees, making it inexpensive to submit transactions at scale and, consequently, increasing exposure to bot spamming and related financial exploitation. Solana bots are typically off-chain software systems that operate in a competitive on-chain execution environment by constructing and submitting transactions, and the bot-related transactions on the decentralized exchanges exceed 250 million dollars in daily trading volume in January 2026. Prior studies on Solana have examined system performance, smart-contract security, and specific on-chain phenomena. However, we still lack a systematic understanding of what Solana bots implement in practice and how these implementations manifest as observable on-chain execution fingerprints. To address this gap, we performed a large-scale empirical study of Solana bots from two complementary views: (i) 586 bot repositories collected from GitHub, and (ii) 200 bot addresses on Solana, with over 44 million on-chain transactions. Our study derives an implementation-grounded taxonomy of Solana bots comprising 15 categories grouped into five domains (e.g., Trading Operations, MEV, and On-chain Analytics), identifies a largely shared five-stage operational pipeline manifested in bot implementations, and uncovers systematic variation in on-chain trading behaviors of Solana bots across diverse trading platforms and assets. Based on our findings, we highlight future research directions, and provide recommendations for building and operating bots on the Solana blockchain.

</details>


### [315] [Emerging Challenges in Threat Modeling for GenAI-Augmented Systems: A View from the Trenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28431)
*Nicolás E. Díaz Ferreyra,Manish Mahesh Kumar,Nohemí Villarreal,Pankaj Pantel,Immo Brueggemann,Riccardo Scandariato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在中小企业环境中对GenAI感知的威胁建模方法进行了探索性评估，通过快速文献综述选出三种方法并应用于一个GenAI增强系统的工业案例研究，发现各方法在识别威胁上存在差异，且对某些GenAI特定风险类别（如软件供应链和人为中心安全问题）支持有限，同时报告了从业者对方法可用性和集成难度的看法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI（GenAI）在软件系统中的普及，传统威胁建模方法（如STRIDE）已不足以评估GenAI特有的安全风险，但缺乏在中小企业环境中对这些新方法的实证评估。

Method: 首先进行快速文献综述以筛选相关威胁建模技术，然后从候选方法中选出三种方法，系统性地将其应用于一个包含GenAI增强系统的工业案例研究，最后收集从业者对这些方法可用性、集成性、工作量及采用挑战的反馈。

Result: 不同威胁建模方法识别的威胁存在显著差异；现有方法对GenAI特定风险类别（特别是软件供应链风险和人机安全问题）支持不足；从业者对方法在中小企业开发流程中的可用性和集成难度给出了具体反馈。

Conclusion: 当前GenAI感知的威胁建模方法在中小企业环境中仍存在能力缺口，需要进一步改进对特定GenAI风险类别的覆盖，并优化方法在中小企业工作流中的可用性和易集成性。

Abstract: Threat modeling remains a central task in secure software engineering, as it enables the identification of security issues from system architectures. As Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) becomes increasingly pervasive across software systems, traditional threat modeling methods (e.g., STRIDE) are insufficient to assess emerging GenAI-specific risks. In this work, we present the first results from an exploratory assessment of GenAI-aware threat modeling methods in a Small and Medium Enterprise (SME) setting. For this, we conducted a rapid literature review to select relevant techniques and systematically applied three shortlisted methods to an industrial case study involving a GenAI-augmented system. The results highlight differences in the threats identified by each technique and reveal limited support for certain GenAI-specific risk categories, particularly those related to software supply chains and human-centered security issues. We further report practitioners' perceptions of the usability and integration of these methods in SME development workflows, including their perceived effort and adoption challenges.

</details>


### [316] [CoGate: Confidence-Gated Co-Decoding for Secure Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28529)
*Minghao Hu,Lannan Luo,Allen Roush,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoGate，一种基于置信度门控的协同解码方法，通过控制安全专家模型在代码生成过程中的影响力来提升生成代码的安全性，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型用于代码生成时可能产生不安全代码，现有的协同解码方法（co-decoding）在结合专家模型时未考虑专家模型的置信度，当专家模型遇到未见过的模式或分布外（OOD）上下文时，其引导可能具有误导性。

Method: 提出CoGate，一种置信度门控的协同解码方法，根据安全专家模型的置信度来控制其在协同解码过程中的影响力。

Result: 在多个LLM后端（CodeGen、DeepSeek-Coder、Qwen-Coder、StarCoder）和多个代码生成基准（HumanEval、security suite、CWEval）上的评估表明，CoGate优于现有协同解码方法（如CoSec+），在CWEval上Func-Sec@10指标最多提升12.6%。

Conclusion: CoGate通过置信度门控机制有效解决了专家模型在低置信度场景下可能产生误导性引导的问题，显著提升了生成代码的安全性。

Abstract: Large language models are widely used for code generation, but they can also produce insecure programs due to patterns learned from their pretraining data. Decoding-time steering has become an important solution to this problem: a small expert model is combined with the target model at each step to generate more secure code, which is referred to as co-decoding. However, the acceptance rule for existing co-decoding approaches does not consider the expert model's confidence. When the security expert is unconfident due to unseen patterns or out-of-distribution (OOD) contexts, its guidance can therefore be misleading. To address the challenge, we propose CoGate, a confidence-gated co-decoding approach that controls the expert's influence on the co-decoding process based on its confidence. We implement our approach and evaluate it across multiple LLM backends (CodeGen, DeepSeek-Coder, Qwen-Coder, StarCoder) on several code generation benchmarks (HumanEval, security suite, and CWEval). Our approach outperforms existing co-decoding methods (CoSec+) across multiple benchmarks, achieving up to a 12.6% gain of Func-Sec@10 on CWEval.

</details>


### [317] [PAIChecker: Uncovering and Checking PR-Issue Misalignment in SWE-Bench-Like Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28587)
*Manyi Wang,Junjielong Xu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了SWE-bench类基准测试中PR-Issue配对错位问题，发现13.6%的实例存在五种模式、十一类细粒度场景的错位，并提出多智能体系统PAIChecker进行检测，在SWE-Gym和SWE-bench Multilingual上分别达到92.12%和91.67%的二元准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-bench类基准广泛用于评估LLM的问题解决能力，但其构建过程中PR与Issue的配对常因大型仓库的复杂性而发生错位，影响基准的可靠性和评估有效性，亟需系统性检测与修复方案。

Method: 提出PAIChecker多智能体系统，采用三阶段设计：特定模式识别、跨智能体标签综合、代码级验证，实现对PR-Issue错位问题的准确、可泛化且可逐步验证的检测。

Result: 在SWE-Gym和SWE-bench Multilingual上，PAIChecker在所有四个LLM骨干网络上均取得最佳性能，二元准确率分别达到92.12%和91.67%。

Conclusion: PAIChecker能够有效检测SWE-bench类基准中的PR-Issue配对错位问题，为未来可靠、可扩展的基准构建提供了实用的自动化检测工具。

Abstract: SWE-bench-like benchmarks are widely used for evaluating LLM's issue resolution capability. They typically follow a common construction pipeline: each PR (Pull Request) is paired with its linked issue by extracting issue references from the PR description; the issue description is used as the problem statement, and the PR patch serves as the test oracle. However, due to the inherent complexity of developing and maintaining large repositories, such PR-Issue pairings are often misaligned in practice. In this work, we systematically study SWE-bench Verified instances, finding that 13.6% exhibit misalignment across five patterns in eleven fine-grained scenarios. To enable reliable and scalable construction of those benchmarks in the future, we propose PAIChecker, a multi-agent system for checking PR-Issue misalignment in SWE-bench-like benchmarks. Specifically, PAIChecker adopts a three-phase design that combines specific pattern identification, cross-agent label synthesis, and code-level validation, thereby enabling more accurate, generalizable, and progressively verified detection. Experiments on SWE-Gym and SWE-bench Multilingual show that PAIchecker achieves the best performance across all four LLM backbones, reaching up to 92.12% and 91.67% binary accuracy, respectively.

</details>


### [318] [Change2Task: From Repository Changes to Executable Coding Agent Tasks and Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28591)
*Haomin Qi,Xingliang Wang,Xuanqi Gao,Baihui Sang,Xin Zhang,Minghua Ma,Pengfei Gao,Yu Kang,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Change2Task系统，基于仓库历史将合并的Pull Request转换为现代健康代码库上的可验证编码任务，涵盖Bug修复、功能添加、测试生成、API迁移和安全修复五类任务，在1130个候选源变更中实现79.6%的验证任务构建成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展编码智能体的可执行数据供给需要持续提供训练、基准测试和持续评估所需的数据，但每个任务都需要将真实软件状态与规格说明、开发工具和可靠验证相结合，现有数据构建方法效率不足。

Method: Change2Task利用仓库历史将合并的Pull Request转换为现代代码健康版本上的可验证任务。通过Patch Reversal、Code Mapping或Agent Reconstruction三种方式重建任务状态，并验证从健康基线到任务状态再到恢复状态的完整生命周期。

Result: 在1130个符合条件的源变更上实现79.6%的验证任务构建成功率；相比基于Pull Request的构建基线多恢复29.2%的验证任务；历史与重建案例在智能体评估中达成98.0%的结果一致性；复用现代基线使全流程测量开销降低10.8%。

Conclusion: Change2Task通过从维护良好的环境中基于开发者证据派生多种任务，为编码智能体的训练和评估提供了可执行数据，同时减少了重复环境搭建、存储和任务构建的工作量。

Abstract: Scaling coding agents requires a continuing supply of executable data for training, benchmarking, and continuous evaluation. Each task must couple a realistic software state with a specification, development tools, and reliable verification. To expand this supply, we present Change2Task, a system grounded in repository history that converts merged pull requests into verified tasks on healthy modern revisions of the same repository. It aligns historical evidence with evolved code, reconstructs task states through Patch Reversal, Code Mapping, or Agent Reconstruction, and validates the lifecycle from a healthy base to a task state and a restored state. By deriving multiple tasks grounded in developer evidence from maintained environments, Change2Task provides executable data for coding agent training and evaluation while reducing repeated environment setup, storage, and task construction effort. We evaluate the system through five common and widely adopted coding agent task families: Bug Fix, Feature Addition, Test Generation, Application Programming Interface Migration, and Security Repair. Starting from 1,130 source changes eligible for construction, Change2Task achieves 79.6% verified task construction success across these task families. On a matched candidate set, it recovers 29.2% more verified tasks than a construction baseline based on pull requests. Historical and reconstructed cases achieve up to 98.0% matched outcome agreement under agent evaluation, while reuse of modern bases reduces measured expenditure across the complete pipeline by 10.8%.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [319] [FAVA: Formal Authorization for Verified Agents with Evidence-Backed Permission Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27267)
*Yifan Zhang,Xinkui Zhao,Sai Liu,Hengxuan Lou,Guanjie Cheng,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FAVA是一个针对LLM智能体的权限携带式授权框架，通过LLM引导的权限中间表示（IR）将自然语言任务转化为结构化约束，再经确定性降级生成证据支持的权限图，利用SMT求解器对安全策略进行数学验证，在效果性动作执行前做出授权或拦截决策。实验在OpenAgentSafety、OctoBench和ActPlane场景中达到90.5%的决策合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在动态环境中自主交错执行语义推理与复杂系统操作，静态工具级权限本质上不够充分，安全授权高度依赖上下文、运行时状态和数据流，需要更精细的上下文感知授权机制。

Method: FAVA框架包含三个核心组件：(1) LLM引导的权限中间表示（Permission IR），将模糊的自然语言任务转化为结构化约束；(2) 确定性降级过程，将IR转换为明确追踪数据流、依赖关系和上下文的证据支持权限图；(3) SMT授权器，在效果性动作执行前对权限图进行数学验证，由运行时网关强制执行求解结果，对不合规操作提供精确反例。

Result: 在OpenAgentSafety、OctoBench和ActPlane三个评测场景中，FAVA在聚合数据集上达到90.5%的决策合规率（DCR），成功拦截了评估的轨迹条件场景中的动态违规序列。

Conclusion: 本文通过将LLM的语义理解能力与SMT的形式化验证能力相结合，为LLM智能体提供了严格可验证的上下文感知授权方案，弥补了传统静态权限机制在动态环境中的不足。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents autonomously interleave semantic reasoning with complex system operations. In these dynamic environments, static tool-level permissions are fundamentally insufficient; safe authorization is highly context-dependent and heavily reliant on evolving runtime states and data flows. We present FAVA (Formal Authorization for Verified Agents), a permission-carrying authorization framework for agent execution. FAVA utilizes an LLM-guided Permission Intermediate Representation (IR) to translate ambiguous natural-language tasks into structured constraints. A deterministic lowering pass then converts this IR into an evidence-backed permission graph that explicitly tracks data flows, dependencies, and contextual labels. To provide strict security guarantees, a Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) authorizer mathematically verifies the current graph against security policies before any effectful action executes. A runtime gateway then enforces the solver's result, either authorizing the execution or intercepting it with a precise counterexample. We evaluate FAVA across OpenAgentSafety, OctoBench, and ActPlane scenarios. Our evaluation demonstrates that FAVA achieves a 90.5% Decision Compliance Rate (DCR) over the aggregate dataset, successfully intercepting dynamic violating traces in the evaluated trace-conditioned scenarios.

</details>


### [320] [Open Security Benchmark: Towards Autonomous Enterprise Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27288)
*Gal Engelberg,Michael Arenzon,Leon Goldberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Open Security Benchmark (OSB)，一个用于端到端评估企业自主网络防御中智能体AI的基准框架。OSB通过冻结的企业安全状态快照，在文本到SQL和厂商原生API两种模态下评估安全态势调查能力，解决了企业环境数据缺口问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业正转向自主网络防御，但缺乏一个公开的、可查询的、跨厂商的企业环境来端到端评估这类智能体。现有方法无法回答"智能体是否值得信任"这一核心问题，存在环境数据缺口。

Method: OSB包含五个组件：数据层、任务与评估集层、多维评分层、最小可审计的测试框架、以及支持公共比较和私有租户评估的bring-your-own路径。它将环境冻结为不可变快照，支持文本到SQL和厂商原生API两种调查模态，并提供两个身份安全包和多种规模的合成组织环境数据集。

Result: 已用两个身份安全包和一族多规模合成组织环境数据集实例化该框架，并规划了向更多态势子域、调查模态和从评估到修复的防御阶段的扩展。

Conclusion: OSB通过冻结环境的封闭式真值锚定答案，为自主网络防御智能体的信任评估提供了可行的公共基准框架，填补了企业环境数据缺口。

Abstract: Enterprises are moving toward autonomous cyber defense: agentic AI that builds situational awareness of an organization's security state and reasons from it to assessments, decisions, and actions. This rests on a holistic view of the enterprise's security state, the continuous, cross-vendor picture of identities, cloud and infrastructure, data, applications, and their configurations that security posture management assembles. As agents take on this work, what matters is not whether an agent can produce an answer but whether it should be trusted to. The field cannot yet answer this question. Real enterprise environments are private, cross-vendor, and deeply correlated, and none is exposed publicly as a shared, queryable target for evaluating such agents end to end. We call this the environment data gap. We present Open Security Benchmark (OSB), a framework that benchmarks agentic AI on this work. OSB surfaces a curated enterprise environment - a frozen, holistic view of the security state - and evaluates posture investigation across two modalities: text-to-SQL over a relational snapshot and each vendor's native API over a served instance of the same environment. Freezing the environment pins the target state as an immutable snapshot and anchors answers to a closed-form ground truth. OSB is built from five components: a data layer, a task and evaluation-set layer, a multi-dimensional scoring layer, a minimal auditable harness, and a bring-your-own path that serves public comparison and private tenant evaluation from one substrate. We instantiate the framework with two identity-security packs and a family of synthetic-organization environment datasets spanning multiple scales, and chart its extension to further posture subdomains, investigation modalities, and defense stages from assessment toward remediation.

</details>


### [321] [RoguePrompt: Dual-Layer Encoding for Self-Reconstruction to Circumvent LLM Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27373)
*Benyamin Tafreshian,Prathamesh Dhake*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RoguePrompt，一种将禁用提示进行分区并应用双重嵌套编码（Vigenere后接ROT13）以及自然语言重建指令的越狱管道。在313个真实硬拒绝提示上测试，实现了93.93%的过滤绕过率、79.02%的重建率和70.18%的执行率，并提供了多阶段越狱失败位置的逐阶段证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱攻击评估通常将可见接受、隐藏请求成功恢复和后续执行合并为一个聚合的攻击成功结果，缺乏关于多阶段提示变换攻击在可观察的黑盒交互中失败位置的具体证据。

Method: RoguePrompt管道将禁用提示进行分区，应用两层嵌套编码（先Vigenere后ROT13），并配合自然语言重建指令。在黑盒威胁模型下开发评估，仅通过API或用户界面访问托管模型，在313个真实硬拒绝提示上进行测试，按调节绕过、指令重建和执行三个独立阶段衡量成功。

Result: RoguePrompt实现了93.93%的过滤绕过率、79.02%的指令重建率和70.18%的执行率，证明了分层提示编码的有效性，同时提供了逐阶段失败证据。

Conclusion: 该研究显示了分层提示编码的有效性，并提供了多阶段越狱在调节绕过、指令重建和执行阶段失败位置的逐阶段证据，有助于理解越狱攻击失败的具体环节。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming increasingly integrated into mainstream development platforms and daily technological workflows, typically behind moderation and safety controls. Despite these controls, preventing prompt-based policy evasion remains challenging, and adversaries continue to "jailbreak" LLMs by crafting prompts that circumvent implemented safety mechanisms. Prior work has established cipher-mediated interaction, code-embedded decryption, prompt decomposition and reconstruction, and layered custom encryption as viable attack primitives. However, reported evaluations generally collapse visible acceptance, successful recovery of the concealed request, and subsequent execution into an aggregate attack-success outcome. This leaves limited evidence about where multistage prompt-transformation attacks fail within an observable black-box interaction. This paper introduces RoguePrompt, a jailbreak pipeline that partitions a forbidden prompt and applies two nested encodings, Vigenere followed by ROT13, along with natural-language reconstruction instructions. RoguePrompt was developed and evaluated under a black-box threat model, with only API or user-interface access to the hosted models, and was tested on 313 real-world, hard-rejected prompts. Success was measured in terms of moderation bypass, instruction reconstruction, and execution when the relevant stage exceeded its automated criterion. RoguePrompt achieved average rates of 93.93% for filter bypass, 79.02% for reconstruction, and 70.18% for execution. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of layered prompt encoding while providing stage-level evidence of where multistage jailbreaks fail during moderation bypass, instruction reconstruction, and execution.

</details>


### [322] [Low-Latency Bootstrapping for CKKS using Roots of Unity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27401)
*Jean-Sebastien Coron,Robin Koestler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPRU（Sparse Roots of Unity）自举算法，通过将模q加法群直接嵌入复数单位根来替代传统CKKS自举中的模归约多项式近似，显著降低了乘法深度，在OpenFHE库中实现了最高5倍的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CKKS自举方法依赖同态评估近似模归约的多项式，导致乘法深度较大，需要较大的环维度，影响整体效率。

Method: SPRU自举算法将模q的加法群直接嵌入复数单位根中，利用CKKS方案原生支持复数运算的特性进行单位根上的同态评估，避免了多项式近似模归约的需要，从而降低乘法深度并允许使用更小的环维度。

Result: 在OpenFHE C++库中实现，对于小槽数（slots）的密文，SPRU自举相比传统方法实现了最高5倍的延迟降低。

Conclusion: SPRU自举通过创新性地利用单位根结构替代多项式近似，实现了CKKS自举效率的显著提升，尤其适用于小槽数场景。

Abstract: We introduce Sparse Roots of Unity (SPRU) bootstrapping, a new bootstrapping algorithm for the CKKS homomorphic encryption scheme for approximate arithmetic. The original CKKS bootstrapping method relies on homomorphically evaluating a polynomial that approximates modular reduction modulo q. In contrast, SPRU bootstrapping directly embeds the additive group modulo q into the complex roots of unity, which can be evaluated natively in the CKKS scheme. This approach significantly reduces the multiplicative depth required for bootstrapping, enabling the use of a smaller ring dimension and improving efficiency. In practice, using the OpenFHE C++ library, SPRU bootstrapping achieves up to a 5x reduction in latency when applied to ciphertexts with a small number of slots.

</details>


### [323] [Granite: A Modular Methodology for Foundational Verification of Hardware-Software Leakage Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27480)
*Stella Lau,Andres Erbsen,Adam Chlipala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Granite是一种用于RTL处理器模块化验证的方法论，同时验证功能正确性（对照ISA契约）和非泄漏性。通过泄漏感知的精化（基于确定性），证明流水线RISC设计的逐周期时序仅由ISA泄漏契约中指定的可观测对象决定，从而排除已知和未知时序侧信道的信息泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件处理器（特别是RTL级别的设计）既需要保证功能正确性，也需要保证安全性（即不泄漏机密信息），但现有方法往往难以同时模块化地验证这两者。时序侧信道（包括已知和未知的）是严重的安全威胁，需要一种形式化方法将指令集级别的泄漏契约与微架构级别的逐周期执行联系起来。

Method: Granite的核心技术是"泄漏感知精化"（leakage-aware refinement via determinism），通过一族周期级、确定性的规范机器，建立正确性和保密性的迹等价。机密无关的非确定性通过存在性参数化规范来处理，其中包含仅作用于公共数据的不可信确定性函数。子模块对照各自的泄漏感知规范进行证明，这些证明可组合成整体设计保证。证明还与认证静态分析（识别密码学常数时间代码）组合，推导出关于硬件-软件密码实现的逐周期保密性的Rocq定理。

Result: 对于保持可观测对象与机密无关的程序（即遵循密码学常数时间纪律），该结果排除了通过已知和未知时序侧信道的信息泄漏。证明消除了包括ISA契约本身在内的所有中间规范的可信计算基（TCB）。这是首次实现指令集级泄漏契约与微架构级逐周期执行（带线级观测）之间的模块化和基础性连接。

Conclusion: Granite是首个在指令集级泄漏契约与微架构具体逐周期执行（带线级观测）之间建立模块化和基础性连接的工作。它允许规范仅约束功能正确性和信息流依赖关系，而不需指定指令周期数、中断处理位置或子模块精确延迟，从而在广泛的安全实现空间中提供可组合的保证。

Abstract: Granite is a methodology for modular verification of both functional correctness and nonleakage of RTL processors against ISA contracts. We prove that the cycle-by-cycle timing of a pipelined RISC design--with speculation, precise interrupts, and I/O--is determined solely by observables specified in an ISA leakage contract. For programs that keep observables independent of secrets (i.e., following the cryptographic-constant-time discipline), this result rules out information leakage through known and unknown timing side channels.
  Granite's specifications only constrain functional correctness and information-flow dependencies: not how many cycles an instruction takes, at which instruction an interrupt is handled, or the exact latencies of submodules such as multipliers and memory. Granite's central technique is leakage-aware refinement via determinism, which establishes correctness and confidentiality together as trace equivalence with respect to a family of cycle-level, deterministic spec machines. Secret-independent nondeterminism is handled by existentially parameterizing specs with untrusted, deterministic functions acting only on public data. Submodules are proved against their own leakage-aware specs, and these proofs compose into the whole-design guarantee--which therefore holds over a space of secure implementations.
  We believe this work is the first to achieve modular and foundational connection between instruction-set-level leakage contracts and microarchitecture-specific cycle-by-cycle execution with wire-level observations. Our proofs compose with a certified static analysis that recognizes cryptographic-constant-time code to derive a single Rocq theorem about the cycle-by-cycle confidentiality of a hardware-and-software cryptographic implementation--eliminating every intermediate specification, including the ISA contract itself, from the trusted computing base.

</details>


### [324] [Flock: Fast Proving for Batch Boolean Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27491)
*Benedikt Bünz,Ron D. Rothblum,William Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Flock是一种基于哈希的SNARK系统，用于极快速地证明大批量布尔电路计算（如SHA-256、Keccak、BLAKE3），在M4 Max单核上每秒可证明82k次BLAKE3压缩函数，比椭圆曲线SNARK快500倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNARK应用中，证明大规模标准密码学哈希函数（如SHA-256、Keccak、BLAKE3）的批量求值是关键瓶颈，现有方案速度不足，需要更高效的证明系统。

Method: Flock结合了lincheck和zerocheck协议的新优化，并采用编码智能体共同设计的激进优化概念验证实现。它证明相同R1CS电路（加上输入输出关系）的批次，支持哈希链、Merkle路径开证明，可扩展至完整哈希签名验证。

Result: 在M4 Max单核上，Flock每秒可证明82k次BLAKE3压缩函数、42k次SHA-256压缩、30k次Keccak排列，开销仅为原生执行的250倍以下；十核上BLAKE3吞吐量超660k/秒；SHA-256证明比Binius64快9倍以上，比最快的椭圆曲线SNARK快500倍以上。

Conclusion: Flock在批量哈希计算的SNARK证明速度上实现了重大突破，显著超越先前的状态最优系统，为大批量哈希证明应用提供了实用的高性能解决方案。

Abstract: For many applications of SNARKs, a key bottleneck is proving large batches of standard cryptographic hash evaluations, such as SHA-256, Keccak, or BLAKE3. We introduce Flock, a hash-based SNARK for extremely fast proving of such batched Boolean computations. Flock proves batches of the same R1CS circuit (plus input/output relations between them), can prove hash-chains and Merkle path openings, and in principle can be extended to full-fledged hash-based signature verification. At its core, Flock combines new optimizations for the lincheck and zerocheck protocols with an aggressively optimized proof-of-concept implementation co-designed by coding agents.
  On a single core of an M4 Max processor, Flock proves 82k evaluations of the BLAKE3 compression function, 42k SHA-256 compressions, and 30k Keccak permutations per second --- less than a $250\times$ overhead over native execution. On ten cores, throughput exceeds 660k BLAKE3 compressions per second; in proving SHA-256, Flock is more than $9\times$ faster than Binius64, the prior state of the art, and more than $500\times$ faster than the fastest elliptic curve-based SNARK we measured against.

</details>


### [325] [Send and Pretend: Exploiting Transcript Consistency Issues in End-to-End Encrypted Group Chats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27510)
*Gabriel K. Gegenhuber,Moritz Grefner,Maximilian Günther,Matthäus Wininger,David Schmidt,Aljosha Judmayer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现当今主流端到端加密（E2EE）消息应用（如Signal、iMessage等）在群聊中无法保证转录一致性（TC），恶意群成员可以针对不同接收者选择性省略、重排或篡改内容而不触发界面警告，并展示了包括操纵投票在内的多种具体攻击场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E2EE消息应用被广泛用于群聊中的敏感协调（如政治决策者），且新引入的投票功能隐含了所有参与者应看到相同对话视图的期望（转录一致性），但现有主流应用是否真正保证这一属性尚未被系统验证。

Method: 在恶意参与者威胁模型下系统性地调查转录一致性问题，识别从协议回退路径到群内点对点投递通道的多种多义向量，并开发具体利用场景进行实证验证。

Result: （1）主流E2EE消息应用均不保证群聊的任何形式的转录一致性；（2）识别出多个利用向量（协议回退、点对点投递等）；（3）演示了社交工程、逃避审核和操纵投票等具体攻击；（4）发现实现相关的隐私问题（如设备OS指纹识别）。

Conclusion: 转录一致性问题跨越多个主流协议和服务，需要结合协议层面的低开销缓解措施和界面信号策略来解决，作者为此提出了实用方案。

Abstract: End-to-end encrypted (E2EE) messaging apps are widely praised for their security and thus also used for sensitive coordination in group chats (e.g., by political decision makers). After Threema and WhatsApp, also Signal and iMessage have recently introduced polls to aid agreement processes in groups. This implicitly sets the expectation that all participants see the same outcome and thus have the same view of the conversation. This property is commonly referred to as transcript consistency (TC). In this work, we demonstrate that today's major E2EE messengers do not guarantee any form of TC for group chats, allowing a malicious group member to selectively omit, reorder, or present altered content to different recipients without triggering warnings in their user interface. We systematically investigate the extent of the problem under a malicious-participant threat model that targets the integrity of the shared transcript, or inconsistent delivery across a user's linked devices. We identify multiple equivocation vectors that range from protocol fallback paths to deliberate use of pairwise delivery channels within groups. We demonstrate concrete exploitation scenarios such as social engineering, evading moderation, and, in particular, rigging polls. Beyond these cross-service design issues, we also uncover implementation-specific behaviors with privacy implications (e.g., device OS fingerprinting). Finally, we contextualize our findings within prior transcript-consistency research and outline practical low-overhead mitigations and UI signaling strategies that can be integrated into state-of-the-art E2EE group protocols.

</details>


### [326] [ThreatForest: Multi-Agent Attack Tree Generation with Pluggable TTP Framework Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27528)
*Cristian Leo,Anton Dykyi,Danny Cortegaca,Daniel Begimher,Prakash Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ThreatForest是一个多智能体系统，能够从源代码仓库自动生成结构化攻击树，将攻击步骤映射到MITRE ATT&CK、CAPEC和云特定威胁矩阵等对抗框架的TTP，并综合可操作的缓解措施。该系统采用多阶段代理流水线，包含确定性验证门、有界重试和三个人机协同验证点。研究发现嵌入阶段（而非流水线本身）是准确性的主要瓶颈，面板评分显示威胁声明、攻击树和缓解措施质量达0.63-0.68，但仅嵌入的TTP映射只有0.29。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 威胁建模对安全软件开发至关重要，但手动分析云原生架构速度慢且需要稀缺的安全专家。现有方法缺乏自动化端到端解决方案，能够将代码仓库转化为结构化的攻击模型和可操作的安全建议。

Method: ThreatForest将威胁建模分解为多阶段代理流水线：仓库分析、上下文细化、威胁生成、并行攻击树构建（含TTP映射和缓解措施综合）以及报告生成，以有向图方式编排并配备确定性验证门、有界重试和三个人机协同验证点。使用域特定的sentence-transformer通过余弦相似度将攻击步骤映射到候选技术。

Result: 在七个应用领域的十六维度评分标准上，由独立LLM评审小组评分的面板质量在威胁声明、攻击树和缓解措施上达到0.63-0.68（0-1尺度），但仅嵌入的TTP映射只有0.29。受控的单次调用基线将映射可辩护性提高了一倍以上，证明瓶颈在于嵌入编码器而非多智能体设计。

Conclusion: ThreatForest是首个将代码仓库端到端转化为跨对抗框架的TTP映射攻击树和基于证据的缓解措施的系统，并提供了可复用的基准测试框架。研究的关键洞见是指出了TTP映射的准确性瓶颈源于嵌入阶段而非整体流水线设计。

Abstract: Threat modeling is essential for secure software development, yet manual analysis of cloud-native architectures is slow and demands scarce security expertise. We present ThreatForest, a multi-agent system that generates structured attack trees from source code repositories, maps attack steps to adversary tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs) from a pluggable set of frameworks (MITRE ATT&CK, CAPEC, and cloud-specific threat matrices), and synthesizes actionable mitigations. ThreatForest decomposes threat modeling into a multi-stage agent pipeline -- repository analysis, context refinement, threat generation, parallel attack-tree construction with TTP mapping and mitigation synthesis, and report generation -- orchestrated as a directed graph with deterministic verification gates, bounded retries, and three human-in-the-loop validation points. A domain-specific sentence-transformer maps each attack step to candidate techniques by cosine similarity; we show empirically that this embedding stage, not the surrounding pipeline, is the dominant accuracy bottleneck. We evaluate ThreatForest across seven application domains on a sixteen-dimension rubric, scored by a panel of independent LLM raters with an adversarial verification pass and expert review. Panel-measured quality reaches 0.63-0.68 (on a 0-1 scale) for threat statements, attack trees, and mitigations, but only 0.29 for embedding-only TTP mapping -- a gap stable across all seven domains that isolates the binding constraint. A controlled single-call baseline on the same model more than doubles mapping defensibility, pinning the limitation on the embedding encoder rather than the multi-agent design. To our knowledge, ThreatForest is the first end-to-end system that turns a code repository into TTP-mapped attack trees with evidence-based mitigations across adversary frameworks, with a reusable framework for benchmarking such systems.

</details>


### [327] [AnchorMark: Robust Diffusion Watermarking via Latent-Space Rotation Synchrony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27551)
*Yuqi Qian,Yun Cao,Haocheng Fu,Haochen Zhao,Hong Zhang,Meineng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AnchorMark，一种无需训练、鲁棒的基于反演的水印方法。利用潜在空间中的"旋转同步性"性质，在初始潜变量的中心区域嵌入同步锚点，从而在提取时准确估计并校正旋转角度，显著提升旋转及组合攻击下的比特准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于反演的水印方法对复合有损后处理不够鲁棒，尤其是旋转攻击会破坏潜空间解码所需的空间对应关系，导致水印提取失败。

Method: 发现潜在空间的"旋转同步性"性质（图像域旋转与恢复的初始潜变量旋转角度一致），据此在初始潜变量中心区域嵌入同步锚点，在提取阶段估计并校正旋转角度，从而恢复水印解码所需的正确空间对齐。

Result: 实验表明，AnchorMark在旋转攻击及组合攻击下显著提高了比特准确率，且对图像质量影响有限。

Conclusion: AnchorMark通过利用旋转同步性并嵌入同步锚点，有效解决了现有反演水印方法在旋转攻击下的脆弱性问题，实现了无需训练的鲁棒水印方案。

Abstract: Inversion-based watermarking embeds watermark payloads directly into the generative process, avoiding a separate post-hoc image-domain embedding stage while preserving the native visual fidelity of synthesized images. However, existing methods remain vulnerable to compound lossy post-processing, particularly when rotation is involved, as it disrupts the spatial correspondence required for latent-space decoding. To overcome this limitation, we introduce AnchorMark, a training-free, robust inversion-based watermarking. We uncover a latent-space property termed Rotation Synchrony: image-domain rotations and their counterparts in the recovered initial latent share the same angle. Building on this property, AnchorMark embeds a synchronization anchor in the central region of the initial latent, enabling accurate estimation and correction of the rotation angle during extraction. Experiments show that AnchorMark substantially improves bit accuracy under rotation and combined attacks, with limited impact on image quality.

</details>


### [328] [Revisiting the Adversarial Robustness of Graph-Based Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27604)
*Qingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了基于图AI的交通流量预测的鲁棒性问题，指出现有对抗攻击研究基于不现实的威胁模型和非目标目标。作者提出了一种实用的物理感知目标攻击，可局部化到特定路段，并引入了一种基于物理信息的检测-缓解防御框架，在15个模型-数据集设置中的13个上超越了对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通预测对抗攻击研究基于不现实的威胁模型（如全网络攻击）和非目标目标，无法反映实际攻击者的能力。实用的攻击者只监控和操作少数传感器，且攻击目标应局部化到特定路段而非整个网络。作者认为需要重新审视攻击和防御两个方面。

Method: 将鲁棒性重新定义为检测问题：设计一个学习型的物理信息检测器，其输出作为输入特征输入硬化后的预测器，并在预测器固定的情况下针对自适应攻击进行训练。同时提出一种物理感知攻击方法，模拟真实拥堵以干扰特定路段的预测。

Result: 物理感知攻击使目标链接的错误倍增数倍而网络范围错误几乎不变；对抗训练（针对范数约束扰动调优）对此类攻击几乎无效。提出的检测-缓解防御在15个模型-数据集设置中的13个上优于针对物理感知攻击本身训练的对抗性防御，在保留攻击上优势最大，且清洁数据成本接近零。

Conclusion: 研究强调需要在应用特定约束下审视抽象的AI对抗攻击，以评估其真实安全影响。物理感知的检测-缓解框架是比对抗训练更有效的防御策略，为交通预测系统的安全性提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting by graph-based AI is a critical component of intelligent transportation systems, motivating security research on robustness to malicious sensor readings. We argue that prior robustness evaluations are largely shaped by unrealistic threat models and untargeted objectives, so both attacks and defenses must be revisited. We study a practical adversary with limited model knowledge and the ability to monitor and manipulate only a few road sensors. More importantly, practical attacks can be localized to specific links or routes, causing incorrect estimated arrival times or unnecessary rerouting while leaving the broader network largely unaffected. This targeted setting remains underexplored, and defenses such as adversarial training do not transfer well from the norm-bounded attacks they train on to structurally different, physics-aware attacks that mimic genuine congestion. We therefore reframe robustness as a detection problem, introducing a learned physics-informed detector whose output is fed to a hardened forecaster as an input feature and trained against adaptive attacks with the forecaster fixed. We evaluate across a variety of model architectures and benchmarks. The physics-aware attack multiplies target-link error several-fold while the network-wide error barely moves, and adversarial training, tuned to norm-bounded perturbations, barely dents it. Our detection--mitigation defense improves even on adversarial training hardened against the physics-aware attack itself, on $13$ of $15$ model--dataset settings and by the widest margin on a held-out attack, at near-zero clean cost. The results emphasize the need to examine abstracted AI adversarial attacks under application-specific constraints to assess their true security impacts.

</details>


### [329] [Strategy Phasing of Cyber Attacks on Digital Substations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27661)
*Akila Herath,Chen-Ching Liu,Junho Hong,Kuchan Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SubCASP，一种基于隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）的方法，用于融合IDS数据日志，推断变电站网络攻击的当前阶段、下一攻击阶段及回溯攻击路径，以弥补现有IDS仅能识别孤立攻击症状而缺乏攻击上下文感知的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符合IEC 61850标准的数字变电站虽提升了电力系统运行效率，但攻击者可利用IEC 61850通信操纵断路器操作，造成严重影响。此类网络攻击基于多阶段策略策划，而现有入侵检测系统（IDS）往往仅标记孤立的攻击症状，缺乏攻击阶段的上下文信息，难以支持缓解措施的有效部署。

Method: 基于ATT&CK威胁建模推导攻击阶段，采用隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）融合IDS数据日志，推断当前攻击阶段、预测下一攻击阶段，并回溯攻击路径。模型在可复现的攻击图数据集上进行训练和评估。

Result: 测试结果表明，SubCASP在各种IDS可观测性水平和IDS数据日志缺失场景下均表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SubCASP能够为变电站网络攻击提供阶段感知能力，弥补现有IDS缺乏攻击上下文的问题，有助于支持更有针对性的缓解措施部署。

Abstract: Digital substations that comply with IEC 61850 have improved the operational efficiency of modern power systems. However, adversaries can abuse IEC 61850 communication to manipulate circuit breaker operations in substations, which can result in severe system impacts. These cyber attacks are crafted based on broader multi-phase strategies. The existing intrusion detection systems (IDSs) often flag only isolated symptoms. Thus, there is a lack of context in the attack phase to support the deployment of mitigation measures. This paper proposes Substation Cyber Attack Strategy Phasing (SubCASP), a Hidden Markov Model(HMM)- based method that fuses IDS data logs to infer the current attack phase, next attack phase, and retrospective attack path. The attack phases are derived from an ATT&CK-based threat modeling. The SubCASP model is trained and evaluated on a reproducible attack-graph dataset. Test results are presented to demonstrate the robustness of SubCASP for various IDS observability levels and missing IDS data logs scenarios.

</details>


### [330] [Distributed Point Functions and Function Secret Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27696)
*Elette Boyle,Niv Gilboa,Yuval Ishai,Peter Scholl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是对分布式点函数（DPF）及其扩展函数秘密共享（FSS）的综述文章，涵盖其定义、构造方法和广泛应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPF作为一种轻量级密码学原语，在隐私信息检索、匿名通信、安全计算预处理和伪随机相关生成器等多个领域有广泛应用，需要系统性地梳理其定义、构造与应用。

Method: 采用综述方法，系统梳理DPF的定义、构造方案及应用场景，并讨论DPF向FSS的扩展，阐明FSS如何将点函数推广到更丰富的函数类别。

Result: 总结了DPF的现有构造方法及其在多个密码学应用中的使用方式，并展示了DPF扩展至FSS后能够为大多数DPF应用提供类似的通用化支持。

Conclusion: DPF是一种高效且应用广泛的密码学原语，其向FSS的扩展进一步拓展了应用边界，为该领域的研究者和实践者提供了全面的参考框架。

Abstract: A distributed point function (DPF) is a cryptographic primitive that enables compressed additive sharing of a secret weight-1 vector (equivalently, a point function) across two or more parties. The appealing lightweight structure of DPF constructions has enabled a wide range of applications. These include private information retrieval, anonymous messaging, secure computation with preprocessing, and pseudorandom correlation generators for expanding small correlated seeds into large pseudorandom instances of cryptographic correlations.
  In this article, we survey definitions, constructions, and applications of DPFs. We also discuss the extension of DPF to function secret sharing (FSS), which generalizes point functions to support richer function classes. Efficient FSS schemes yield a similar generalization for most of the applications of DPFs.

</details>


### [331] [CHARGE: Leveraging CWE Hierarchies for Hardware Security SystemVerilog Assertion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27776)
*Xiao Tan,Cynthia Sturton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CHARGE框架，利用CWE层次结构和大型语言模型自动生成未验证RTL模块的安全属性，无需可信设计规格，在Hack@DAC18/19/21设计中检测出27/42个已知漏洞，并发现一个新漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全属性生成依赖可信的设计规格和大量人工工程投入，而许多RTL模块未经验证且缺乏规范文档，导致硬件安全验证困难且成本高昂。

Method: 利用CWE条目的层次结构特性来提升识别未验证RTL模块中安全关键资产的准确性，通过推理推断预期安全行为，从已识别的资产和CWE语义生成安全属性，避免了可信设计规格的需求。

Result: 在Hack@DAC18/19/21开源SoC设计上使用GPT-4.1评估，检测出42个已知漏洞中的27个；Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC上89%的生成SVA可在JasperGold FPV中运行，92.2%为非空洞断言；相比人工编写的属性，CHARGE正确生成了三个人工属性错误漏洞的属性，并发现了一个此前未被识别的新漏洞。

Conclusion: CHARGE框架通过结合CWE层次结构和LLM，能够在不依赖可信规格的情况下自动生成有效的安全属性，准确性和实用性均优于或补充了人工编写的属性，展示了自动化硬件安全验证的可行方向。

Abstract: This paper presents CHARGE, an automated framework for generating security properties for unverified RTL modules using CWEs and large language models (LLMs). The hallmark is a reasoning process that leverages the hierarchical nature of CWE entries to improve accuracy when identifying security-critical assets in unverified RTL modules. As a result, the approach can infer expected security behaviors and generate properties from identified assets and CWE semantics, avoiding the need for trusted design specifications and reducing manual engineering effort. We evaluate the framework on the Hack@DAC18, 19, and 21 open source SoC designs using OpenAI's GPT-4.1. CHARGE detects 27 of 42 known bugs in these designs. For Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC, 89% of the generated SVAs run in Cadence JasperGold FPV, and 92.2% are non-vacuous. We compare to an open-source, manually written set of properties for these designs and find that CHARGE correctly writes properties for three bugs in which the manually written properties were incorrect. In addition, CHARGE-generated properties identify a new bug in the Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC that was not previously identified.

</details>


### [332] [Adaptive Security at the Edge for 6G-Enabled Healthcare IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27858)
*Ijaz Ahmad,Ijaz Ahmad,Erkki Harjula*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出NANOEDGEGUARD，一种运行在边缘网关内核平面的闭环速率控制器，通过内核流量控制钩子执行可审计的多级速率策略，以缓解突发流量对医疗物联网告警延迟的影响。实验表明，相比用户空间防火墙基线，99分位告警RTT降低了13.3%，同时超额突发流量比无管控时减少46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网边缘网关需要同时转发常规遥测数据和罕见的紧急告警，即使是短暂的流量突发也会显著放大最坏情况下的延迟，干扰紧急消息的及时送达。

Method: 设计了一个内核平面的闭环控制器，观察每个数据源的流量强度，并通过内核流量控制钩子实施可审计的多级速率策略。采用迟滞机制实现快速执行和显式恢复，并记录策略转换以供审计。

Result: 在树莓派网关上部署MQTT代理和两个ESP32端点进行实验，自适应内核平面速率控制相比用户空间防火墙基线将99分位告警RTT降低13.3%，且保持与无执行策略相当的RTT水平；相比无管控，多余放行的突发流量减少46%。

Conclusion: 自适应、可审计的网关级执行能够提升医疗物联网的弹性，且该方法可扩展至未来边缘智能中的按需策略部署。

Abstract: Healthcare IoT services increasingly rely on edge gateways to relay routine telemetry and deliver rare but timecritical alarms. Even short traffic bursts can inflate worstcase delay and interfere with urgent messages. We present NANOEDGEGUARD, a kernel-plane closed-loop controller that observes per-source traffic intensity at the edge and enforces an auditable, multi-tier rate policy using in-kernel traffic-control hooks. Unlike static firewall rules or user-space control loops, our design prioritizes fast actuation and explicit recovery through hysteresis, and it records policy transitions for auditability. Using a Raspberry Pi gateway hosting an MQTT broker and two ESP32 endpoints generating vitals, alarms, and a timed burst, we show that adaptive kernel-plane rate control reduces the 99th-percentile alarm RTT by 13.3% compared to a user-space firewall baseline while maintaining no-enforcement-level RTT, and it reduces excess admitted burst traffic by 46% compared to no enforcement. These early results indicate that adaptive, auditable enforcement at the gateway can improve resilience for healthcare IoT, and it can be extended toward on-demand policy deployment in future edge intelligence.

</details>


### [333] [Don't Trust the AI Ecosystem: Analyzing Privacy Leakage in Compromised Open-Source Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27886)
*Jin-Seong Kim,Han-Ju Lee,Seok-Won Hong,Takeshi Takahashi,Chansu Han,Tomohiro Morikawa,Seok-Hwan Choi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GradLock，一种训练时注入攻击方法，通过在供应链环境下向模型参数中隐蔽注入敏感训练数据，利用无状态确定性索引和动态梯度锁定机制，实现像素级完美数据重建（SSIM≈1.0）和瞬时提取（<1秒），且对量化、剪枝和微调等部署优化具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型反演（MI）攻击依赖训练后优化来恢复私有数据，但受到目标模型泛化瓶颈的约束，特别是在高维数据集上往往只能恢复出通用特征而非特定身份。本文旨在解决这一根本性局限。

Method: GradLock采用两种核心机制：（1）无状态确定性索引，在模型参数中建立隔离的数据保险库；（2）动态梯度锁定，在优化过程中防止有效载荷退化。攻击者在供应链环境中操作，无需保留对训练环境的访问权限即可完成数据提取。

Result: 在MNIST、Imagenette和CelebA数据集上，GradLock实现了近无损重建（SSIM≈1.0）和瞬时提取（<1.0秒）。相比现有训练时注入方法，对量化、剪枝和微调等标准部署优化具有更强鲁棒性。用户部署研究显示93.3%的参与者未能检测到恶意逻辑。

Conclusion: GradLock揭示了现代AI供应链安全中的一个严重盲区，训练时注入攻击能够实现比传统MI攻击更精确、更鲁棒的数据窃取，对AI供应链安全构成重大威胁。

Abstract: Existing model inversion (MI) attacks predominantly rely on post-training optimization to recover private data from model outputs. However, these methods are fundamentally constrained by the target model's generalization bottleneck, often yielding generic features rather than specific identities, particularly on high-dimensional datasets. In this paper, we introduce GradLock, a novel training-time injection attack that stealthily injects sensitive training data directly into the model parameters. Operating within a compromised supply chain context, GradLock leverages stateless deterministic indexing to establish isolated data vaults and employs dynamic gradient locking to prevent payload degradation during the optimization process. This mechanism allows the adversary to extract pixel-perfect data from the final model without retaining access to the training environment. Extensive experiments on MNIST, Imagenette, and CelebA demonstrate that GradLock achieves near-lossless reconstruction (SSIM ~ 1.0) and instant extraction (< 1.0s). Compared to existing training-time injection methods, our approach exhibits superior robustness against standard deployment optimizations, including quantization, pruning, and fine-tuning. Furthermore, a user deployment study reveals that 93.3% of participants failed to detect the malicious logic, highlighting a severe blind spot in the security of modern AI supply chains.

</details>


### [334] [Benign on Label, Malicious by Design: Clean-Label Dormant-to-Activated Backdoor via Machine Unlearning with Removable Camouflage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27936)
*Dongdong Zhao,Can Li,Xiang Yao,Fan He,Qihang Ge,Baogang Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双生成器学习的干净标签"未学习激活"后门攻击框架，通过双层优化交替学习建立潜在触发-目标关联的样本特定触发器和提供可移除抑制的标签一致伪装样本。当少量伪装样本被未学习移除后，抑制被解除，潜伏后门被激活。实验表明该方法在预未学习攻击成功率和未学习后激活效果上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击在植入后立即生效，容易被暴露。虽然"未学习激活的休眠后门"通过在训练后保持不活跃来规避行为暴露，但现有方法难以在干净标签约束和现实未学习请求下同时实现低预未学习攻击成功率和强后未学习激活效果，因为需要同时建立持久的潜在关联和可移除的抑制影响。

Method: 提出基于双生成器学习的干净标签未学习激活后门框架，将其形式化为双层优化问题：通过模拟潜在后门建立和机器未学习过程，交替学习(1)建立潜在触发-目标关联的样本特定触发器，(2)提供可移除抑制的标签一致伪装样本。当少量伪装样本被未学习后，抑制解除，休眠后门被激活。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-10上的实验表明，该方法在多个未学习算法下比代表性后门基线维持更低的预未学习攻击成功率，同时实现更强的后未学习激活效果。

Conclusion: 通过协调持久的潜在关联与可移除的抑制，可以在干净标签和现实删除约束下实现可靠的休眠-激活转换，证明了此类后门攻击的可实现性。

Abstract: Existing backdoor attacks often become effective immediately after backdoor implantation and may therefore be exposed before exploitation. Machine unlearning activated dormant backdoors mitigate such behavioral exposure by remaining inactive after training and becoming effective only after selected training records are unlearned. However, existing methods struggle to simultaneously achieve a low pre-unlearning attack success rate and strong post-unlearning activation under clean-label constraints and realistic unlearning requests. Achieving this transition requires jointly establishing a persistent latent association and a removable suppressive influence. To address this challenge, we propose a clean-label unlearning-activated backdoor framework based on dual-generator learning and formulate it as a bilevel optimization problem: By simulating latent backdoor establishment and machine unlearning, the framework alternately learns sample-specific triggers that establish a latent trigger-to-target association and label-consistent camouflage samples that provide removable suppression. Once a small subset of camouflage samples is unlearned, the suppression is lifted and the dormant backdoor is activated. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-10 show that our method maintains lower pre-unlearning attack success rates while achieving stronger post-unlearning activation across multiple unlearning algorithms than representative backdoor baselines. These results demonstrate that reliable dormancy-to-activation transitions can be achieved by coordinating a persistent latent association with removable suppression under clean-label and realistic deletion constraints.

</details>


### [335] [Safeguards Based on Copyable Context Cannot Provide Reliable Safety for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27951)
*Pingyu Wu,Lingyao Zhu,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出大语言模型安全防护存在根本性缺陷：模型在回答前无法预知答案将如何被使用。作者通过数学推导证明了一个"安全三难困境"：有用能力、可靠安全和开放访问三者无法兼得，并提出可信凭证机制作为补充方案来降低攻击者受益下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM防护机制在回答前无法区分合法用户与伪装成正常请求的攻击者，因为攻击者可以模仿任何良性请求和交互历史。这是一个双用途任务（dual-use）的根本性问题，现有防护手段无法从机制上解决。

Method: 将模型释放的能力与下游使用的证据分开建模；推导当证据可复制时，攻击者获得帮助的最坏情况下限的精确表达式；提出安全三难困境理论；引入可信凭证（trusted credential）作为难以复制的信息来预测真实用途；分析消除下限所需的条件。

Result: 证明"有用能力、可靠安全、开放访问"三者不可兼得的安全三难困境；证明可信凭证可以补充现有防护机制，降低攻击者受益下限；但需要更强的条件才能完全消除该下限；双用途评估、自适应攻击和已部署的可信访问程序验证了这些条件的实践相关性。

Conclusion: LLM安全防护不能仅依赖模型层面的过滤，需要引入外部不可复制凭据来区分下游用途。理解并接受三难困境的存在是制定合理安全策略的前提。

Abstract: Large language model safeguards decide whether to answer before seeing how an answer will be used. This creates a basic problem for dual-use tasks: the same answer can help an authorized professional or an attacker, while an attacker can imitate a benign request and interaction history. We separate the capability released by the model from the evidence available about downstream use. When that evidence is copyable, we derive the exact worst-case floor on attacker assistance while preserving useful answers. The result yields a safety trilemma: Useful Capability, Reliable Safety, and Open Access cannot coexist. We then show how a trusted credential can complement existing safeguards by adding hard-to-copy information that predicts actual downstream use, and identify the stronger condition needed to eliminate the floor. Evidence from dual-use evaluations, adaptive attacks, and deployed trusted-access programs supports the practical relevance of these conditions.

</details>


### [336] [Driving up Inference Energy on SNNs: Per-Sample and Universal Sponge Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27990)
*Spyridon Raptis,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了脉冲神经网络（SNN）的稀疏二值事件驱动特性使其面临独特的"海绵攻击"安全风险。作者提出了两种攻击模型：逐样本海绵攻击（通过梯度优化为每个输入定制对抗性脉冲序列，使SynOps增加1.5-2.6倍）和首个面向原生事件输入SNN的通用海绵攻击（固定二值扰动通过XOR应用于所有输入，SynOps增加1.09-1.24倍）。在三个数据集（NMNIST、SHD、IBM DVS Gesture）上验证了攻击有效性，映射到Loihi-1硬件上每推理能耗增加14μJ至13.24mJ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN因其稀疏二值脉冲通信和低能耗特性被广泛应用于边缘设备，但这种效率优势同时引入了新的安全风险。已有的效率攻击主要针对率编码（rate-coded）SNN进行逐样本优化，缺乏对原生事件驱动（event-based）二值输入的攻击研究，更缺少通用性攻击的探索，因此需要填补这一安全研究空白。

Method: 本文提出两种攻击模型：（1）逐样本海绵攻击：通过基于梯度的优化为每个输入定制对抗性脉冲序列，在保持预测类别的同时最大化神经突触运算量（SynOps）；（2）通用海绵攻击：离线计算固定二值扰动，通过XOR运算应用于所有输入，无需逐样本优化。攻击在三个SNN模型和三个数据集上进行评估，并将SynOps膨胀映射为Loihi-1硬件上的能量开销。

Result: 逐样本海绵攻击使每次推理的SynOps增加1.5-2.6倍，同时至少在98%的评估样本上保持预测类别不变；通用海绵攻击使SynOps增加1.09-1.24倍。映射到Loihi-1估计能量后，每推理能耗开销为14μJ至13.24mJ。

Conclusion: 原生事件驱动的SNN容易受到实用性的输入空间效率攻击；可复用的通用扰动在持续部署的边缘系统中能累积为显著的电池消耗，构成真实的安全威胁。这强调了在SNN能量效率和安全性之间需要更全面的权衡考虑。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) communicate through sparse binary spike events rather than dense activations, enabling energy-efficient inference on neuromorphic hardware and motivating their use in always-on, battery-powered edge systems. We show that this same efficiency advantage creates a distinct security risk: sponge attacks can increase inference-time spike activity and synaptic workload, inflating energy consumption while remaining difficult to detect through correctness-based monitoring alone. Prior input-space efficiency attacks on SNNs have focused on per-sample optimization, primarily in rate-coded settings. We extend this threat to native event-based binary inputs and study two attack models. First, we develop a per-sample sponge attack that crafts a custom adversarial spike train for each input via gradient-based optimization. This attack increases per-inference SynOps by 1.5-2.6x on three SNN models for the NMNIST, SHD, and IBM DVS Gesture datasets, while preserving the predicted class on at least 98% of evaluated samples. Second, to the best of our knowledge, we introduce the first universal sponge attack for native event-based SNN inputs: a fixed binary perturbation computed offline and applied via XOR to all subsequent inputs. Although weaker, it still inflates SynOps by 1.09-1.24x across all three datasets and represents a more realistic deployment threat because it requires no per-input optimization. Mapping SynOp inflation to estimated Loihi-1 energy yields per-inference overheads from 14 $μ$J to 13.24 mJ. These results show that native event-based SNNs are vulnerable to practical input-space efficiency attacks, and that reusable universal perturbations can accumulate into meaningful battery drain in continuously deployed edge systems.

</details>


### [337] [Temporal Poisoning: Clean-Label Backdoors via Event Redistribution in SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28075)
*Roberto Riaño,Gorka Abad,Stjepan Picek,Aitor Urbieta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次在脉冲神经网络（SNN）上研究了干净标签的时序后门攻击，通过对目标类训练流施加固定的时间戳变换（保持事件计数不变）实现攻击，在三个神经形态数据集和两种受害者模型上达到最高1.00的攻击成功率，并提出了基于逐时间步事件质量的检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN后门攻击主要假设脏标签投毒（即需篡改标签），而干净标签场景下SNN的时序投毒尚未被研究。作者希望探索是否能在不改变标签、仅通过时间维度变换的情况下，在SNN上实现隐蔽的后门攻击，并检验现有防御的盲区。

Method: 提出一种干净的时序投毒方法：仅对目标类训练流施加固定的时间戳变换，保持逐像素、逐极性的事件计数完全不变，但改变SNN处理的时序顺序。使用卷积和Transformer两类SNN作为受害者模型，在三个神经形态数据集上评估。同时进行投毒预算和触发形状的消融实验，并设计基于逐时间步事件质量的模型无关检测器。

Result: 在最强化配置下攻击成功率（ASR）达到1.00。在防御评估中，凡是在检查前折叠时间轴的防御方法在结构上完全失效；特征空间方法仅在特定设置下能检测到投毒。提出的模型无关检测器能够检测所有被评估的时序变换。

Conclusion: 干净标签时序后门攻击在SNN上是可行且高度隐蔽的，现有基于率折叠（rate-collapsed）的防御存在结构性盲区，而基于逐时间步事件质量的检测方法能够暴露此类攻击的边界，为SNN安全研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have primarily assumed dirty-label poisoning, in which triggered training samples are relabeled to an attacker-selected class. We study clean-label temporal poisoning, where a fixed timestamp transformation is applied only to the target-class training streams, leaving their labels unchanged. The transformation preserves the per-pixel, per-polarity event count exactly, making clean and triggered samples identical after temporal aggregation while altering the sequence processed by the SNN. Across three neuromorphic datasets and both convolutional and transformer-based victims, the attack reaches an ASR of 1.00 in the strongest configurations. We analyze the attack through poison-budget and trigger-shape ablations and evaluate established backdoor defenses adapted to spiking models. Defenses that collapse the time axis before inspection are blind by construction, while feature-space methods detect the poison only in selected settings. Our model-free detector, based on per-step event mass, detects the evaluated temporal transformations, demonstrating both the limitation of rate-collapsed defenses and the boundary of the attack's stealth. To our knowledge, this is the first clean-label backdoor attack evaluated on SNNs and neuromorphic event data.

</details>


### [338] [Checking Information Flow in Cloud-based IoT Access Control Policies (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28088)
*Lorenzo Ceragioli,Letterio Galletta,Edoardo Lunati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析IoT访问控制策略以识别设备间非预期信息流安全漏洞的方法，通过形式化建模AWS IoT Core组件并定义信息流图，借助SMT求解器构建有限表示来实现信息流验证，并实现了工具IOT:POKER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在IoT云平台中，即使各设备的访问控制权限单独配置正确，在不同信任级别设备或跨子系统隔离的场景下，仍然可能存在非预期的信息流安全漏洞。传统上仅验证权限配置本身是不够的，需要从系统层面分析设备间信息流动的安全性。

Method: 首先形式化建模AWS IoT Core的组件，然后定义信息流图来捕获访问控制策略所允许的设备间通信。利用SMT求解器构建图的有限表示，从而支持对设备间信息流进行形式化验证。整个方法实现为一个名为IOT:POKER的工具。

Result: 该工具在真实场景和多个真实世界策略上进行了评估，验证了其在识别IoT访问控制策略中信息流安全漏洞方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了仅验证单个设备权限配置不足以保障IoT系统整体安全，通过形式化建模和图论方法可以系统性发现跨设备的信息流安全漏洞，为IoT云平台访问控制策略验证提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Many cloud providers for IoT technologies offer access control mechanisms whose proper configuration is critical for security. However, verifying permissions in isolation is insufficient in a setting where devices have different levels of trust or are compartmentalised in various subsystems. This work analyses IoT access control policies to identify potential security vulnerabilities from unwanted information flow between devices. To this end, we formally model AWS IoT Core's components and define an information flow graph to capture the communication among devices permitted by the access control policies. We build a finite representation of the graph by leveraging an SMT solver, thus enabling the verification of information flow between devices. We implement our approach in a tool called IOT:POKER, and assess it on a realistic scenario and several real-world policies.

</details>


### [339] [Agent Harness Distillation: Inference-Time Harness Extraction and Exploitation in Autonomous Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28147)
*Yu Cui,Wuli Yang,Yirui Shi,Junhao Xia,Hui Jiang,Lei Gao,Chenfu Bao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent Harness Distillation (AHD)框架，研究基于大语言模型构建的自主多智能体系统(AMAS)中推理时harness的知识产权泄露风险。AHD通过黑盒交互在蒸馏前、后两个阶段提取目标智能体的推理时harness能力，实验证明其有效性，并提出基于欺骗的防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究关注静态多智能体系统中的知识产权泄露，但自主多智能体系统(AMAS)中harness行为在推理时动态涌现，其知识产权泄露风险尚未被探索。

Method: AHD框架包含两个阶段：(1)预处理阶段——从目标智能体的响应中推断推理时harness行为并构建初始harness；(2)后处理阶段——迭代精炼初始harness以对齐目标智能体的行为模式。整个提取过程通过黑盒交互完成，并配套开发了量化此类风险的评估框架。

Result: 在多个真实世界AMAS和多种骨干LLM上的实验表明，AHD能有效提取推理时harness，揭示出显著的IP泄露风险；同时提出的基于欺骗的防御方法能在保持受保护智能体功能的前提下降低harness提取的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了AMAS中一个此前未被充分探索的安全威胁——推理时harness提取导致的IP泄露，并为量化风险和设计防御措施提供了基础框架。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent systems (AMAS) built on large language models (LLMs), such as Hermes, increasingly rely on inference-time harnesses to coordinate reasoning and action. Constructing these harnesses requires substantial engineering effort and computational resources, as they are iteratively optimized over a combinatorial search space while co-evolving with the underlying LLM. Inference-time harnesses therefore constitute valuable intellectual property (IP). Although prior work has investigated IP leakage in static multi-agent systems with pre-configured architectures, it remains unclear whether similar risks arise in AMAS, where harness behavior emerges dynamically during inference. To address this gap, we introduce Agent Harness Distillation (AHD), a framework for studying the security risks arising from inference-time harness extraction in AMAS. We formalize harness extraction as a new security problem and develop an evaluation framework for quantifying such risks.
