AHD extracts inference-time harness capabilities from a target agent through black-box interactions and consists of two stages. In the pre-distillation stage, AHD infers inference-time harness behaviors from the responses of the target agent and constructs an initial harness. In the post-distillation stage, AHD iteratively refines the initial harness to align with the behavioral patterns of the target agent. Experiments on real-world AMAS across multiple backbone LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of AHD and reveal substantial IP leakage risks. We further propose a deception-based defense that reduces harness extraction effectiveness while preserving the utility of the protected agent. Our findings uncover a previously underexplored security threat to AMAS.

</details>


### [340] [Piggybacking on Perception: Stealthy Concurrent Audio Prompt Injections against Multimodal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28165)
*Mingxiao Liu,Yitong Li,Haoren Zhao,Yaoxiang Bian,Jianan Ma,Jian Zhang,Jialuo Chen,Xinhao Deng,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了针对多模态智能体的并发音频提示注入攻击，提出指令增强和场景隐蔽技术，构建了涵盖8个真实任务场景和10种攻击模式的音频注入攻击基准，并设计了基于声源分离和跨模态一致性分析的级联防御机制，在真实世界实验中验证了攻击的高隐蔽性和防御的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型驱动的多模态智能体通过持续音频交互执行自主任务，音频输入不可避免包含用户无法控制的环境噪声，这为攻击者提供了新的攻击面。现有研究缺乏对音频提示注入攻击的系统性研究，需要量化这种威胁并开发相应的防御手段。

Method: 提出两种核心技术：(1)指令增强与场景隐蔽技术，使恶意音频指令以不可感知的方式"寄生"在用户语音上，劫持智能体执行恶意操作；(2)构建AudioAgentSecurity基准，覆盖8个真实任务场景和10种攻击模式，评估11个先进智能体；(3)提出级联音频解耦与验证（CADV）防御机制，基于声源分离和跨模态一致性分析检测注入攻击。

Result: 在11个先进智能体上的评估表明该方法具有高攻击成功率，其中对Gemini 3 Pro的平均攻击成功率达69.10%。CADV防御机制在多种攻击向量下实现超过90%的检测成功率。在豆包AI智能手机上的人类志愿者真实世界实验证实了攻击的高隐蔽性和有效性，同时证明防御能可靠缓解这些漏洞。

Conclusion: 音频提示注入攻击对LLM驱动的多模态智能体构成严重且未充分认识的安全威胁，但通过基于声源分离和跨模态一致性分析的级联防御可以有效地检测和缓解这一问题，为未来多模态智能体的安全设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven multimodal agents are increasingly deployed to execute autonomous tasks via continuous audio interaction. While this paradigm enhances interaction naturalness, it introduces a critical yet under-explored attack surface, as audio inputs inevitably contain environmental noise beyond user control. In this paper, we investigate concurrent audio prompt injection attacks targeting multimodal agents. Distinct from traditional acoustic attacks on voice devices, we propose novel techniques for instruction augmentation and scenario concealment. These methods allow malicious audio instructions to imperceptibly "piggyback" onto user speech, thereby hijacking agents to execute malicious actions. To systematically quantify this threat, we construct AudioAgentSecurity, the first comprehensive benchmark for audio instruction injection attacks, encompassing 8 real-world task scenarios and 10 distinct attack patterns. We evaluate 11 state-of-the-art agents, including Gemini 3 Pro and GPT-4o-audio. Notably, our methods achieve an average Attack Success Rate (ASR) of 69.10\% against the advanced Gemini 3 Pro. To counter this threat, we further introduce Cascaded Audio Decoupling and Verification (CADV), a defense mechanism based on source separation and consistency analysis. Compared with existing prompt-level defenses, CADV leverages acoustic source separation and cross-modal consistency analysis to detect audio instruction injections more robustly, achieving over 90\% detection success across diverse attack vectors. Finally, real-world experiments with human volunteers on Doubao AI Smartphone in diverse dynamic real-world scenarios confirm the attacks' high stealth and efficacy, while demonstrating that our defense reliably mitigates these vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [341] [Secure Aggregation for Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning on Clinical EEG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28191)
*Pouya Rajabi,Mohsen Toorani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向临床EEG数据的隐私保护联邦学习框架，以基于掩码的安全聚合为核心，结合图通信、阈值秘密共享、抗掉线聚合、更新裁剪、Bloom过滤记录链接和辅助公证可验证性，支持半诚实和恶意两种聚合场景，并在Flower框架中实现和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许多机构在不交换原始EEG数据的情况下协同训练模型，但单独模型更新仍可能泄露隐私，需要更强的隐私保护机制。

Method: 以掩码安全聚合为核心，整合图通信、阈值秘密共享、抗掉线聚合、本地更新裁剪、可选的基于Bloom过滤的隐私保护记录链接初始化模块、辅助公证可验证性，在Flower联邦学习框架中实现，支持半诚实和恶意两种聚合设置。

Result: 在模拟跨机构医疗场景中使用TUH EEG数据进行评估，安全变体能够对聚合服务器隐藏个体更新，且与联邦模型训练兼容；半诚实变体开销最低，恶意和辅助公证变体提供更强的一致性、完整性和轻量级验证，但代价是更高的计算、通信和回合时长开销。

Conclusion: 所提出的安全聚合联邦学习框架在保护临床EEG数据隐私的同时保持了训练兼容性，半诚实变体是开销最优的选择，恶意设置虽成本更高但提供了更强的安全保障。

Abstract: Federated learning enables multiple institutions to train shared models without exchanging raw clinical EEG data, but it does not fully prevent privacy leakage from individual model updates. This paper presents a privacy-preserving federated learning framework for clinical EEG data using masking-based secure aggregation as the core protection mechanism. The framework combines graph-based communication, threshold secret sharing, dropout-resilient aggregation, local update clipping, an optional Bloom filter-based privacy-preserving record-linkage initialization module, and auxiliary-notary-based verifiability. It supports both semi-honest and malicious aggregation settings and is implemented using the Flower federated learning framework. The secure-aggregation variants are evaluated in a simulated cross-silo healthcare setting using TUH EEG-derived data under different client configurations. Under the stated assumptions, the secure variants hide individual updates from the aggregation server. The results show that these variants remain compatible with federated model training, although malicious-setting safeguards and lightweight consistency-checking mechanisms introduce additional computation, communication, and round-duration overhead. The semi-honest variant provides the lowest overhead among the secure configurations, while malicious and auxiliary-notary variants offer stronger consistency, integrity, and lightweight verification support at higher cost.

</details>


### [342] [Security of World-Model-Based Embodied AI: A Lifecycle of Threats, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28226)
*Fazhong Liu,Zhuoyan Chen,Haozhen Tan,Yan Meng,Guoxing Chen,Haojin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 这篇综述论文系统梳理了具身AI世界模型（world models）在其整个生命周期中面临的安全威胁，指出世界模型作为预测核心带来了新的安全边界——从数据、传感器、提示词或反馈中的妥协可能传播到物理行动。论文提出了生命周期分类法，将现有攻击映射到世界模型的安全属性，并设计了防御框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型为具身AI提供了预测核心，但这一预测层开启了新的安全边界，攻击可以在数据、传感器、提示词或反馈层面妥协，并最终传播到物理行动。然而现有研究往往将世界模型视为孤立组件，缺乏对其全生命周期安全威胁的系统性认识。

Method: 采用综述方法，沿着世界模型的完整生命周期（数据构建与表示学习→状态接地与想象→轨迹评估与执行→通过记忆和工具进行长期适应）追踪安全威胁；将投毒、后门、对抗样本、传感器欺骗、提示注入、轨迹操纵和供应链攻击等熟悉攻击家族映射到世界模型的具体组件；提出生命周期分类法，并设计防御结构（包括数据溯源、鲁棒接地、不确定性感知预测、轨迹门控、反馈审计和部署保障）。

Result: 论文揭示了攻击在世界模型语境中的独特含义：当攻击破坏世界状态、学习到的动力学、可供性估计或安全成本时，熟悉的攻击家族呈现出不同的威胁模式。同时指出了一种二元性：世界模型可以充当运行时安全盾牌，但当被妥协或过度信任时，会产生预测性安全幻觉。

Conclusion: 该综述提供了世界模型安全的全生命周期分类框架，映射了现有攻击类型，概述了安全失败的评估协议，并系统化地构建了多层防御策略，为具身AI世界模型的安全研究提供了结构化蓝图。

Abstract: World models give embodied AI a predictive core: they compress observations into states, simulate action-conditioned futures, and enable planning beyond reactive control. This predictive layer, however, opens a new security boundary-compromise can propagate from data, sensors, prompts, or feedback into physical action. Rather than treating world models as an isolated component, this survey traces threats across their entire lifecycle-from data construction and representation learning, through state grounding and imagination, to trajectory evaluation, execution, and long-term adaptation via memory and tools. We show that familiar attack families: poisoning, backdoors, adversarial examples, sensor spoofing, prompt injection, trajectory manipulation, and supply-chain attacks take on distinct meanings when they corrupt world states, learned dynamics, affordance estimates, or safety costs. We also highlight a duality: world models can serve as runtime safety shields, yet when compromised or over-trusted they generate predictive safety illusions. The survey offers a lifecycle taxonomy, maps existing attacks to world-model security properties, outlines evaluation protocols for safety failures, and structures defenses across provenance, robust grounding, uncertainty-aware prediction, trajectory gating, feedback auditing, and deployment assurance.

</details>


### [343] [Implementing Homomorphic Encryption-Based Logic Locking in System-on-Chip Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28542)
*Ye Ziyang,Makoto Ikeda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于二元环学习误差（Ring Learning With Errors）算法的逻辑锁定方案，实现在RISC-V系统级芯片设计中，在不暴露敏感锁定参数的前提下保护特权切换过程中的关键逻辑路径。模块消耗3519个LUT和2645个寄存器，整体开销为LUT增加6.0%、寄存器增加6.9%，解锁耗时约2.6微秒，对用户级运算效率影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逻辑锁定方法需要向用户提供原始锁定参数，存在参数泄露的安全风险，因此需要一种无需暴露敏感参数即可保护关键逻辑路径的方案。

Method: 基于二元环学习误差算法构建逻辑锁定机制，在RISC-V系统级芯片设计中用于保护特权切换过程中的关键逻辑路径，避免向用户提供原始锁定参数。

Result: 锁定模块消耗3519个查找表（LUT）和2645个寄存器，整体硬件开销为LUT增加6.0%、寄存器增加6.9%；解锁过程约需2.6微秒，主要影响系统级操作，用户级计算效率得到保留。

Conclusion: 该方案在确保芯片逻辑安全性的同时，实现了可接受的硬件开销和性能影响，是传统逻辑锁定方法的一种有效替代方案。

Abstract: This study presents a logic locking scheme based on the binary Ring Learning With Errors algorithm, implemented in a RISC-V System-on-Chip design. Unlike traditional logic locking methods that require providing users with raw locking parameters, the proposed approach secures critical logic paths in the privilege switching process without exposing these sensitive parameters. The implemented locking module itself consumes 3519 Look-Up Tables and 2645 Registers, leading to an overall overhead of 6.0% in Look-Up Tables and 6.9% in Registers compared to the baseline system. The unlock process requires about 2.6 us, introducing moderate performance impact, primarily affecting system-level operations while preserving user-level computational efficiency.

</details>


### [344] [Formalization of security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28551)
*Gilles Barthe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本章聚焦于证明助手在系统安全、语言安全、安全编译和密码学领域的应用，并探讨其用于支持认证的动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明助手常被用于验证设计与实现是否满足预期的安全属性，此外还有一个重要动机是支持安全认证流程。

Method: 本章从四个安全应用领域系统阐述证明助手的应用：系统安全、基于语言的安全、安全编译以及密码学。

Result: 文章概述了证明助手在多个安全关键领域中的应用场景和认证支持价值。

Conclusion: 证明助手不仅是验证安全属性的工具，更是支撑安全认证的重要技术手段。

Abstract: Proof assistants are often used to validate that designs and implementations meet their expected security properties. A further motivation for using proof assistants is to support certification. This chapter focuses on their applications to system security, language-based security, secure compilation, and cryptography.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [345] [Auditing Emergent LLM-Agent Collaboration through Cooperation-Obligation Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27429)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Hanqing Yang,Carlee Joe-Wong,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出iCORE（集成合作-义务表示）框架，通过统一编码观测交互（合作图G）、演化工作与分配（义务图Q）及审计映射Π，来弥合LLM-agent系统审计中的空白，实现对工作声音性（Work soundness）和代理分配稳定性（Agent-assignment stability）的认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent系统通过动态自组织和涌现合作处理复杂任务，但中间或最终输出可能掩盖不完整的工作、缺乏证据支撑的工作以及责任分配不当的问题。现有方法分别记录了消息、工具调用、来源或任务依赖，但无法联合表示"剩余工作是什么、谁负责、以及每个工作状态转换的证据依据"，存在审计空白。

Method: 提出iCORE表示，将可观测交互编码为合作图G、将演变中的工作和分配编码为义务图Q，并通过审计映射Π将两者链接起来，附带可验证属性和证据。基于此表示，审计者可以认证两个互补属性：工作声音性（每个活跃且决策相关的工作断言必须通过G和Π具有有限理由链）和代理分配稳定性（不存在可行替代代理能使已评估义务的声明贡献值改善超过ε）。iCORE作为工作流之上的仪器化层运行。

Result: 建立了局部到全局的声音性和分配遗憾保证，以及特定条件下的性能界限。数值结果表明，完整的耦合状态能在两种执行模式下精确重建声音性和分配缺陷；相比被动全状态观测，iCORE-Audit在受控和真实LLM执行中的绝对轨迹质量分别提升11.5%和26.4%，终端性能分别提升15.1%和31.0%。

Conclusion: iCORE通过统一的合作-义务表示有效弥合了LLM-agent审计空白，可同时认证工作完整性证据与责任分配的合理性，并在实际执行中显著提升审计质量和终端性能。

Abstract: LLM-agent systems can solve complex tasks through dynamic self-organization and emergent cooperation. Auditing this process is essential because plausible intermediate or final outputs can conceal incomplete or unsupported work and poorly allocated responsibility, ultimately compromising response quality. While existing approaches may record messages, tool calls, provenance, or task dependencies, an auditability gap exists as they do not jointly represent what work remains, who is responsible for it, and what evidence justifies each work-state transition. We address this auditability gap by proposing \emph{Integrated Cooperation-Obligation REpresentation} (iCORE). It creates a unified encoding $X=(G,Q,Π)$ integrating observable interactions as a cooperation graph $G$, evolving work and assignments as an obligation graph $Q$, and the audit map $Π$ linking them with verifiable properties and evidence. This iCORE representation enables the auditor to certify two complementary properties: {Work soundness}, where every active decision-relevant work assertion must have a finite justification through $G$ and $Π$; and {Agent-assignment stability}, which requires that no feasible alternative agent improve the declared contribution value for an evaluated obligation by more than $ε$. We establish local-to-global soundness and assignment-regret guarantees and a performance bound under stated conditions. iCORE is an instrumentation layer over workflows. Numerical results show that the full coupled state exactly reconstructs soundness and assignment defects in two execution modes and that, relative to passive full-state observation, iCORE-Audit yields absolute trajectory-quality improvements of $11.5\%$ and $26.4\%$ in controlled and real-LLM execution, respectively, with corresponding absolute terminal-performance improvements of $15.1\%$ and $31.0\%$.

</details>


### [346] [Stop Shipping AI Agents on Faith: Capability Is Not Production Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27677)
*Fouad Bousetouane*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出ProofAgent Index (PAI)，一个面向AI代理的生产就绪性治理指数，将部署就绪评估从单纯能力信号重构为结合评估、上下文、合规和治理四维度的可审计决策框架，并在医疗和金融领域验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理已进入生产环境，但现有发布决策过度依赖能力信号、演示或行为测试，无法反映代理是否能在生产约束下正常运行。能力不等于生产就绪性，需要新的评估框架来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出PAI治理就绪性指数，包含四个维度的部署证据：评估（测量可观测行为）、上下文（测量塑造行为的运行环境）、合规（测量与适用规则的匹配度）、治理（测量组织能否授权、监控、审计和控制代理）。PAI实现在ProofAgent Harness开源基础设施中，用于可审计的AI代理评估与治理。

Result: 在医疗和金融两个高度监管领域的验证表明，PAI携带了留出集就绪信号，能够区分高风险与低风险配置。结果显示：上下文工程显著改变可靠性，能力提升行为但不等同于就绪，治理证据必须保持可见而非被平均化掩盖。

Conclusion: PAI将代理发布从基于"信念"的部署决策重构为可审计的就绪性决策，为AI代理进入生产环境提供了治理驱动的评估范式。

Abstract: AI agents are moving into production workflows where they retrieve information, call tools, maintain state, and act on behalf of users or organizations, but many release decisions still rely on capability signals, demos, or behavioral tests that do not show whether an agent is ready to operate under production constraints. Capability is therefore not production readiness. This paper introduces the ProofAgent Index (PAI), a governance readiness index for AI agents. PAI combines four dimensions of deployment evidence: Evaluation, Context, Compliance, and Governance. Evaluation measures observed behavior, Context measures the operating environment that shapes that behavior, Compliance measures alignment with applicable rules and controls, and Governance measures whether the organization can authorize, monitor, audit, and control the agent during operation. PAI is implemented inside ProofAgent Harness, an open source infrastructure for auditable AI agent evaluation and governance. Validation across two heavily regulated domains, healthcare and finance, shows that PAI carries held out readiness signal and separates higher risk from lower risk configurations. The results show that context engineering strongly changes reliability, capability improves behavior but does not determine readiness, and governance evidence must remain visible rather than averaged away. PAI reframes agent release from a faith based deployment decision into an auditable readiness decision.

</details>


### [347] [Scaling LLM-Driven Multi-Agent Systems: Design Principles and Architectural Scalability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27942)
*Linus Sander,Fengjunjie Pan,Vahid Zolfaghari,Andre Schamschurko,Nenad Petrovic,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文从结构化分析中提炼出四个可扩展多智能体系统（MAS）架构的设计原则，并对其进行操作化及实验验证。研究发现，扩展能带来约线性成本增长的精度提升，但仅在底层LLM超过最低能力阈值时成立；性能在中等复杂度时达到峰值，之后因超时和评估限制而下降，且一致性问题在所有扩展级别上持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统虽有潜力实现集体智能并解决复杂任务，但其架构设计空间缺乏系统化，缺少广泛认可的设计原则，且可扩展性特征尚未被充分理解。

Method: 先从先前工作的结构化分析中提炼四项设计原则（简单性、弹性反馈、带可选循环的顺序工作流、基于摘要的通信），再将这些原则操作化为参考架构（拓扑形式化为约束有向工作流图），并使用两个能力不同的LLM在标准化终端系统工程项目基准上评估四种递增复杂度的配置。

Result: 扩展仅在底层LLM超过最低能力阈值时带来约线性成本增长的精度提升；性能在中等复杂度达到峰值后因超时和评估限制而下降；一致性问题在所有扩展级别上持续存在。

Conclusion: 研究为从业者提供了具体的设计指导，并指出一致性与评估标准化是未来研究的关键目标。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have the potential to enable collective intelligence and scale toward solving highly complex tasks through coordinated ensembles of specialized agents. However, despite their theoretical potential, the architectural design space remains largely non-systematized and lacks broadly established design principles. Furthermore, the scalability characteristics of such systems are only partially understood so far. This paper makes two contributions. We first distill four design principles for scalable MAS architectures from a structured analysis of prior work: simplicity, elastic feedback, sequential workflows with optional loops, and summary-based communication. We operationalize these principles in a reference architecture whose topology is formalized as a constrained directed workflow graph, and we evaluate four configurations of increasing complexity on a standardized benchmark of terminal-based system engineering tasks using two LLMs of differing capability. Our findings show that scaling yields measurable accuracy improvements with approximately linear cost growth, but only when the underlying LLM exceeds a minimum capability threshold. Performance peaks at intermediate complexity, then degrades due to timeouts and evaluation limitations. In addition, persistent consistency issues emerge as a central challenge across all scaling levels. These results provide concrete design guidance for practitioners and highlight consistency and evaluation standardization as key targets for future research.

</details>


### [348] [Argonaut: Interactive Visual Exploration for Distributed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27946)
*Srijoni Majumdar,Chuhao Qin,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Argonaut是一个轻量级、容器化的优化仪表盘，用于分布式多智能体离散选择优化问题。它提供了交互式、可视化的搜索全过程探索界面，支持用户上传数据集、构建智能体和选项、实时修改决策空间参数，并运行多种算法后端来观察每个配置如何影响局部决策和全局目标。该系统将系统构建、优化和分析整合在一个交互循环中，实现了人类在环的优化过程而非一次性黑盒计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式离散选择优化方法大多集中式且可视化支持有限，仅展示最终解决方案或将算法后端应用于固定数据集，而解决方案如何达成的过程仍是一个黑盒。随着系统规模扩大，理解其他智能体的选择、选择间的相互依赖关系以及如何共同达成全局目标变得极其困难，核心限制在于搜索过程的可观测性不足。

Method: Argonaut采用容器化架构，基于Node.js构建交互界面，并配备可扩展的Java和Python优化后端。用户可以上传数据集、构建智能体和选项、动态修改决策空间及参数，并运行多种算法后端来检查各配置如何塑造局部智能体决策和全局目标。系统将构建、优化和分析整合在图化界面中，支持多算法后端的并行检查。

Result: 在真实世界的家庭用电、共享出行和传感器数据交换数据集上进行了评估，可扩展到5600个智能体和最多100万个解决方案（暴力搜索下）。典型运行时间为200个智能体、100个决策属性，耗时不超过30秒。

Conclusion: Argonaut作为首个将分布式离散选择优化从一次性黑盒计算转变为人类在环交互过程的系统，有效解决了搜索过程可观测性不足的问题，使多智能体离散选择优化变得更加直观和可探索。

Abstract: Distributed discrete-choice optimization in decentralized settings is often hard to explore and navigate: disentangling what other agents choose, how their choices are interdependent, and how they collectively reach a global objective quickly becomes intractable as the system scales. The major limitation is observability of the search process. Existing methods are largely centralized and offer limited support, visualizing only the final solution or providing algorithm backends over a fixed dataset, so how a solution is reached stays a black box. We present Argonaut, a lightweight, containerized optimization dashboard that enables interactive, visual exploration of the entire search process for multi-agent discrete-choice optimization in decentralized settings. Users upload datasets, construct agents and options, modify the decision space and its parameters on the fly, and run multiple algorithm backends to inspect how each configuration shapes local agent decisions and the resulting global objective. By uniting system construction, optimization, and analysis in one interactive loop, the first of its kind, Argonaut makes distributed discrete-choice optimization a human-in-the-loop process rather than a one-shot, black-box computation. We evaluate Argonaut on real-world household-electricity, shared-mobility, and sensor-data-exchange datasets scaling to 5600 agents and up to 1M solutions under brute force. Built on a Node.js interface with extensible Java and Python optimization backends, it maintains a typical runtime of 200 agents over 100 decision attributes in under 30 seconds.

</details>


### [349] [$Σ$-Mem: An Online Reliability Memory for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27958)
*Peilin Feng,Suorong Yang,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Σ-Mem，一种在线可靠性记忆系统，用于记录LLM多智能体系统中各智能体的历史能力证据及各智能体间的信任关系证据。该系统通过Weyl不等式保证谱变化有界，实现无需重训练的稳定在线自适应，并提供统一的读写接口，可支持残差引导、免响应路由和可靠性加权投票等多种下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的记忆系统主要保存交互内容，而忽视了建模"哪些智能体可以被信任以及信任的条件"。在多智能体系统中，中心模型无法直接验证来自其他智能体的看似合理或相关的响应，因此需要一种能记录和更新信任关系的可靠性记忆机制。

Method: Σ-Mem维护两类证据：(1)个体智能体的历史能力证据；(2)智能体之间的关联证据。两者均以实数对称矩阵形式存储，并在决策后根据正确性反馈进行更新。利用Weyl不等式证明每次事件级更新的谱变化有界，从而保证在线稳定自适应。系统提供统一的写读接口，支持中心模型残差引导、免响应智能体路由和可靠性加权投票三种使用模式。

Result: 在五个Qwen系列模型上的实验表明，Σ-Mem能够适应反事实可靠性变化，并泛化到未见过的智能体和任务领域。直接读取记忆的表现在完整的OOD评测集上优于多数投票和最佳固定智能体。随着更多正确性反馈的积累，系统性能持续提升，说明其能渐进地积累可行动的可靠性信息。

Conclusion: 可靠性记忆可作为LLM多智能体系统中自适应协调的可复用基础，为智能体间的信任建模和动态协调提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Memory is central to long-horizon LLM agents, yet existing memory systems primarily preserve interaction content rather than modeling which agents can be trusted and under what conditions. This limitation is particularly important in multi-agent systems, where a central model may be unable to directly verify plausible or correlated peer responses. We introduce $Σ$-Mem, an online reliability memory that records historical competence evidence for individual peers and peer relationship evidence across the peer set. Both forms of evidence are maintained as real symmetric states and updated from post-decision correctness feedback. By Weyl's inequality, the spectral change caused by each event-level update is bounded, enabling stable online adaptation without retraining the underlying models. $Σ$-Mem provides a general write-and-read interface: the same memory can be used for residual steering of a central model, response-free peer routing, or reliability-weighted voting. Across five Qwen-family models, $Σ$-Mem adapts to counterfactual reliability shifts and generalizes to unseen peers and task domains. Direct memory readouts also outperform majority voting and the best fixed peer over the full OOD evaluation set. Moreover, performance improves consistently as more correctness feedback becomes available, indicating that $Σ$-Mem progressively accumulates actionable reliability information. These results establish reliability memory as a reusable foundation for adaptive coordination in LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [350] [VISA: A Structured Description Protocol for Agent-Based Simulation Models Towards Machine Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28027)
*Zhou He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出VISA协议，一种基于符号的结构化描述协议，通过八个互相关联的表格和十九条可执行一致性规则，使基于代理的模型（ABM）可机器解析、无歧义，并配套三个可复用的LLM技能（撰写、检查、代码生成），在三个外部模型上验证了跨语言复现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABM模型难以复现，其行为分散在散文叙述、平台特定代码和隐含假设中，导致不同读者从相同文档中重构出不同模型，缺乏结构化的规范描述方法。

Method: 提出VISA协议：用八个互相关联的表格（代理层的Agent、Variable、Sensing、Internal Function，模型层的Associated Data、Input/Output、Schedule、Validation）描述模型，遵循最小性与完整性原则；提供十九条可执行一致性规则将模型有效性变为可检查属性；开发三个LLM可执行技能实现撰写-检查-代码-复现的完整循环。

Result: 在三个外部独立撰写的ABM上验证：成功从VISA规范直接跨语言复现两个模型（NetLogo到Python）；第三个工业AnyLogic模型用八个表格完整捕获并通过全部十九条规则，同时诚实标注了因专有移动库和不可用数据导致的复现障碍。

Conclusion: VISA将复现障碍从模型内部（不可见）转移到已命名、可定位的依赖上（可操作），将复现问题转化为可诊断、可解决的具体依赖问题，是一种透明度贡献。

Abstract: Agent-based models (ABMs) are difficult to reproduce: their behavior is spread across prose narratives, platform-specific code, and implicit assumptions, so that two readers routinely reconstruct different models from the same documentation. We present VISA, a structured, symbol-based description protocol that specifies a model in eight interconnected tables---four at the agent level (Agent, Variable, Sensing, Internal Function) and four at the model level (Associated Data, Input/Output, Schedule, Validation)---under the principle of minimality with completeness. VISA makes a model machine-parseable and unambiguous via two artifacts: nineteen executable consistency rules that turn model validity into a checkable property, and three reusable LLM-executable skills (authoring, checking, and code generation) that operationalize the full author--check--code--reproduce loop. We validate the protocol on three external, independently authored ABMs spanning three platforms: we reproduce two cross-language (NetLogo to Python) directly from their VISA specifications, and we capture a third, an industrial AnyLogic model, in eight tables (passing all nineteen rules) while honestly demarcating where reproduction is blocked by a proprietary movement library and unavailable data---itself a transparency contribution. VISA moves the reproduction barrier from the model, where it is invisible, to a named, localized dependency, where it is actionable.

</details>


### [351] [AgentRadio: Passive Awareness for Long-Horizon Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28430)
*Xinxing Ren,Qianbo Zang,Ziyan Wang,Caelum Forder,Suman Deb,Peter Carroll,Zekun Guo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentRadio，一个异步消息传递层，为编码智能体提供线程、消息和等待提及三种原语，使多个智能体在执行过程中能够进行后台被动感知和持续协调。在SWE-Atlas QnA基准上，四个由AgentRadio组织的智能体解决了62.1%的任务，比单个智能体高29.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型代码库理解是LLM智能体的长时程任务，单个智能体解决率仅32.3%。尽管多智能体分工能缓解此限制，但代码理解的子任务相互依赖，一个智能体的发现可能改写另一个的任务。现有系统仅在阶段边界支持通信，执行中的发现无法及时共享。

Method: 提出AgentRadio异步消息传递层，提供三个原语：线程（threads）、消息（messages）和等待提及（waiting for mentions）。最后一种作为后台任务运行，在不中断前台工作的同时展示队友消息，使智能体保持被动感知。采用五阶段协议组织四个智能体的分工与协商。

Result: 在SWE-Atlas QnA基准上，四个由AgentRadio组织的智能体解决了62.1%的任务，比单个Opus 4.6智能体（32.3%）高29.8个百分点，也超过使用更新Opus 4.8的Claude Code（57.2%）。评分级别分析显示收益随任务难度增长。

Conclusion: AgentRadio通过异步消息传递和背景感知机制，使多智能体在长时程代码理解任务中实现执行中期的协同修正，显著提升任务解决率，且优势在困难任务上更为明显。

Abstract: Understanding large codebases is a long-horizon task for Large Language Model (LLM) agents: answering a single question can require building and running the software, tracing execution across files, and synthesizing evidence over tens of minutes. On SWE-Atlas QnA, a benchmark of long-horizon questions over production repositories, a single Claude Code agent (Opus 4.6) resolves only 32.3% of tasks. Dividing the work among agents with clean contexts mitigates this limitation. However, the subtasks of code comprehension are interdependent. One agent's findings can rewrite another's task, so agents must coordinate during execution, not only at phase boundaries. Existing multi-agent systems support such exchange only between phases, through staged handoffs or synchronized rounds. Communication and work remain mutually exclusive. A discovery made mid-execution cannot be shared until the next boundary. We present AgentRadio, an asynchronous message-passing layer that equips coding-agent harnesses with three primitives: threads, messages, and waiting for mentions. The last runs as a background task, surfacing teammates' messages without interrupting foreground work, so each agent remains passively aware of its peers and folds new findings into its ongoing task. Under a five-phase protocol of division of labor and negotiation, four agents organized by AgentRadio resolve 62.1% of tasks, 29.8 points above a single agent and above Claude Code with the newer Opus 4.8 (57.2%). Rubric-level analysis shows the gain growing with task difficulty, consistent with mid-course correction as the underlying mechanism. Our code is available at https://github.com/Coral-Protocol/AgentRadio.

</details>


### [352] [Using Theory of Mind to Arbitrate between Social and Non-social Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28601)
*Lance Ying,Ryan Truong,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Samuel J. Gershman*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"理性心智化"模型（Rational Mentalizing model），用于解释人类如何在社交学习与非社交学习之间做出权衡。该模型通过推理他人的目标和未来行为的信息量来估算社交学习的效用，并将其与非社交学习的效用进行比较。作者设计了一个新颖的游戏验证了模型能够定量捕捉人类在这两种策略间的权衡，表明选择性社交学习由"心智理论"服务于效用最大化所引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交学习是人类了解世界的重要机制，但现实中人们有时会选择直接经验而非社交学习，因为后者可能带来时间和认知资源的成本。已有研究未充分解释人们究竟如何在社交学习与非社交学习之间进行权衡，因此作者希望从计算建模的角度揭示这一决策过程背后的机制。

Method: 作者提出了理性心智化模型（Rational Mentalizing model），该模型通过两个步骤运作：（1）推理他人的目标及其未来行为的信息量，估算社交学习的效用；（2）将社交学习的效用与非社交学习的效用进行比较，从而决定采取何种学习策略。作者设计了一个新颖的游戏，让玩家在观察其他代理与自主探索环境之间做出选择，并用该模型定量拟合人类行为数据。

Result: 实验结果表明，理性心智化模型能够定量捕捉人类在社交学习与非社交学习之间的权衡行为，即模型预测与人类实际选择高度一致。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了选择性社交学习是由"心智理论"（Theory of Mind）驱动的，即人们通过推理他人的心智状态来评估社交学习的信息价值，并在此基础上追求效用最大化。这为理解人类社交决策的认知机制提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Social learning is a powerful mechanism through which agents learn about the world from others. However, humans sometimes choose direct experience over social learning, which can carry time and cognitive resource costs. How do people balance social and non-social learning? We propose a Rational Mentalizing model of the decision to engage in social learning. This model estimates the utility of social learning by reasoning about another agent's goal and the informativeness of their future actions. It then weighs the utility of social learning against the utility of non-social learning. Using a novel game where players choose between observing other agents or exploring the environment, we show that the Rational Mentalizing model can quantitatively capture human trade-offs between these strategies. These findings suggest that selective social learning is guided by 'Theory of Mind' in the service of utility maximization.

</details>
